
FILE repository\Stadiul0\calib_probe.py
1: """Stage 0 calibration probes (short experiments, not runs of the study).
2: 
3:     python calib_probe.py childhood --lr 2e-3 --seed 9101
4:         childhood only (3,072 steps): reference accuracy (fact, rule) at the end.
5:     python calib_probe.py task --lr 1e-3 --kind rule --steps 2048 --seed 9101
6:         childhood, then one target task of the given kind trained alone for --steps steps;
7:         probe-B accuracy and loss every 128 steps (learnability of the task type).
8: """
9: import os
10: 
11: for _v in ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS"):
12:     os.environ[_v] = "1"
13: 
14: import argparse
15: import json
16: import time
17: from pathlib import Path
18: 
19: import torch
20: 
21: from p2.run import Runner, load_config
22: 
23: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
24: 
25: 
26: def main():
27:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
28:     ap.add_argument("mode", choices=["childhood", "task"])
29:     ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
30:     ap.add_argument("--life_lr", type=float, default=None)
31:     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=9101)
32:     ap.add_argument("--family", default="F1")
33:     ap.add_argument("--kind", default="rule")
34:     ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=2048)
35:     ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=32)
36:     ap.add_argument("--set", action="append", default=[])
37:     a = ap.parse_args()
38:     cfg, sha = load_config(HERE / "config_stage0.yaml", [f"optim.lr={a.lr}"] + a.set)
39:     R = Runner(cfg, a.family, a.seed, HERE / "runs" / "_calib_unused", sha)
40:     t0 = time.perf_counter()
41:     R.childhood_phase()
42:     out = {"mode": a.mode, "lr": a.lr, "seed": a.seed, "childhood": R.childhood,
43:            "childhood_loss_curve": [round(float(R.childhood_loss[max(0, i - 32): i + 32].mean()), 3)
44:                                     for i in range(256, len(R.childhood_loss) + 1, 256)]}
45:     if a.mode == "task":
46:         k = next(i for i, t in enumerate(R.world.targets) if t.kind == a.kind)
47:         t = R.world.targets[k]
48:         lr = a.life_lr if a.life_lr is not None else a.lr
49:         curve = []
50:         for j, (x, y) in enumerate(R.batches(t.train_x, t.train_y, a.steps, a.batch)):
51:             R.agent.step(torch.from_numpy(x), torch.from_numpy(y), lr, None)
52:             if (j + 1) % 128 == 0:
53:                 LB, AB = R.eval_task_B(k)
54:                 tr = R.forward_items(torch.from_numpy(t.train_x), torch.from_numpy(t.train_y))
55:                 curve.append((j + 1, round(AB, 3), round(LB, 3), round(tr["correct"].double().mean().item(), 3)))
56:         out.update({"task": k, "kind": a.kind, "modulus": t.modulus, "life_lr": lr,
57:                     "curve_step_accB_lossB_acctrain": curve})
58:     out["wall_s"] = round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1)
59:     print(json.dumps(out))
60: 
61: 
62: if __name__ == "__main__":
63:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\checks_stage0.py
1: """Automated checks for the Stage 0 code (run from the Stadiul0 folder: python checks_stage0.py).
2: 
3: 1. World: vocabulary partition, disjointness of R, B_beh and probes, probe halves (gate G4, structural).
4: 2. Schedules: every family sums to the life length, segments on the 64-step quantum.
5: 3. Optimizer: the explicit AdamW step equals torch.optim.AdamW (with clipping and parameter groups).
6: 3b. Deep Review corrections (6 Nov 2026): max_ctx = input width; embeddings out of weight decay; C10c, L_clip, q_hist.
7: 4. Identity: a same-step D2 equals lr * ||m_hat|| up to epsilon (section 5.1).
8: 5. Determinism: two short runs with the same seed give bit-identical arrays (gate G7).
9: """
10: import os
11: 
12: for _v in ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS"):
13:     os.environ[_v] = "1"
14: 
15: import copy
16: import shutil
17: import sys
18: import tempfile
19: from pathlib import Path
20: 
21: import numpy as np
22: import torch
23: 
24: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
25: sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
26: 
27: from p2.agent import FlatAgent                      # noqa: E402
28: from p2.run import load_config, run_one              # noqa: E402
29: from p2.streams import build_schedule                # noqa: E402
30: from p2.world import World                           # noqa: E402
31: 
32: CFG_PATH = HERE / "config_stage0.yaml"
33: results = []
34: 
35: 
36: def check(name, ok, detail=""):
37:     results.append((name, bool(ok)))
38:     print(f"[{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}{(': ' + detail) if detail else ''}")
39: 
40: 
41: def main():
42:     torch.set_num_threads(1)
43:     torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
44:     cfg, _ = load_config(CFG_PATH)
45: 
46:     # 1. world
47:     for fam in ["F1", "F5"]:
48:         f = cfg["families"][fam]
49:         w = World(cfg["world"], np.random.default_rng(7), f["rule_frac"], f["pi_overlap"])   # asserts inside
50:         n_rule = sum(t.kind == "rule" for t in w.targets)
51:         check(f"world {fam}: partition and disjointness", True,
52:               f"{len(w.targets)} target tasks ({n_rule} rule), |R| = {len(w.R_x)}, |B_beh| = {len(w.B_x)}")
53:         ov = w.key_overlap()
54:         facts = [i for i, t in enumerate(w.targets) if t.kind == "fact"]
55:         mean_ov = ov[np.ix_(facts, facts)][~np.eye(len(facts), dtype=bool)].mean()
56:         check(f"world {fam}: fact-task pair overlap is positive", mean_ov > 0, f"mean pairwise {mean_ov:.4f}")
57: 
58:     # 2. schedules
59:     r = cfg["run"]
60:     for fam_name, fam in cfg["families"].items():
61:         segs, lr, restarts = build_schedule(fam, cfg["world"]["n_target"], r["life_steps_per_task"], r["q0"],
62:                                             cfg["optim"]["lr"], np.random.default_rng(3))
63:         kinds = {k: sum(s.kind == k for s in segs) for k in ["new", "reexp", "reh", "distractor", "idle"]}
64:         ok = (len(lr) == cfg["world"]["n_target"] * r["life_steps_per_task"]
65:               and all(s.steps % 64 == 0 and s.ticks % 64 == 0 for s in segs)
66:               and kinds["new"] == cfg["world"]["n_target"])
67:         check(f"schedule {fam_name}", ok, f"{len(lr)} steps, {kinds}, lr [{lr.min():.2e}, {lr.max():.2e}], "
68:                                           f"{len(restarts)} restart(s)")
69: 
70:     # 3. explicit AdamW == torch.optim.AdamW
71:     torch.manual_seed(0)
72:     a = FlatAgent(cfg["agent"], cfg["optim"])
73:     ref = copy.deepcopy(a.model)
74:     o = cfg["optim"]
75:     dec = set(a.decayed_names)                     # matrices under weight decay (embeddings out if wd_embeddings false)
76:     groups = [{"params": [p for n, p in ref.named_parameters() if n in dec], "weight_decay": o["weight_decay"]},
77:               {"params": [p for n, p in ref.named_parameters() if n not in dec], "weight_decay": 0.0}]
78:     opt = torch.optim.AdamW(groups, lr=o["lr"], betas=tuple(o["betas"]), eps=o["eps"], foreach=False)
79:     g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(1)
80:     max_dev = 0.0
81:     for s in range(30):
82:         x = torch.randint(0, 512, (32, 4), generator=g)
83:         y = torch.randint(0, 512, (32,), generator=g)
84:         a.step(x, y, o["lr"])
85:         opt.zero_grad()
86:         torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(ref(x), y).backward()
87:         torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(ref.parameters(), o["clip"])
88:         opt.step()
89:         mine = torch.cat([p.detach().reshape(-1) for _, p in a.order])
90:         theirs = torch.cat([dict(ref.named_parameters())[n].detach().reshape(-1) for n, _ in a.order])
91:         max_dev = max(max_dev, (mine - theirs).abs().max().item())
92:     check("explicit AdamW is bit-identical to torch.optim.AdamW (30 steps)", max_dev == 0.0,
93:           f"max |diff| = {max_dev:.2e}")
94: 
95:     # 3b. Deep Review corrections (6 Nov 2026): context = input width; embeddings out of weight decay
96:     w0 = World(cfg["world"], np.random.default_rng(7), 0.0, 0.25)
97:     width = w0.targets[0].train_x.shape[1]
98:     check("max_ctx equals the input width (Deep Review D2)", cfg["agent"]["max_ctx"] == width,
99:           f"max_ctx {cfg['agent']['max_ctx']}, input width {width}")
100:     torch.manual_seed(5)
101:     b = FlatAgent(cfg["agent"], cfg["optim"])
102:     rows0 = b.model.tok.weight[256:].detach().clone()
103:     g2 = torch.Generator().manual_seed(2)
104:     for _ in range(20):
105:         b.step(torch.randint(0, 256, (32, 4), generator=g2), torch.randint(0, 256, (32,), generator=g2), o["lr"])
106:     unchanged = torch.equal(b.model.tok.weight[256:], rows0)
107:     want = not o.get("wd_embeddings", True)
108:     check("unused embedding rows: unchanged iff embeddings are out of weight decay (Deep Review D3)",
109:           unchanged == want, f"wd_embeddings={o.get('wd_embeddings', True)}, rows unchanged={unchanged}, "
110:                              f"decayed matrices {len(b.decayed_names)} of {len(b.layers)}")
111: 
112:     # 4. same-step D2 identity: (sum v_hat dpre^2)^(1/2) ~= lr ||m_hat|| (with eps)
113:     x = torch.randint(0, 512, (32, 4), generator=g)
114:     y = torch.randint(0, 512, (32,), generator=g)
115:     m_prev, v_prev, t_prev = a.m.clone(), a.v.clone(), a.t
116:     flat_prev = a.flat.clone()
117:     stats, dpre, _ = a.step(x, y, o["lr"], None)
118:     vh = a.vhat()
119:     same = torch.sqrt((vh.double() * dpre.double() ** 2).sum()).item()
120:     lrm = o["lr"] * a.mhat().double().norm().item()
121:     check("same-step D2 identity (section 5.1)", abs(same - lrm) / lrm < 1e-3, f"{same:.6e} vs {lrm:.6e}")
122:     del m_prev, v_prev, t_prev, flat_prev
123: 
124:     # 5. determinism (G7): two tiny runs with the same seed
125:     tiny = ["world.n_target=4", "run.childhood_steps=128", "run.life_steps_per_task=512",
126:             "run.d3_strides=[[8,0,null],[1,64,192]]", "run.chunked_param_D=true", "run.d2_lag_mode=both"]
127:     tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="p2chk_", dir=str(HERE)))
128:     try:
129:         hashes = []
130:         for fam in ["F4", "F6"]:
131:             m1 = run_one(CFG_PATH, fam, 11, tmp / f"{fam}_a", tiny)
132:             m2 = run_one(CFG_PATH, fam, 11, tmp / f"{fam}_b", tiny)
133:             same = m1["sha256_arrays"] == m2["sha256_arrays"]
134:             check(f"determinism {fam}: bit-identical reruns", same,
135:                   f"{m1['n_evals']} evals, {m1['n_episodes']} episodes, wall {m1['timing_s']['wall']} s")
136:             hashes.append(m1["sha256_arrays"])
137:             st = np.load(tmp / f"{fam}_a" / "steps.npz")
138:             ok = np.isfinite(st["D2_exact"][300:]).all() and np.isfinite(st["D2"][300:]).all()
139:             rel = np.abs(st["D2"] - st["D2_exact"]) / st["D2_exact"]
140:             check(f"D2 blocked vs exact lag {fam}", ok, f"median rel. diff {np.nanmedian(rel):.2e}, "
141:                                                        f"max {np.nanmax(rel):.2e}")
142:             ch = np.load(tmp / f"{fam}_a" / "chunks.npz")
143:             ev = np.load(tmp / f"{fam}_a" / "evals.npz"); eps = np.load(tmp / f"{fam}_a" / "episodes.npz")
144:             c10c = ch["m8_s0_C10c"]
145:             check(f"C10c present, finite and >= C10b {fam} (Deep Review D5)",
146:                   len(c10c) > 0 and np.isfinite(c10c).all() and np.all(c10c >= ch["m8_s0_C10b"] - 1e-12),
147:                   f"{len(c10c)} chunks, median C10c / C10b {np.median(c10c / np.maximum(ch['m8_s0_C10b'], 1e-12)):.1f}")
148:             okc = np.nanmax(ev["LAc"] - ev["LA"]) <= 1e-12 and np.nanmax(ev["LAc"]) <= np.log(64) + 1e-9
149:             check(f"clipped losses L_A,clip <= L_A and <= ln 64 {fam} (Deep Review A3)", okc,
150:                   f"max L_A,clip {np.nanmax(ev['LAc']):.3f}")
151:             ov = eps["overlap"]
152:             check(f"q_hist before the segment: first exposure has no history {fam} (Deep Review D4)",
153:                   np.isnan(ov[0]) and np.all((ov[1:] >= 0) & (ov[1:] <= 1)), f"overlap[0] = {ov[0]}, then {np.round(ov[1:4], 3)}")
154:             d1c, d1 = ch["m1_s64_D1c"], st["D1"][64:192]
155:             check(f"chunked D1 at m_ch = 1 equals per-step D1 {fam}",
156:                   np.allclose(d1c, d1, rtol=1e-5), f"max rel {np.max(np.abs(d1c - d1) / d1):.1e}")
157:         check("different families give different runs", hashes[0] != hashes[1])
158:     finally:
159:         shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
160: 
161:     n_fail = sum(not ok for _, ok in results)
162:     print(f"\n{len(results) - n_fail}/{len(results)} checks passed")
163:     return 1 if n_fail else 0
164: 
165: 
166: if __name__ == "__main__":
167:     raise SystemExit(main())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\g2_cpu_checks.py
1: # Stage 0, two CPU checks on the technical runs before the LM arm (6 Nov 2026). Run from Stadiul0:
2: #   python g2_cpu_checks.py
3: # Blind to T1: F_A, F_B, dAcc_A are dropped on load; no model predicts forgetting.
4: # (a) G2 with family F3 left out entirely (F3 is the only test family with learning-rate restarts):
5: #     train on tech3_F2, tech2_F1, tech2_F2; test on tech3_F3 and tech2_F3.
6: # (b) Where does the cheap reconstruction "dN + rate of sum eta*|m_hat| (C5d)" fail? Residuals of a leave-one-run-out
7: #     OLS, binned by interval length, by segment boundaries and by learning-rate restarts inside the interval.
8: import json, numpy as np, pandas as pd
9: from pathlib import Path
10: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
11: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
12: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
13: from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
14: 
15: R = Path(r"D:\Time\02_Pilot2\Stadiul0\runs")
16: runs = {"tech3_F2": R/"tech3_lr3e-4/F2_s9004", "tech3_F3": R/"tech3_lr3e-4/F3_s9004",
17:         "tech2_F1": R/"tech2_lr3e-4/F1_s9002", "tech2_F2": R/"tech2_lr3e-4/F2_s9002", "tech2_F3": R/"tech2_lr3e-4/F3_s9002"}
18: FORBID = {"F_A", "F_B", "dAcc_A"}
19: 
20: def boundaries(rd):
21:     s = json.load(open(rd/"schedule.json"))
22:     b = np.cumsum([seg["steps"] for seg in s["segments"]])
23:     return np.unique(np.concatenate([[0], b])), np.asarray(s["restarts"], dtype=float)
24: 
25: dfs = []
26: for name, rd in runs.items():
27:     d = pd.read_parquet(rd/"triplets.parquet")
28:     d = d.drop(columns=[c for c in FORBID if c in d])
29:     d = d[d["incl_nocap"] if "incl_nocap" in d else d["incl"]]
30:     d = d[(d.x_rule == 0) & (d.dN < 1280) & (d.dN >= 64)].copy()
31:     E = d.ep.nunique(); d["w"] = 1.0/(E*d.groupby("ep").ep.transform("size")); d["run"] = name
32:     bnd, rst = boundaries(rd)
33:     n0, n1 = d.N0.to_numpy(float), d.N1.to_numpy(float)
34:     last = bnd[np.searchsorted(bnd, n0, side="right") - 1]
35:     d["since_bnd0"] = n0 - last
36:     d["n_bnd"] = np.searchsorted(bnd, n1, side="left") - np.searchsorted(bnd, n0, side="right")
37:     d["n_rst"] = (np.searchsorted(rst, n1, side="right") - np.searchsorted(rst, n0, side="right")) if len(rst) else 0
38:     dfs.append(d)
39: df = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)
40: assert not (set(df.columns) & FORBID)
41: df["C1_a"] = df.dN; df["C1_b"] = np.log(df.dN+1); df["C1_c"] = np.log(df.dN+10); df["C1_d"] = np.log(df.dN+100)
42: xcov = ["x_LB0","x_AB0","x_prior_LB","x_overlap","x_N0","x_tenure","x_prior_exposures","x_K_active","x_theta_norm0","x_sqrt_trv0"]
43: c6 = [c for c in df.columns if c.startswith("C6_")]
44: bstrong = xcov + ["C0","C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","C2","C3","C4","C5","C7a","C5a","C5b_g","C5b_gt","C5b_L","C5c_sq","C5c_work",
45:           "C5d","C5e","C5f","C2b","C6b_theta","C6b_v","C10","C10b"] + c6 + \
46:           [f"rate_{z}" for z in ["C2","C2b","C3","C4","C5","C5a","C5b_g","C5b_gt","C5b_L","C5c_sq","C5c_work","C5d","C5e","C5f","C10","C10b"]]
47: assert not any(c.startswith("ce_") or "Disp" in c for c in bstrong)
48: df[bstrong] = df[bstrong].fillna(0.0)
49: dN4 = ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d"]
50: ladder = {"dN": dN4, "dN+C3": dN4+["rate_C3"], "dN+C5d": dN4+["rate_C5d"], "dN+C3+C5d+C5e": dN4+["rate_C3","rate_C5d","rate_C5e"]}
51: 
52: def r2w(y, p, w):
53:     m = (w*y).sum()/w.sum(); return 1 - (w*(y-p)**2).sum()/(w*(y-m)**2).sum()
54: def ols(): return make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LinearRegression())
55: def gbdt(): return HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20, random_state=0)
56: 
57: print(f"triplets: {len(df)}; runs: {df.run.nunique()}; B_strong features: {len(bstrong)}")
58: print("\n(a) G2 with F3 left out of training entirely (train: tech3_F2, tech2_F1, tech2_F2)")
59: train = ~df.run.str.endswith("F3")
60: rows = []
61: for D in ["D1", "D1_rel", "D2", "D3"]:
62:     y = np.nan_to_num(df[f"rate_ce_{D}"].to_numpy(), nan=0.0)
63:     for test in ["tech3_F3", "tech2_F3"]:
64:         te = (df.run == test).to_numpy(); w = df.w.to_numpy()[te]; out = {"D": D, "test": test}
65:         for nm, mk in [("OLS_Bstrong", ols), ("GBDT_Bstrong", gbdt)]:
66:             out[nm] = r2w(y[te], mk().fit(df.loc[train, bstrong], y[train]).predict(df.loc[te, bstrong]), w)
67:         for nm, cols in ladder.items():
68:             out[nm] = r2w(y[te], ols().fit(df.loc[train, cols], y[train]).predict(df.loc[te, cols]), w)
69:         rows.append(out)
70: print(pd.DataFrame(rows).round(3).to_string(index=False))
71: 
72: print("\n(b) residual of OLS 'dN + rate C5d', leave-one-run-out; relative error |y-p|/mean(y) and bias (y-p)/mean(y)")
73: for D in ["D1", "D3"]:
74:     y = np.nan_to_num(df[f"rate_ce_{D}"].to_numpy(), nan=0.0); p = np.zeros_like(y)
75:     for r in df.run.unique():
76:         te = (df.run == r).to_numpy()
77:         p[te] = ols().fit(df.loc[~te, ladder["dN+C5d"]], y[~te]).predict(df.loc[te, ladder["dN+C5d"]])
78:     t = df[["run", "w", "since_bnd0", "n_bnd", "n_rst"]].copy(); t["y"] = y; t["res"] = y - p
79:     t["dN"] = df.dN.to_numpy()   # every interval starts at a segment start (N0 on a boundary), so the transient after a
80:     t["bin_dN"] = pd.cut(t.dN, [63, 127, 255, 511, 1279], labels=["64-127", "128-255", "256-511", "512-1279"])  # boundary = short dN
81:     t["bin_bnd"] = pd.cut(t.n_bnd, [-1, 0, 2, 5, 1e9], labels=["0", "1-2", "3-5", ">=6"])
82:     t["rst"] = np.where(t.n_rst > 0, "restart inside", "no restart")
83:     mu = (t.w*t.y).sum()/t.w.sum()
84:     def summ(g):
85:         ww = g.w.sum()
86:         return pd.Series({"n": len(g), "weight_%": 100*ww/t.w.sum(), "abs_err_rel": (g.w*g.res.abs()).sum()/ww/mu,
87:                           "bias_rel": (g.w*g.res).sum()/ww/mu})
88:     print(f"\n  {D}: overall R2 = {r2w(y, p, t.w.to_numpy()):.3f}")
89:     print(f"  N0 on a segment boundary in {100*(t.since_bnd0 == 0).mean():.0f}% of intervals")
90:     print("  by interval length dN (short = dominated by the transient after the boundary):")
91:     print(t.groupby("bin_dN", observed=True).apply(summ, include_groups=False).round(3).to_string())
92:     print("  by number of further segment boundaries inside the interval:")
93:     print(t.groupby("bin_bnd", observed=True).apply(summ, include_groups=False).round(3).to_string())
94:     print("  by learning-rate restart inside the interval (F3 only has restarts):")
95:     print(t.groupby("rst").apply(summ, include_groups=False).round(3).to_string())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\g2_preview.py
1: # Stage 0 preview of gate G2 on technical runs (4 Nov 2026). Run from Stadiul0: python g2_preview.py
2: # G2 (identifiability) on technical runs: out-of-sample R^2 of the interval rate rho = dce/max(dN,1) predicted from
3: # B_strong. Blind to T1: F_A, F_B, dAcc_A are never read; no model predicts forgetting.
4: import sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
5: from pathlib import Path
6: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
7: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
8: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
9: from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
10: R = Path(r"D:\Time\02_Pilot2\Stadiul0\runs")
11: runs = {"tech3_F2": R/"tech3_lr3e-4/F2_s9004", "tech3_F3": R/"tech3_lr3e-4/F3_s9004",
12:         "tech2_F1": R/"tech2_lr3e-4/F1_s9002", "tech2_F2": R/"tech2_lr3e-4/F2_s9002", "tech2_F3": R/"tech2_lr3e-4/F3_s9002"}
13: FORBID = {"F_A", "F_B", "dAcc_A"}
14: dfs = []
15: for name, rd in runs.items():
16:     d = pd.read_parquet(rd/"triplets.parquet")
17:     d = d[d["incl_nocap"] if "incl_nocap" in d else d["incl"]]
18:     d = d[(d.x_rule == 0) & (d.dN < 1280) & (d.dN >= 64)].copy()
19:     E = d.ep.nunique(); d["w"] = 1.0/(E*d.groupby("ep").ep.transform("size")); d["run"] = name
20:     dfs.append(d.drop(columns=[c for c in FORBID if c in d]))
21: df = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)
22: df["C1_a"] = df.dN; df["C1_b"] = np.log(df.dN+1); df["C1_c"] = np.log(df.dN+10); df["C1_d"] = np.log(df.dN+100)
23: xcov = ["x_LB0","x_AB0","x_prior_LB","x_overlap","x_N0","x_tenure","x_prior_exposures","x_K_active","x_theta_norm0","x_sqrt_trv0"]
24: c6 = [c for c in df.columns if c.startswith("C6_")]
25: bstrong = xcov + ["C0","C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","C2","C3","C4","C5","C7a","C5a","C5b_g","C5b_gt","C5b_L","C5c_sq","C5c_work",
26:           "C5d","C5e","C5f","C2b","C6b_theta","C6b_v","C10","C10b"] + c6 + \
27:           [f"rate_{z}" for z in ["C2","C2b","C3","C4","C5","C5a","C5b_g","C5b_gt","C5b_L","C5c_sq","C5c_work","C5d","C5e","C5f","C10","C10b"]]
28: assert not any(c.startswith("ce_") or "Disp" in c or c in FORBID for c in bstrong)
29: df[bstrong] = df[bstrong].fillna(0.0)
30: def r2w(y, p, w):
31:     m = (w*y).sum()/w.sum(); return 1 - (w*(y-p)**2).sum()/(w*(y-m)**2).sum()
32: print(f"triplets: {len(df)}; runs: {df.run.nunique()}; features: {len(bstrong)}")
33: rows = []
34: for D in ["D1", "D1_rel", "D2", "D3"]:
35:     y = np.nan_to_num(df[f"rate_ce_{D}"].to_numpy(), nan=0.0)
36:     for test in ["tech3_F2", "tech3_F3"]:
37:         tr, te = (df.run != test).to_numpy(), (df.run == test).to_numpy()
38:         out = {"D": D, "test": test}
39:         for name, mdl in [("OLS", make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LinearRegression())),
40:                           ("GBDT", HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20, random_state=0))]:
41:             mdl.fit(df.loc[tr, bstrong], y[tr], **({"histgradientboostingregressor__sample_weight": None} if False else {}))
42:             p = mdl.predict(df.loc[te, bstrong]); w = df.w.to_numpy()[te]
43:             out[f"R2_{name}"] = r2w(y[te], p, w)
44:         # how variable is the rate within the test run (CV), and how much does dN alone explain it
45:         yt = y[te]; w = df.w.to_numpy()[te]; mu = (w*yt).sum()/w.sum()
46:         out["CV_rate"] = np.sqrt((w*(yt-mu)**2).sum()/w.sum())/mu
47:         g = HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=200, learning_rate=0.05, random_state=0).fit(df.loc[tr, ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d"]], y[tr])
48:         out["R2_dN_only"] = r2w(yt, g.predict(df.loc[te, ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d"]]), w)
49:         rows.append(out)
50: print(pd.DataFrame(rows).round(3).to_string(index=False))
51: 
52: print("\n--- scara: R2 (OLS) al ratei din seturi minime de telemetrie, medie pe cele doua rulari test ---")
53: sets = {"ΔN": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d"],
54:         "ΔN + rata Ση (C3)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C3"],
55:         "ΔN + rata Σ‖g̃‖ (C5a)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C5a"],
56:         "ΔN + rata Ση‖g̃‖ (C5b)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C5b_gt"],
57:         "ΔN + rata Ση‖m̂‖ (C5d)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C5d"],
58:         "ΔN + rata Σ‖m̂‖ (C5e)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C5e"],
59:         "ΔN + C3 + C5d + C5e (rate)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C3","rate_C5d","rate_C5e"],
60:         "ΔN + rata pierderii pe R (C10b)": ["C1_a","C1_b","C1_c","C1_d","rate_C10b"]}
61: res = []
62: for D in ["D1","D1_rel","D2","D3"]:
63:     y = np.nan_to_num(df[f"rate_ce_{D}"].to_numpy(), nan=0.0)
64:     row = {"D": D}
65:     for nm, cols in sets.items():
66:         r = []
67:         for test in ["tech3_F2","tech3_F3"]:
68:             tr, te = (df.run != test).to_numpy(), (df.run == test).to_numpy()
69:             m = make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LinearRegression()).fit(df.loc[tr, cols], y[tr])
70:             r.append(r2w(y[te], m.predict(df.loc[te, cols]), df.w.to_numpy()[te]))
71:         row[nm] = np.mean(r)
72:     res.append(row)
73: print(pd.DataFrame(res).set_index("D").T.round(3).to_string())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\g7_ram_compare.py
1: # G7 after the RAM upgrade: are the reruns bit-identical to the tech3 runs made with one module?
2: # Compares (1) the array hashes stored in meta.json, (2) hashes recomputed now from the .npz files of both runs,
3: # (3) wall time and ms per step. Run from the Stadiul0 folder: python <this file>
4: import json, sys
5: from pathlib import Path
6: import numpy as np
7: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path.cwd()))
8: from p2.run import array_hashes
9: 
10: ARRAYS = ["steps", "evals", "episodes", "disp", "chunks", "world"]
11: R = Path("runs")
12: pairs = [("tech3_lr3e-4", "g7_ram_lr3e-4"), ("tech3_lr2e-4", "g7_ram_lr2e-4")]
13: ok_all = True
14: for old, new in pairs:
15:     for run in ["F2_s9004", "F3_s9004"]:
16:         a, b = R / old / run, R / new / run
17:         ma = json.loads((a / "meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
18:         mb = json.loads((b / "meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
19:         same_cfg = {k: v for k, v in ma["config"].items() if k not in ("analysis", "meta")} == \
20:                    {k: v for k, v in mb["config"].items() if k not in ("analysis", "meta")}
21:         res = []
22:         for n in ARRAYS:
23:             stored = ma["sha256_arrays"][n] == mb["sha256_arrays"][n]
24:             with np.load(a / f"{n}.npz") as za, np.load(b / f"{n}.npz") as zb:
25:                 ha = array_hashes({k: za[k] for k in za.files}); hb = array_hashes({k: zb[k] for k in zb.files})
26:             res.append((n, stored, ha == hb, ha == ma["sha256_arrays"][n], hb == mb["sha256_arrays"][n]))
27:         ok = same_cfg and all(all(r[1:]) for r in res)
28:         ok_all &= ok
29:         ta, tb = ma["timing_s"], mb["timing_s"]
30:         print(f"{old} -> {new} {run}: config equal={same_cfg}; " +
31:               "; ".join(f"{n}: {'=' if s and r else 'DIFF'}" for n, s, r, _, _ in res) + f"  => {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}")
32:         print(f"    timing old: {ta}\n    timing new: {tb}\n    peak RSS MB old/new: {ma.get('peak_rss_mb')} / {mb.get('peak_rss_mb')}")
33: for old, new in pairs:
34:     la = json.loads((R / old / "launch_summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
35:     lb = json.loads((R / new / "launch_summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
36:     print(f"wall {old}: {la['total_wall_s']} s ({[r.get('wall_s') for r in la['runs']]}); {new}: {lb['total_wall_s']} s ({[r.get('wall_s') for r in lb['runs']]})")
37: print("G7 after RAM upgrade:", "PASS (all arrays bit-identical)" if ok_all else "FAIL")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\hw_watchdog.py
1: """Hardware watchdog for unattended rounds (6 Nov 2026). Run with the CPU environment:
2:     python -I hw_watchdog.py --until "2026-11-09 08:00"
3: 
4: Every 30 s it logs GPU (nvidia-smi: temperature, power, SM clock, utilisation, thermal slowdown flags), the ACPI thermal
5: zone (typeperf: temperature in kelvin, % passive limit = firmware throttling), CPU performance %, AC power and free space
6: on D: to runs/_hw/hw_log_<date>.csv. It keeps Windows awake until --until (SetThreadExecutionState; no setting changed).
7: It stops the training processes (lm_forever_run.py, the synthetic-world runs) and writes runs/_HW_STOP when, for the stated
8: number of consecutive samples:
9:   GPU >= 87 C (3) | GPU hardware/software thermal slowdown (3) | thermal zone >= 370 K = 97 C (3)
10:   | passive limit < 100% (6) | on battery (2) | D: free < 10 GB (1).
11: Then it exits with one line saying why (the caller is notified). Launchers check runs/_HW_STOP before each run.
12: """
13: import argparse, ctypes, csv, datetime as dt, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, time
14: from pathlib import Path
15: 
16: RUNS = Path(r"D:\Time\02_Pilot2\Stadiul0\runs"); HW = RUNS / "_hw"; STOP = RUNS / "_HW_STOP"
17: RULES = {"gpu_hot": 3, "gpu_slowdown": 3, "tz_hot": 3, "passive": 6, "battery": 2, "disk": 1}
18: NV = ["temperature.gpu", "power.draw", "clocks.sm", "utilization.gpu", "memory.used",
19:       "clocks_event_reasons.hw_thermal_slowdown", "clocks_event_reasons.sw_thermal_slowdown"]
20: 
21: class SPS(ctypes.Structure):
22:     _fields_ = [("ACLineStatus", ctypes.c_byte), ("BatteryFlag", ctypes.c_byte), ("BatteryLifePercent", ctypes.c_byte),
23:                 ("SystemStatusFlag", ctypes.c_byte), ("BatteryLifeTime", ctypes.c_ulong), ("BatteryFullLifeTime", ctypes.c_ulong)]
24: 
25: def sample():
26:     r = {"time": dt.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}
27:     try:
28:         out = subprocess.run(["nvidia-smi", f"--query-gpu={','.join(NV)}", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
29:                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20).stdout.strip().split(", ")
30:         r.update(gpu_t=float(out[0]), gpu_w=float(out[1]), gpu_mhz=float(out[2]), gpu_util=float(out[3]),
31:                  gpu_mem=float(out[4]), gpu_slow=int(any(x.strip() == "Active" for x in out[5:7])))
32:     except Exception as e:
33:         r.update(gpu_t=float("nan"), gpu_err=str(e)[:60])
34:     try:
35:         out = subprocess.run(["typeperf", r"\Thermal Zone Information(*)\Temperature",
36:                               r"\Thermal Zone Information(*)\% Passive Limit",
37:                               r"\Processor Information(_Total)\% Processor Performance", "-sc", "1"],
38:                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30).stdout.splitlines()
39:         vals = [float(v.strip('"')) for v in out[2].split(",")[1:]]
40:         r.update(tz_k=vals[0], passive=vals[1], cpu_perf=vals[2])
41:     except Exception:
42:         r.update(tz_k=float("nan"), passive=float("nan"), cpu_perf=float("nan"))
43:     s = SPS(); ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetSystemPowerStatus(ctypes.byref(s))
44:     r.update(ac=int(s.ACLineStatus == 1), batt=int(s.BatteryLifePercent))
45:     r["d_free_gb"] = round(shutil.disk_usage("D:\\").free / 2**30, 1)
46:     return r
47: 
48: def flags(r):
49:     return {"gpu_hot": r.get("gpu_t", 0) >= 87, "gpu_slowdown": r.get("gpu_slow", 0) == 1,
50:             "tz_hot": r.get("tz_k", 0) >= 370, "passive": r.get("passive", 100) < 100,
51:             "battery": r["ac"] == 0, "disk": r["d_free_gb"] < 10}
52: 
53: def stop_training(reason):
54:     STOP.write_text(f"{dt.datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S} {reason}\n", encoding="utf-8")
55:     ps = ("Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name='python.exe'\" | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -match "
56:           "'lm_forever_run|p2[.\\\\/]run|launch\\.py|run_one' } | ForEach-Object { Stop-Process -Id $_.ProcessId -Force; $_.ProcessId }")
57:     out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60).stdout.split()
58:     return out
59: 
60: def main():
61:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--until", required=True); ap.add_argument("--every", type=int, default=30)
62:     a = ap.parse_args(); until = dt.datetime.strptime(a.until, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
63:     HW.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
64:     ES_CONTINUOUS, ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = 0x80000000, 0x00000001
65:     ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(ES_CONTINUOUS | ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED)
66:     streak = {k: 0 for k in RULES}; fields = None
67:     while dt.datetime.now() < until:
68:         r = sample(); f = flags(r)
69:         path = HW / f"hw_log_{dt.date.today():%Y%m%d}.csv"; new = not path.exists()
70:         cols = ["time", "gpu_t", "gpu_w", "gpu_mhz", "gpu_util", "gpu_mem", "gpu_slow", "tz_k", "passive", "cpu_perf",
71:                 "ac", "batt", "d_free_gb"]
72:         with open(path, "a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
73:             w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=cols, extrasaction="ignore")
74:             if new: w.writeheader()
75:             w.writerow(r)
76:         for k in RULES:
77:             streak[k] = streak[k] + 1 if f[k] else 0
78:         hit = [k for k in RULES if streak[k] >= RULES[k]]
79:         if hit:
80:             pids = stop_training(",".join(hit))
81:             print(f"OPRIRE HARDWARE {r['time']}: {hit}; GPU {r.get('gpu_t')} C, zona termica {r.get('tz_k')} K, "
82:                   f"limita pasiva {r.get('passive')}%, priza {r['ac']}, D: {r['d_free_gb']} GB; procese oprite: {pids}", flush=True)
83:             ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(ES_CONTINUOUS)
84:             sys.exit(2)
85:         time.sleep(a.every)
86:     ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(ES_CONTINUOUS)
87:     print(f"paznic terminat normal la {dt.datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M}", flush=True)
88: 
89: if __name__ == "__main__":
90:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\launch.py
1: """Run several Stage 0 runs as parallel single-threaded workers (protocol v0.3, sections 3 and 12a).
2: 
3:     python launch.py --runs F1:9001 F2:9001 ... [--workers 8] [--tag tech] [--set key=value ...]
4: 
5: Each run is a separate process with OMP_NUM_THREADS = MKL_NUM_THREADS = 1 and writes to
6: runs/<tag>/<family>_s<seed>/ (stdout and stderr in run.log). A summary with wall times is written to
7: runs/<tag>/launch_summary.json.
8: 
9: A run whose folder already holds a complete result (meta.json, written last, with every array file present
10: and the same resolved configuration) is skipped, so an interrupted campaign is resumed by repeating the
11: same command; runs are bit-identical, so a run cut off midway is simply redone from the start. An earlier
12: run.log of an incomplete run is kept as run.log.prevN (nothing is deleted). A completed run with a
13: different resolved configuration stops the launcher.
14: """
15: import argparse
16: import json
17: import os
18: import subprocess
19: import sys
20: import time
21: from pathlib import Path
22: 
23: import numpy as np
24: 
25: from p2.run import load_config, array_hashes
26: 
27: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
28: ARRAYS = ["steps", "evals", "episodes", "disp", "chunks", "world"]
29: 
30: 
31: def completed(out, cfg, verify):
32:     """True if out holds a complete run with configuration cfg; raises on a configuration mismatch."""
33:     m = out / "meta.json"
34:     if not m.exists() or not all((out / f"{n}.npz").exists() for n in ARRAYS):
35:         return False
36:     meta = json.loads(m.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
37:     run_part = lambda c: {k: v for k, v in c.items() if k not in ("analysis", "meta")}
38:     if run_part(meta["config"]) != run_part(cfg):
39:         raise SystemExit(f"{out}: completed run with a different resolved configuration; use another --tag")
40:     if verify:
41:         for n in ARRAYS:
42:             with np.load(out / f"{n}.npz") as z:
43:                 if array_hashes({k: z[k] for k in z.files}) != meta["sha256_arrays"][n]:
44:                     raise SystemExit(f"{out}: array hashes of {n}.npz do not match meta.json")
45:     return True
46: 
47: 
48: def main():
49:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
50:     ap.add_argument("--runs", nargs="+", required=True, help="FAMILY:SEED items")
51:     ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=8)
52:     ap.add_argument("--tag", default="tech")
53:     ap.add_argument("--set", action="append", default=[])
54:     ap.add_argument("--verify-skipped", action="store_true", help="re-hash the arrays of skipped runs")
55:     ap.add_argument("--pin-pcores", action="store_true",
56:                     help="pin worker i to logical CPU 2i (one thread per P-core, i5-12500H: 0, 2, 4, 6); results are "
57:                          "bit-identical either way (pin_test.py, 6 Nov 2026: childhood -15%%)")
58:     a = ap.parse_args()
59:     if a.pin_pcores and a.workers > 4:
60:         raise SystemExit("--pin-pcores supports at most 4 workers (4 P-cores)")
61:     cfg, _ = load_config(HERE / "config_stage0.yaml", a.set)
62:     env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS="1", MKL_NUM_THREADS="1", PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")
63:     base = HERE / "runs" / a.tag
64:     base.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
65:     queue = [tuple(item.split(":")) for item in a.runs]
66:     running, done = [], []
67:     t0 = time.time()
68:     while queue or running:
69:         while queue and len(running) < a.workers:
70:             if (HERE / "runs" / "_HW_STOP").exists():          # hardware watchdog (hw_watchdog.py): start nothing new
71:                 print(json.dumps({"stopped_by_watchdog": (HERE / "runs" / "_HW_STOP").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip(),
72:                                   "not_started": [f"{f}:{s}" for f, s in queue]}), flush=True)
73:                 queue.clear()
74:                 break
75:             fam, seed = queue.pop(0)
76:             out = base / f"{fam}_s{seed}"
77:             if completed(out, cfg, a.verify_skipped):
78:                 done.append({"family": fam, "seed": int(seed), "returncode": 0, "skipped": True})
79:                 print(json.dumps(done[-1]), flush=True)
80:                 continue
81:             out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
82:             if (out / "run.log").exists():
83:                 i = 1
84:                 while (out / f"run.log.prev{i}").exists():
85:                     i += 1
86:                 (out / "run.log").rename(out / f"run.log.prev{i}")
87:             cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "p2.run", "--family", fam, "--seed", seed, "--out", str(out)]
88:             for s in a.set:
89:                 cmd += ["--set", s]
90:             log = open(out / "run.log", "w", encoding="utf-8")
91:             p = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=HERE, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
92:             if a.pin_pcores:
93:                 import ctypes
94:                 used = {r[5] for r in running}
95:                 slot = next(i for i in range(4) if i not in used)
96:                 ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetProcessAffinityMask(ctypes.c_void_p(int(p._handle)), ctypes.c_size_t(1 << (2 * slot)))
97:             else:
98:                 slot = None
99:             running.append((p, fam, seed, time.time(), log, slot))
100:         time.sleep(2)
101:         for item in list(running):
102:             p, fam, seed, ts, log, _slot = item
103:             if p.poll() is not None:
104:                 log.close()
105:                 running.remove(item)
106:                 done.append({"family": fam, "seed": int(seed), "returncode": p.returncode,
107:                              "wall_s": round(time.time() - ts, 1)})
108:                 print(json.dumps(done[-1]), flush=True)
109:     if (base / "launch_summary.json").exists():
110:         i = 1
111:         while (base / f"launch_summary.prev{i}.json").exists():
112:             i += 1
113:         (base / "launch_summary.json").rename(base / f"launch_summary.prev{i}.json")
114:     summary = {"workers": a.workers, "overrides": a.set, "total_wall_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "runs": done}
115:     (base / "launch_summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
116:     print(json.dumps(summary, indent=1))
117:     return 0 if all(d["returncode"] == 0 for d in done) else 1
118: 
119: 
120: if __name__ == "__main__":
121:     raise SystemExit(main())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\membw.py
1: # STREAM-like memory bandwidth test with torch on CPU (copy and triad), threads and processes.
2: # Arrays of 2^26 float32 (256 MB) each, far larger than the L3 cache (18 MB on i5-12500H).
3: import sys, time, os
4: import multiprocessing as mp
5: 
6: N = 1 << 26  # elements per array
7: B = N * 4    # bytes per array
8: 
9: 
10: def kernel(nthreads, reps=8):
11:     import torch
12:     torch.set_num_threads(nthreads)
13:     a = torch.ones(N); b = torch.full((N,), 2.0); c = torch.empty(N)
14:     best_copy = best_triad = 0.0
15:     for _ in range(reps):
16:         t = time.perf_counter(); c.copy_(a); dt = time.perf_counter() - t
17:         best_copy = max(best_copy, 2 * B / dt / 1e9)
18:         t = time.perf_counter(); torch.add(a, b, alpha=3.0, out=c); dt = time.perf_counter() - t
19:         best_triad = max(best_triad, 3 * B / dt / 1e9)
20:     assert float(c[12345]) == 7.0
21:     return best_copy, best_triad
22: 
23: 
24: def worker(barrier, q, reps):
25:     import torch
26:     torch.set_num_threads(1)
27:     a = torch.ones(N); b = torch.full((N,), 2.0); c = torch.empty(N)
28:     torch.add(a, b, alpha=3.0, out=c)
29:     barrier.wait()
30:     t = time.perf_counter()
31:     for _ in range(reps):
32:         torch.add(a, b, alpha=3.0, out=c)
33:     q.put(reps * 3 * B / (time.perf_counter() - t) / 1e9)
34: 
35: 
36: if __name__ == "__main__":
37:     print("threads | copy GB/s | triad GB/s (best of 8, one process)")
38:     for th in [1, 2, 4, 8, 12]:
39:         cp, tr = kernel(th)
40:         print(f"{th:7d} | {cp:9.1f} | {tr:10.1f}")
41:     print("\nprocesses (1 thread each, simultaneous triad) | total GB/s | per process")
42:     for P in [1, 2, 4, 6, 8]:
43:         bar = mp.Barrier(P); q = mp.Queue()
44:         ps = [mp.Process(target=worker, args=(bar, q, 12)) for _ in range(P)]
45:         [p.start() for p in ps]; r = [q.get() for _ in ps]; [p.join() for p in ps]
46:         print(f"{P:46d} | {sum(r):10.1f} | {sum(r)/P:.1f}")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\pin_test.py
1: # Deep Review B7 (6 Nov 2026): does pinning the 4 workers to the P-cores change throughput? Run from Stadiul0:
2: #   python pin_test.py
3: # Three conditions in sequence, each = 4 childhood probes (3,072 steps, the same work as in a run) in parallel:
4: #   free (Windows scheduler), P-cores (logical 0, 2, 4, 6: one thread per physical P-core), E-cores (logical 8-11).
5: # Assumes the usual Alder Lake numbering (P-core threads first); the result itself shows whether it holds.
6: import ctypes, json, subprocess, sys, time
7: from pathlib import Path
8: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
9: COND = {"liber": None, "nuclee_P": [1 << 0, 1 << 2, 1 << 4, 1 << 6], "nuclee_E": [1 << 8, 1 << 9, 1 << 10, 1 << 11]}
10: res = {}
11: for name, masks in COND.items():
12:     if (HERE / "runs" / "_HW_STOP").exists():
13:         print("OPRIT de paznic"); sys.exit(3)
14:     ps, t0 = [], time.time()
15:     for i in range(4):
16:         p = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "calib_probe.py", "childhood", "--lr", "3e-4", "--seed", str(9200 + i),
17:                               "--family", "F2"], cwd=HERE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True)
18:         if masks:
19:             ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetProcessAffinityMask(ctypes.c_void_p(int(p._handle)), ctypes.c_size_t(masks[i]))
20:         ps.append(p)
21:     outs = [json.loads(p.communicate()[0].strip().splitlines()[-1]) for p in ps]
22:     res[name] = {"wall_total_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "wall_each_s": [o["wall_s"] for o in outs],
23:                  "acc_reference": [round(o["childhood"]["acc_reference"], 4) for o in outs]}
24:     print(name, json.dumps(res[name]), flush=True)
25: (HERE / "runs" / "pin_test_2026-11-06.json").write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
26: same = all(res[k]["acc_reference"] == res["liber"]["acc_reference"] for k in res)
27: print("acuratetea identica in toate conditiile (determinism, independent de nuclee):", same)

FILE repository\Stadiul0\preview_headroom.py
1: # Usage (from Stadiul0): python preview_headroom.py runs/tech2_lr3e-4  (needs triplets.parquet from stage0_summary.py)
2: """Headroom preview (G3-type quantity) on technical runs: R^2 of Delta N alone and of B_strong (no c_e).
3: Leave-one-run-out, untuned GBDT, episode weights, weighted within-run R^2. c_e is deliberately NOT used."""
4: import sys
5: import numpy as np
6: import pandas as pd
7: from pathlib import Path
8: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
9: 
10: tag = Path(sys.argv[1])
11: dfs = [pd.read_parquet(p) for p in sorted(tag.glob("*/triplets.parquet"))]
12: df = pd.concat([d[d["incl"]] for d in dfs], ignore_index=True)
13: df["run"] = df["family"] + "_" + df["seed"].astype(str)
14: df["C1_a"] = df["dN"]; df["C1_b"] = np.log(df["dN"] + 1); df["C1_c"] = np.log(df["dN"] + 10); df["C1_d"] = np.log(df["dN"] + 100)
15: 
16: xcov = ["x_rule", "x_log_nvalues", "x_LB0", "x_AB0", "x_prior_LB", "x_overlap", "x_N0", "x_tenure",
17:         "x_prior_exposures", "x_K_active", "x_theta_norm0", "x_sqrt_trv0"]
18: counts = ["C0", "C1_a", "C1_b", "C1_c", "C1_d"]
19: bstd = counts + ["C2", "C3", "C4", "C5", "C7a"]
20: c6 = [c for c in df.columns if c.startswith("C6_")]
21: bstrong = xcov + bstd + ["C5a", "C5b_g", "C5b_gt", "C5b_L", "C5c_sq", "C5c_work", "C5d", "C5e", "C5f", "C2b",
22:                          "C6b_theta", "C6b_v", "C10", "C10b"] + c6 + \
23:           [f"rate_{z}" for z in ["C2", "C2b", "C3", "C4", "C5", "C5a", "C5b_g", "C5b_gt", "C5b_L", "C5c_sq",
24:                                  "C5c_work", "C5d", "C5e", "C5f", "C10", "C10b"]]
25: assert not any(c.startswith("ce_") or c.startswith("Disp") for c in bstrong)
26: 
27: 
28: def r2w(y, p, w):
29:     m = (w * y).sum() / w.sum()
30:     return 1 - (w * (y - p) ** 2).sum() / (w * (y - m) ** 2).sum()
31: 
32: 
33: def wcorr(x, y, w):
34:     w = w / w.sum(); mx, my = (w * x).sum(), (w * y).sum()
35:     return (w * (x - mx) * (y - my)).sum() / np.sqrt((w * (x - mx) ** 2).sum() * (w * (y - my) ** 2).sum())
36: 
37: 
38: rows = []
39: for r in sorted(df["run"].unique()):
40:     tr, te = df[df["run"] != r], df[df["run"] == r]
41:     out = {"test_run": r, "n": len(te), "r_AB": wcorr(te["F_A"].values, te["F_B"].values, te["w"].values)}
42:     for name, cols in [("dN_only", ["C1_a", "C1_b", "C1_c", "C1_d"]), ("dN+X_cov", counts[1:] + xcov),
43:                        ("B_standard+X_cov", bstd + xcov), ("B_strong", bstrong)]:
44:         m = HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=200, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20,
45:                                           random_state=0)
46:         m.fit(tr[cols], tr["F_A"], sample_weight=tr["w"])
47:         out[name] = r2w(te["F_A"].values, m.predict(te[cols]), te["w"].values)
48:     rows.append(out)
49: res = pd.DataFrame(rows)
50: res["headroom_vs_B_strong"] = res["r_AB"] - res["B_strong"]
51: pd.set_option("display.width", 200)
52: print(res.round(3).to_string(index=False))
53: # shape of forgetting vs Delta N (pooled)
54: bins = [64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 1e9]
55: df["dN_bin"] = pd.cut(df["dN"], bins, right=False)
56: g = df.groupby("dN_bin", observed=True).apply(lambda d: pd.Series({"n": len(d), "F_A_mean": np.average(d["F_A"], weights=d["w"]),
57:                                                     "F_A_sd": d["F_A"].std(), "dAcc_mean": np.average(d["dAcc_A"], weights=d["w"])}))
58: print(g.round(3).to_string())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\reproduce_analyses.py
1: # Re-runs the analyses of Pilot 2 from the stored results and compares what they print and write with the stored logs and
2: # JSON files. Written for the archive on 9 Nov 2026, after the study; it changes nothing in the analyses themselves.
3: #   Usage, from the repository root, after unpacking the Zenodo data part into it:  python -I Stadiul0/reproduce_analyses.py
4: #   Optional: names of checks to run (e.g. T-A T-B); default: all.
5: # Every stored JSON file that an analysis rewrites is restored byte for byte afterwards, so SHA256SUMS.txt still verifies.
6: # Four scripts contain the author's absolute path to Stadiul0/runs; they run from a temporary copy in their own folder with
7: # that path replaced, and the copy is removed afterwards. CPU only; about 3 minutes on the author's laptop.
8: # Library versions of the original runs: Python 3.14.6, numpy 2.5.1, pandas 3.0.3, scikit-learn 1.9.0, scipy 1.18.0,
9: # torch 2.13.0+cpu. With these, every stored log was reproduced line for line on 9 Nov 2026; three JSON files differ only in
10: # the last digits of regression coefficients (relative difference below 1e-15), which the comparison below tolerates.
11: import json, re, subprocess, sys, time
12: from pathlib import Path
13: 
14: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
15: S = Path(__file__).resolve().parent  # Stadiul0
16: # the author's absolute path, as written in four scripts: r"D:\Time\02_Pilot2\Stadiul0\runs..."
17: ABS_RE = re.compile(r'r"D:\\Time\\02_Pilot2\\Stadiul0((?:\\[^"\\]+)*)"')
18: TOL = 1e-9
19: 
20: L, F = "lm", "forever_repro"
21: # name, folder (relative to Stadiul0), arguments, stored log (relative to Stadiul0) or None, JSON files the script writes
22: CHECKS = [
23:     ("T-A", ".", ["ta_analysis.py"], "runs/ta_analysis.log", ["runs/ta_verdict.json"]),
24:     ("T-B", ".", ["tb_part1_g2.py"], "runs/tb_part1_g2.log", ["runs/tb_part1_verdict.json"]),
25:     ("T-D", ".", ["td_audit.py"], "runs/td_audit.log", []),
26:     ("T-C", L, ["lm_d3_competitors.py", "../runs/lmtech1", "../runs/lmtech2", "../runs/lmsens_bf16", "../runs/lmsens_a32"],
27:      "runs/tc_lm_d3_competitors.log", []),
28:     ("SuperNI", ".", ["sni_analysis.py"], "runs/sni_analysis_final.log", ["runs/sni_verdict.json"]),
29:     ("SuperNI-S2-secondary", ".", ["sni_s2_secondary.py"], "runs/sni_S2_secundar.log", []),  # script written 9 Nov
30:     ("LM-tau-lmtech1", L, ["lm_forever_g2.py", "../runs/lmtech1"], "runs/lmtech1_g2.log", []),
31:     ("LM-tau-lmtech2", L, ["lm_forever_g2.py", "../runs/lmtech2"], "runs/lmtech2_g2.log", []),
32:     ("LM-tau-bf16", L, ["lm_forever_g2.py", "../runs/lmsens_bf16"], "runs/lmsens_bf16_g2.log", []),
33:     ("LM-tau-a32", L, ["lm_forever_g2.py", "../runs/lmsens_a32"], "runs/lmsens_a32_g2.log", []),
34:     ("tech-G2", ".", ["g2_preview.py"], None, []),  # no output was stored when it ran; its numbers are report §5.1, table 1
35:     ("G2-CPU-checks", ".", ["g2_cpu_checks.py"], "runs/g2_cpu_checks_2026-11-06.log", []),
36:     ("tech4", ".", ["tech4_analysis.py"], "runs/tech4_analysis.log", []),
37:     ("FOREVER-R2-reconstruction", F, ["r2_reconstruction.py"], "runs/forever_repro_r2_reconstruction.log",
38:      ["runs/forever_repro_r2_reconstruction.json"]),
39:     ("FOREVER-R2-arm-eta_m", F, ["r2_reconstruction_arm.py", "eta_m"], None, ["runs/forever_repetari_r2_eta_m.json"]),
40:     ("FOREVER-R2-arm-eta_m_A1", F, ["r2_reconstruction_arm.py", "eta_m_A1"], "runs/forever_repetari_r2.log",
41:      ["runs/forever_repetari_r2_eta_m_A1.json"]),
42:     ("FOREVER-d", F, ["d_same_trajectory.py"], None, ["runs/forever_d_same_trajectory.json"]),
43:     ("FOREVER-stage1", F, ["rep_verdict.py"], None, ["runs/forever_repetari_verdict.json"]),
44:     ("FOREVER-drift-ratio", F, ["drift_ratio_posthoc.py"], "runs/forever_drift_ratio_posthoc.log", []),  # post hoc, 9 Nov
45:     # post hoc, after the final review round of 9 Nov (Recenzii/Runda_finala_2026-11-09): (a), (b), (c)
46:     ("POSTHOC-a-eval-noise", F, ["eval_noise_summary.py"], "runs/forever_eval_noise_posthoc_summary.log",
47:      ["runs/forever_eval_noise_posthoc.json"]),
48:     ("POSTHOC-b-TA-memory", ".", ["ta_memory_posthoc.py"], "runs/ta_memory_posthoc.log", ["runs/ta_memory_posthoc.json"]),
49:     ("POSTHOC-c-skipped-steps", F, ["skip_check_posthoc.py"], "runs/forever_skip_check_posthoc.log", []),
50: ]
51: 
52: 
53: def clean(text):
54:     """Lines of a log without Python warnings (they carry local paths) and the source line printed under each."""
55:     out, skip = [], False
56:     for line in text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n"):
57:         if re.search(r"(Warning|warn)\b.*:", line) and ("\\" in line or "/" in line):
58:             skip = True
59:             continue
60:         if skip and line.startswith("  "):
61:             skip = False
62:             continue
63:         skip = False
64:         out.append(line.rstrip())
65:     while out and not out[-1]:
66:         out.pop()
67:     return out
68: 
69: 
70: def leaves(x, p=""):
71:     if isinstance(x, dict):
72:         for k, v in x.items():
73:             yield from leaves(v, f"{p}/{k}")
74:     elif isinstance(x, list):
75:         for i, v in enumerate(x):
76:             yield from leaves(v, f"{p}[{i}]")
77:     else:
78:         yield p, x
79: 
80: 
81: def same_json(a, b):
82:     la, lb = dict(leaves(a)), dict(leaves(b))
83:     if la.keys() != lb.keys():
84:         return False, "different keys"
85:     worst = 0.0
86:     for k, x in la.items():
87:         y = lb[k]
88:         if x == y:
89:             continue
90:         if isinstance(x, (int, float)) and isinstance(y, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool):
91:             d = abs(x - y) / max(1.0, abs(x))
92:             if d > TOL:
93:                 return False, f"{k}: {x!r} vs {y!r}"
94:             worst = max(worst, d)
95:         else:
96:             return False, f"{k}: {x!r} vs {y!r}"
97:     return True, ("identical" if worst == 0 else f"equal within {worst:.1e}")
98: 
99: 
100: def run(name, folder, args):
101:     cwd = S / folder
102:     script = cwd / args[0]
103:     src = script.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
104:     tmp = None
105:     new, n = ABS_RE.subn(lambda m: repr(str(S.joinpath(*[q for q in m.group(1).split("\\") if q]))), src)
106:     if n:
107:         tmp = cwd / f"_repro_tmp_{script.stem}.py"
108:         tmp.write_text(new, encoding="utf-8")
109:         args = [tmp.name] + args[1:]
110:     try:
111:         cmd = [sys.executable, "-I", "-X", "utf8"] + args
112:         p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
113:         if p.returncode != 0 and "ModuleNotFoundError" in p.stderr:
114:             p = subprocess.run(cmd[:1] + cmd[2:], cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
115:     finally:
116:         if tmp is not None and tmp.exists():
117:             tmp.unlink()
118:     return p
119: 
120: 
121: def main():
122:     import numpy, pandas, sklearn, scipy
123:     try:
124:         import torch
125:         tv = torch.__version__
126:     except ImportError:
127:         tv = "not installed (needed by T-C)"
128:     print(f"Python {sys.version.split()[0]}, numpy {numpy.__version__}, pandas {pandas.__version__}, "
129:           f"scikit-learn {sklearn.__version__}, scipy {scipy.__version__}, torch {tv}\n")
130:     want = set(sys.argv[1:])
131:     n_ok = n_all = 0
132:     for name, folder, args, log, jsons in CHECKS:
133:         if want and name not in want:
134:             continue
135:         n_all += 1
136:         saved = {j: (S / j).read_bytes() for j in jsons}
137:         t0 = time.time()
138:         try:
139:             p = run(name, folder, args)
140:             msgs, ok = [f"rc {p.returncode}, {time.time() - t0:.0f} s"], p.returncode == 0
141:             if log:
142:                 a, b = clean((S / log).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")), clean(p.stdout)
143:                 if a == b:
144:                     msgs.append(f"output = {log} ({len(a)} lines)")
145:                 else:
146:                     ok = False
147:                     d = [i for i, (x, y) in enumerate(zip(a, b)) if x != y]
148:                     first = d[0] if d else min(len(a), len(b))
149:                     msgs.append(f"output differs from {log} (stored {len(a)} lines, new {len(b)}; first at line {first + 1})")
150:             elif not jsons:
151:                 msgs.append("no stored output; printed below")
152:             for j in jsons:
153:                 good, why = same_json(json.loads(saved[j]), json.loads((S / j).read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
154:                 ok &= good
155:                 msgs.append(f"{Path(j).name}: {why}")
156:         finally:
157:             for j, data in saved.items():  # restore the stored files exactly
158:                 (S / j).write_bytes(data)
159:         n_ok += ok
160:         print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'DIFFERS'}] {name}: " + "; ".join(msgs), flush=True)
161:         if (not log and not jsons) or p.returncode != 0:
162:             print("    " + "\n    ".join((p.stdout + p.stderr).strip().splitlines()[:40]))
163:     print(f"\n{n_ok} of {n_all} checks reproduce the stored results.")
164: 
165: 
166: if __name__ == "__main__":
167:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\sni_analysis.py
1: # SuperNI check analysis (S1, S2), as fixed in preinregistrare_SuperNI_2026-11-07.md. Run from Stadiul0: python -I sni_analysis.py
2: import sys, json, numpy as np, pandas as pd
3: from pathlib import Path
4: from scipy import stats
5: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
6: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
7: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
8: R = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "runs" / "sni"
9: W = 8
10: 
11: def windows(rd):
12:     z = np.load(rd / "steps.npz"); S = pd.DataFrame(z["data"], columns=list(z["cols"]))
13:     S["eta_m"] = S.lr * S.m_hat_norm; rows = []
14:     for (task, ph), g in S[S.counted == 1].groupby(["task", "phase"]):
15:         g = g.sort_values("gstep")
16:         for j in range(len(g) // W):
17:             w = g.iloc[j * W:(j + 1) * W]
18:             rows.append(dict(run=rd.name, task=int(task), y=w.delta.mean(), S_eta=w.lr.sum(), S_eta_m=w.eta_m.sum(),
19:                              lr=w.lr.mean(), g=w.gnorm.mean(), gc=w.gnorm_clipped.mean(), m=w.m_hat_norm.mean(),
20:                              v=w.sqrt_trv.mean(), loss=w.loss.mean(), sip=w.step_in_phase.mean(), kind=int(w.kind.iloc[0]),
21:                              ntok=w.ntok.mean()))
22:     return rows
23: 
24: def r2(y, p):
25:     return 1 - ((y - p) ** 2).sum() / ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum()
26: 
27: tau_runs = sorted(p for p in (R / "tau").glob("s*") if (p / "meta.json").exists())
28: D = pd.DataFrame([r for rd in tau_runs for r in windows(rd)])
29: print(f"S1: rulări τ {[p.name for p in tau_runs]}; ferestre {len(D)}; pe sarcini {D.groupby('task').size().to_dict()}")
30: full = ["lr", "g", "gc", "m", "v", "loss", "sip", "kind", "task", "ntok"]
31: models = {"Ση": ["S_eta"], "Ση‖m̂‖": ["S_eta_m"], "Ση + Ση‖m̂‖": ["S_eta", "S_eta_m"], "GBDT telemetrie": full}
32: res = {}
33: for scheme, key in [("o sămânță lăsată deoparte", "run"), ("o sarcină lăsată deoparte", "task")]:
34:     print(f"\n  {scheme}:")
35:     for nm, cols in models.items():
36:         rr = []
37:         for test in sorted(D[key].unique()):
38:             tr, te = D[key] != test, D[key] == test
39:             mdl = (HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20,
40:                                                  random_state=0) if nm.startswith("GBDT") else LinearRegression())
41:             if te.sum() < 3:
42:                 continue
43:             rr.append(r2(D.loc[te, "y"].to_numpy(), mdl.fit(D.loc[tr, cols], D.loc[tr, "y"]).predict(D.loc[te, cols])))
44:         res[(scheme, nm)] = (float(np.mean(rr)), float(np.min(rr)), [round(x, 3) for x in rr])
45:         print(f"    {nm:18s} R² mediu {np.mean(rr):.3f}  minim {np.min(rr):.3f}  pe pliuri {res[(scheme, nm)][2]}")
46: s1 = all(res[(s, "Ση + Ση‖m̂‖")][0] >= 0.90 for s in ["o sămânță lăsată deoparte", "o sarcină lăsată deoparte"])
47: print("\nS1:", "RECONSTRUCȚIA SE EXTINDE la SuperNI" if s1 else "NU se extinde (concluzia rămâne limitată la faptele sintetice)")
48: out = {"S1_extends": s1, "results": {f"{k[0]} | {k[1]}": v for k, v in res.items()}}
49: 
50: eta_runs = sorted(p for p in (R / "eta_m").glob("s*") if (p / "meta.json").exists())
51: seeds = [p.name for p in eta_runs if (R / "tau" / p.name / "meta.json").exists()]
52: if seeds:
53:     E = lambda rd: float(np.nanmean(np.load(rd / "evals.npz")["loss"][-1]))
54:     d = np.array([(E(R / "tau" / s) - E(R / "eta_m" / s)) / E(R / "tau" / s) for s in seeds]); n = len(d)
55:     half = stats.t.ppf(0.975, n - 1) * d.std(ddof=1) / np.sqrt(n) if n > 1 else np.inf
56:     lo, hi = d.mean() - half, d.mean() + half
57:     v = ("τ SUPERIOR" if d.mean() <= -0.05 and hi < 0 else "ECHIVALENȚĂ PRACTICĂ" if (lo > -0.05 and hi < 0.05) else "NECONCLUDENT")
58:     print(f"\nS2 ({n} semințe): diferența relativă E1(τ) − E1(eta_m) = {d.mean():+.4f}, IC 95% [{lo:+.4f}; {hi:+.4f}] -> {v}")
59:     for s in seeds:
60:         print(f"  {s}: E1 τ {E(R / 'tau' / s):.4f}, eta_m {E(R / 'eta_m' / s):.4f}")
61:     out["S2"] = dict(seeds=seeds, rel_diff=float(d.mean()), ci=[float(lo), float(hi)], verdict=v)
62: (R.parent / "sni_verdict.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\sni_s2_secondary.py
1: # The quantities are preregistered secondaries; only this script is later. Written on 9 Nov 2026 so that the S2 numbers quoted in the closure report
2: # (section 5.2: replay triggers and old-task loss) can be recomputed from the archive. On 7 Nov the same numbers were
3: # printed by a command typed by hand and saved as runs/sni_S2_secundar.log; this script reproduces that file byte for byte.
4: # The secondary quantities are those listed in preinregistrare_SuperNI_2026-11-07.md, with the definitions of ta_analysis.py:
5: #   old-task loss before the final replay: mean over tasks 1-4 of the mean test loss on the earlier tasks (evals_pre.npz);
6: #   |difference of the trigger step|: counted steps of a task before each mid-task replay, tau versus eta_m, median per seed;
7: #   replays: number of mid-task replays (phase kind 2) per run.
8: # Usage, from Stadiul0:  python -I sni_s2_secondary.py   (prints only; writes nothing)
9: import sys
10: from pathlib import Path
11: import numpy as np
12: 
13: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
14: R = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "runs" / "sni"
15: 
16: 
17: def info(rd):
18:     p = np.load(rd / "evals_pre.npz")
19:     old = float(np.mean([np.nanmean(p["loss"][i, :int(t)]) for i, t in enumerate(p["task"])]))
20:     z = np.load(rd / "steps.npz"); cols = list(z["cols"]); D = z["data"]; ph = z["phases"]
21:     task, gstep, counted = D[:, cols.index("task")], D[:, cols.index("gstep")], D[:, cols.index("counted")]
22:     trig = {}
23:     for t_, kind, start, *_ in ph:
24:         if kind == 2:  # mid-task replay
25:             trig.setdefault(int(t_), []).append(int(((task == t_) & (counted == 1) & (gstep <= start)).sum()))
26:     return old, trig
27: 
28: 
29: for s in ["s7401", "s7402", "s7403"]:
30:     a, ta = info(R / "tau" / s); b, tb = info(R / "eta_m" / s)
31:     diffs = [abs(x - y) for t in ta if t in tb for x, y in zip(ta[t], tb[t])]
32:     print(f"{s} | pierderea pe sarcinile vechi înainte de repetiția finală: τ {a:.4f} eta_m {b:.4f} | "
33:           f"|Δ pas declanșare| mediana {np.median(diffs):.1f} | repetiții τ / eta_m {sum(map(len, ta.values()))} / "
34:           f"{sum(map(len, tb.values()))}")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\stage0_summary.py
1: """Summary of a set of Stage 0 runs: measurability (section 12a, first row), timing and memory.
2: 
3:     python stage0_summary.py runs/tech1        (writes runs/tech1/summary.json and summary.md)
4: """
5: import json
6: import sys
7: from pathlib import Path
8: 
9: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
10: sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
11: 
12: from p2.triplets import build, run_summary      # noqa: E402
13: 
14: 
15: def main(tag_dir):
16:     base = Path(tag_dir)
17:     rows = []
18:     for d in sorted(p for p in base.iterdir() if (p / "meta.json").exists()):
19:         df, meta = build(d)
20:         df.to_parquet(d / "triplets.parquet")
21:         s = run_summary(df, meta)
22:         s["life_steps"] = meta["life_steps"]
23:         s["ms_per_train_step"] = round(1000 * s["timing_s"]["train"] / (meta["life_steps"]), 1)
24:         s["n_evals"] = meta["n_evals"]
25:         rows.append(s)
26:     (base / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=1, default=float), encoding="utf-8")
27:     cols = ["family", "childhood_acc", "childhood_acc_rule", "episodes", "valid_episodes", "episodes_competent_fact",
28:             "episodes_fact", "episodes_competent_rule", "episodes_rule", "included", "r_AB", "F_A_mean", "F_A_sd",
29:             "dAcc_A_mean", "rate_D1_cv", "n_evals", "ms_per_train_step"]
30:     lines = ["| " + " | ".join(cols) + " | wall min | peak MB |", "|" + "---|" * (len(cols) + 2)]
31:     for s in rows:
32:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(str(s.get(c, "")) for c in cols)
33:                      + f" | {s['timing_s']['wall'] / 60:.1f} | {s['peak_rss_mb']} |")
34:     (base / "summary.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
35:     print("\n".join(lines))
36: 
37: 
38: if __name__ == "__main__":
39:     main(sys.argv[1])

FILE repository\Stadiul0\ta_analysis.py
1: # T-A analysis, as fixed in preinregistrare_TA_TB_2026-11-06.md. Run from Stadiul0: python -I ta_analysis.py
2: # E1 final average accuracy (last row of evals.npz); E2 BWT; E3 mean old-task accuracy just before each final replay
3: # (evals_pre.npz, tasks 1-9); E4 replay counts and |difference of the trigger step| vs tau (counted steps of the task
4: # before the j-th replay; median over tasks, per threshold index). Paired by seed: tau vs eta_m, eta, steps.
5: import json, sys, numpy as np
6: from pathlib import Path
7: from scipy import stats
8: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
9: R = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "runs" / "ta"
10: CLOCKS = ["tau", "eta_m", "eta", "steps"]; DELTA = 0.02
11: seeds = sorted({p.name for c in CLOCKS for p in (R / c).glob("s*") if (p / "meta.json").exists()})
12: seeds = [s for s in seeds if all((R / c / s / "meta.json").exists() for c in CLOCKS)]   # complete seed sets only
13: 
14: def endpoints(rd):
15:     e = np.load(rd / "evals.npz"); acc = e["acc"]; n = acc.shape[1]
16:     E1 = float(np.nanmean(acc[-1])); E2 = float(np.mean([acc[-1, t] - acc[t, t] for t in range(n - 1)]))
17:     p = np.load(rd / "evals_pre.npz"); E3 = float(np.mean([np.nanmean(p["acc"][i, :int(t)]) for i, t in enumerate(p["task"])]))
18:     z = np.load(rd / "steps.npz"); cols = list(z["cols"]); D = z["data"]; ph = z["phases"]
19:     task, gstep, counted = D[:, cols.index("task")], D[:, cols.index("gstep")], D[:, cols.index("counted")]
20:     trig = {}
21:     for t_, kind, start, *_ in ph:
22:         if kind == 2:                                     # mid-task replay
23:             trig.setdefault(int(t_), []).append(int(((task == t_) & (counted == 1) & (gstep <= start)).sum()))
24:     return dict(E1=E1, E2=E2, E3=E3, trig=trig, n_replay=sum(len(v) for v in trig.values()))
25: 
26: V = {c: {s: endpoints(R / c / s) for s in seeds} for c in CLOCKS}
27: print(f"semințe complete: {seeds}")
28: for c in CLOCKS:
29:     a = V[c]
30:     print(f"  {c:6s} E1 {np.mean([a[s]['E1'] for s in seeds]):.4f}  E2 {np.mean([a[s]['E2'] for s in seeds]):+.4f}  "
31:           f"E3 {np.mean([a[s]['E3'] for s in seeds]):.4f}  repetiții medii {np.mean([a[s]['n_replay'] for s in seeds]):.1f}")
32: out = {}
33: for c in CLOCKS[1:]:
34:     d = np.array([V["tau"][s]["E1"] - V[c][s]["E1"] for s in seeds]); n = len(d)
35:     half = stats.t.ppf(0.975, n - 1) * d.std(ddof=1) / np.sqrt(n) if n > 1 else np.inf
36:     lo, hi = d.mean() - half, d.mean() + half
37:     sep = {}
38:     for j in range(3):
39:         diffs = [abs(V["tau"][s]["trig"][t][j] - V[c][s]["trig"][t][j]) for s in seeds for t in V["tau"][s]["trig"]
40:                  if t in V[c][s]["trig"] and len(V["tau"][s]["trig"][t]) > j and len(V[c][s]["trig"][t]) > j]
41:         if diffs:
42:             sep[f"d_index_{j}"] = float(np.median(diffs))
43:     equiv_sched = bool(sep) and all(v <= 2 for v in sep.values())
44:     verdict = ("τ SUPERIOR" if d.mean() >= DELTA and lo > 0 else
45:                "ECHIVALENȚĂ PRACTICĂ" if (lo > -DELTA and hi < DELTA) else "NECONCLUDENT")
46:     out[c] = dict(mean_diff=float(d.mean()), ci=[float(lo), float(hi)], separation=sep, schedule_equivalent=equiv_sched,
47:                   verdict=verdict, dE2=float(np.mean([V["tau"][s]["E2"] - V[c][s]["E2"] for s in seeds])),
48:                   dE3=float(np.mean([V["tau"][s]["E3"] - V[c][s]["E3"] for s in seeds])))
49:     print(f"\nτ față de {c}: E1(τ) − E1({c}) = {d.mean():+.4f}, IC 95% [{lo:+.4f}; {hi:+.4f}] -> {verdict}")
50:     print(f"  separarea declanșărilor (mediana |Δ pas|, pe indicele pragului): {sep} -> "
51:           f"{'ECHIVALENTE ca planificare (≤ 2 pași)' if equiv_sched else 'separate'}")
52:     print(f"  ΔE2 (BWT) {out[c]['dE2']:+.4f}; ΔE3 (vechi, înainte de repetiția finală) {out[c]['dE3']:+.4f}")
53: (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "runs" / "ta_verdict.json").write_text(json.dumps({"seeds": seeds, **out}, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\ta_memory_posthoc.py
1: # POST HOC test (b), 9 Nov 2026, after the final review round: T-A retention on the 16 facts per task held in memory.
2: # Reads runs/ta_memory_facts_posthoc.npz (written by lm/ta_memory_facts_posthoc.py from the stored final LoRA factors).
3: # Per clock: final recall of the memory facts of tasks 0-8 and of the other facts of those tasks (the latter from the
4: # stored per-task accuracy minus the memory facts recalled); per task, the memory recall averaged over seeds; paired by
5: # seed, tau minus each other clock on memory recall, with a t interval of 95% (descriptive; no margin was fixed for it).
6: # Writes runs/ta_memory_posthoc.json. Run from Stadiul0: python -I ta_memory_posthoc.py
7: import json, sys
8: from pathlib import Path
9: import numpy as np
10: from scipy import stats
11: 
12: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
13: S = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
14: z = np.load(S / "runs" / "ta_memory_facts_posthoc.npz")
15: C, M, A, clocks, seeds = z["correct"], z["memory"], z["acc_stored"], list(z["clocks"]), list(z["seeds"])
16: for ci in range(len(clocks)):
17:     for si in range(len(seeds)):
18:         assert (C[ci, si][M[si]] >= 0).all(), f"missing: {clocks[ci]} {seeds[si]}"
19: v = np.isfinite(z["acc_cpu"][:, :, 0])
20: for ci, si in zip(*v.nonzero()):
21:     d = np.round(np.abs(z["acc_cpu"][ci, si] - A[ci, si]) * 512).astype(int)
22:     print(f"validation run {clocks[ci]} s{seeds[si]}: all 5120 facts recomputed on the CPU; "
23:           f"|recomputed - stored| correct facts per task: {d.tolist()}")
24: mem_t = np.zeros((len(clocks), len(seeds), 9)); oth_t = mem_t.copy()
25: for ci in range(len(clocks)):
26:     for si in range(len(seeds)):
27:         for t in range(9):
28:             m = M[si, t * 512:(t + 1) * 512]; c = C[ci, si, t * 512:(t + 1) * 512]
29:             k = int((c[m] == 1).sum()); n = int(m.sum())
30:             mem_t[ci, si, t] = k / n
31:             oth_t[ci, si, t] = (round(A[ci, si, t] * 512) - k) / (512 - n)
32: mem, oth = mem_t.mean(2), oth_t.mean(2)
33: out = {"seeds": [int(s) for s in seeds], "per_clock": {}, "tau_minus": {}}
34: print(f"\nmemory: {int(M[0, :4608].sum() // 9)} facts per task, tasks 0-8; seeds {[int(s) for s in seeds]}")
35: for ci, c in enumerate(clocks):
36:     out["per_clock"][c] = {"memory_recall": float(mem[ci].mean()), "other_recall": float(oth[ci].mean()),
37:                            "memory_recall_by_task": [round(float(x), 4) for x in mem_t[ci].mean(0)]}
38:     print(f"  {c:6s} final recall: memory facts {mem[ci].mean():.4f}, other facts of tasks 0-8 {oth[ci].mean():.4f}; "
39:           f"memory recall by task 0-8: {[round(float(x), 3) for x in mem_t[ci].mean(0)]}")
40: for ci, c in enumerate(clocks[1:], 1):
41:     d = mem[0] - mem[ci]; n = len(d)
42:     h = stats.t.ppf(0.975, n - 1) * d.std(ddof=1) / np.sqrt(n)
43:     out["tau_minus"][c] = {"mean": float(d.mean()), "ci95": [float(d.mean() - h), float(d.mean() + h)]}
44:     print(f"tau - {c}: memory recall {d.mean():+.4f}, paired 95% CI [{d.mean() - h:+.4f}; {d.mean() + h:+.4f}]")
45: (S / "runs" / "ta_memory_posthoc.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\tb_part1_g2.py
1: # T-B, part 1 (Stage 0a, blind to T1): G2 for D3 within each condition, as fixed in preinregistrare_TA_TB_2026-11-06.md.
2: # Run from Stadiul0 after the night: python -I tb_part1_g2.py
3: # Builds triplets.parquet for each T-B run (p2.triplets.build; prints nothing about T1), then drops F_A, F_B, dAcc_A,
4: # F_A_clip, F_B_clip before any analysis. Writes the verdict to runs/tb_part1_verdict.json (read by part 2).
5: import json, sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
6: from pathlib import Path
7: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
8: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
9: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
10: from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
11: from scipy.stats import spearmanr
12: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent; sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
13: from p2.triplets import build
14: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
15: R = HERE / "runs"
16: COND = {"F6_z0.0": "tb_F6_n00", "F6_z0.1": "tb_F6_n01", "F6_z0.3": "tb_F6_n03", "F6_z0.5": "tb_F6_n05", "F1": "tb_F1"}
17: FORBID = {"F_A", "F_B", "dAcc_A", "F_A_clip", "F_B_clip"}
18: xcov4 = ["x_LB0", "x_AB0", "x_prior_LB", "x_overlap", "x_N0", "x_tenure", "x_prior_exposures", "x_K_active",
19:          "x_theta_norm0", "x_sqrt_trv0", "x_LR0"]
20: dN4 = ["C1_a", "C1_b", "C1_c", "C1_d"]
21: def r2w(y, p, w):
22:     m = (w * y).sum() / w.sum(); return 1 - (w * (y - p) ** 2).sum() / (w * (y - m) ** 2).sum()
23: def ols():
24:     return make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LinearRegression())
25: def gbdt():
26:     return HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20, random_state=0)
27: 
28: def load(tag):
29:     out = []
30:     for rd in sorted(p for p in (R / tag).iterdir() if (p / "meta.json").exists()):
31:         f = rd / "triplets.parquet"
32:         if not f.exists():
33:             df, _ = build(rd); df.to_parquet(f)
34:         d = pd.read_parquet(f)
35:         d = d.drop(columns=[c for c in FORBID if c in d])
36:         d = d[d.incl & (d.dN >= 64) & (d.dN < 1280)].copy(); d["run"] = rd.name; out.append(d)
37:     D = pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True)
38:     D["C1_a"] = D.dN; D["C1_b"] = np.log(D.dN + 1); D["C1_c"] = np.log(D.dN + 10); D["C1_d"] = np.log(D.dN + 100)
39:     return D
40: 
41: models = {"(i) dN + C10c": lambda D: dN4 + ["rate_C10c"],
42:           "(ii) dN + C10c + C10p + C10h": lambda D: dN4 + ["rate_C10c", "rate_C10p", "rate_C10h"],
43:           "(iii) GBDT pe B+": lambda D: xcov4 + dN4 + ["C0"] + [c for c in D.columns if c.startswith("rate_")
44:                                                              and not c.startswith(("rate_ce_", "rate_Disp_"))]}
45: res, per_seed = {}, {}
46: for cond, tag in COND.items():
47:     D = load(tag)
48:     assert not (set(D.columns) & FORBID)
49:     for clock in ["D3", "D1_rel"]:
50:         y = np.nan_to_num(D[f"rate_ce_{clock}"].to_numpy())
51:         for nm, colf in models.items():
52:             cols = colf(D); D[cols] = D[cols].fillna(0.0); rr = []
53:             for test in sorted(D.run.unique()):
54:                 tr, te = (D.run != test).to_numpy(), (D.run == test).to_numpy()
55:                 mdl = gbdt() if nm.startswith("(iii)") else ols()
56:                 rr.append(r2w(y[te], mdl.fit(D.loc[tr, cols], y[tr]).predict(D.loc[te, cols]), D.w.to_numpy()[te]))
57:             res[(cond, clock, nm)] = (float(np.mean(rr)), float(np.min(rr)), float(np.max(rr)))
58:             per_seed[(cond, clock, nm)] = rr
59:     print(f"{cond}: {D.run.nunique()} rulări, {len(D)} tripleți")
60: print("\nR² în afara sămânței (o sămânță lăsată deoparte, în interiorul condiției): medie / min / max")
61: for k, v in res.items():
62:     print(f"  {k[0]:8s} {k[1]:7s} {k[2]:32s} {v[0]:.3f} / {v[1]:.3f} / {v[2]:.3f}")
63: k3 = ("F6_z0.3", "D3", "(iii) GBDT pe B+")
64: n_below = int(sum(r < 0.90 for r in per_seed[k3]))
65: g2_pass = n_below >= 6
66: u = {z: 1 - res[(f"F6_z{z}", "D3", "(iii) GBDT pe B+")][0] for z in ["0.0", "0.1", "0.3", "0.5"]}
67: zs = [0.0, 0.1, 0.3, 0.5]; us = [u["0.0"], u["0.1"], u["0.3"], u["0.5"]]
68: mono = all(us[i] < us[i + 1] for i in range(3)); rho = float(spearmanr(zs, us).correlation)
69: dose_pass = mono and u["0.3"] >= 2 * u["0.0"]
70: verdict = {"G2_F6_pass": g2_pass, "seeds_below_0.90_at_z0.3": n_below, "per_seed_R2_GBDT_z0.3": per_seed[k3],
71:            "u": u, "spearman": rho, "monotone": mono, "dose_response_pass": dose_pass,
72:            "part2_allowed": g2_pass}
73: (R / "tb_part1_verdict.json").write_text(json.dumps(verdict, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
74: print(f"\nG2-F6 (z = 0,3, GBDT pe B+ < 0,90 în ≥ 6/8 semințe): {'TRECE' if g2_pass else 'PICĂ'} ({n_below}/8)")
75: print(f"Doza–răspuns: u = {dict((k, round(v, 3)) for k, v in u.items())}; monoton {mono}; Spearman {rho:.2f}; "
76:       f"u(0,3) ≥ 2·u(0): {u['0.3'] >= 2 * u['0.0']} -> {'TRECE' if dose_pass else 'PICĂ'}")
77: print("Partea 2 (H1):", "PERMISĂ" if g2_pass else "NU SE RULEAZĂ (D3 se închide ca indice experiențial în acest cadru)")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\tb_part2_h1.py
1: # T-B, part 2 (Stage 0b, automatic): H1 for D3 in F6, as fixed in preinregistrare_TA_TB_2026-11-06.md.
2: # Runs ONLY if runs/tb_part1_verdict.json says part2_allowed. Run from Stadiul0: python -I tb_part2_h1.py
3: # Per run: 5-fold cross-validation grouped by task; baseline GBDT on B+ (telemetry rates without C6, X_cov, dN terms,
4: # pause ticks, C10c, C10p, C10h); augmented = B+ plus rate_ce_D3 and ce_D3. Out-of-fold predictions pooled per run,
5: # R² weighted by the triplet weights w (as every G2 analysis); models fitted unweighted. dR² = R²(aug) - R²(base).
6: import json, sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
7: from pathlib import Path
8: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
9: from sklearn.model_selection import GroupKFold
10: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
11: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
12: R = HERE / "runs"
13: v = json.loads((R / "tb_part1_verdict.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
14: if not v["part2_allowed"]:
15:     raise SystemExit("Part 1 did not pass: H1 is not identifiable, part 2 is not run (preregistration).")
16: xcov4 = ["x_LB0", "x_AB0", "x_prior_LB", "x_overlap", "x_N0", "x_tenure", "x_prior_exposures", "x_K_active",
17:          "x_theta_norm0", "x_sqrt_trv0", "x_LR0"]
18: dN4 = ["C1_a", "C1_b", "C1_c", "C1_d"]
19: def r2w(y, p, w):
