20:     m = (w * y).sum() / w.sum(); return 1 - (w * (y - p) ** 2).sum() / (w * (y - m) ** 2).sum()
21: def gbdt():
22:     return HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20, random_state=0)
23: 
24: def run_dr2(tag):
25:     out = []
26:     for rd in sorted(p for p in (R / tag).iterdir() if (p / "triplets.parquet").exists()):
27:         d = pd.read_parquet(rd / "triplets.parquet")
28:         d = d[d.incl & (d.dN >= 64) & (d.dN < 1280)].reset_index(drop=True)
29:         d["C1_a"] = d.dN; d["C1_b"] = np.log(d.dN + 1); d["C1_c"] = np.log(d.dN + 10); d["C1_d"] = np.log(d.dN + 100)
30:         base = xcov4 + dN4 + ["C0"] + [c for c in d.columns if c.startswith("rate_") and not c.startswith(("rate_ce_", "rate_Disp_"))]
31:         aug = base + ["rate_ce_D3", "ce_D3"]
32:         d[aug] = d[aug].fillna(0.0)
33:         row = {"run": rd.name, "n": len(d), "tasks": d.task.nunique()}
34:         for tgt in ["F_A", "dAcc_A"]:
35:             y = d[tgt].to_numpy(); pb, pa = np.zeros(len(d)), np.zeros(len(d))
36:             for tr, te in GroupKFold(n_splits=5).split(d, y, groups=d.task):
37:                 pb[te] = gbdt().fit(d.loc[tr, base], y[tr]).predict(d.loc[te, base])
38:                 pa[te] = gbdt().fit(d.loc[tr, aug], y[tr]).predict(d.loc[te, aug])
39:             w = d.w.to_numpy()
40:             row[f"R2_base_{tgt}"] = r2w(y, pb, w); row[f"R2_aug_{tgt}"] = r2w(y, pa, w)
41:             row[f"dR2_{tgt}"] = row[f"R2_aug_{tgt}"] - row[f"R2_base_{tgt}"]
42:         out.append(row)
43:     return pd.DataFrame(out)
44: 
45: def verdict(T, label):
46:     dr = T.dR2_F_A.to_numpy(); rng = np.random.default_rng(20261107)
47:     boot = np.array([rng.choice(dr, len(dr), replace=True).mean() for _ in range(10000)])
48:     lo = float(np.percentile(boot, 2.5)); pos = int((dr > 0).sum()); same_sign = bool(T.dR2_dAcc_A.mean() > 0)
49:     ok = dr.mean() >= 0.02 and pos >= 6 and lo > 0 and same_sign
50:     print(f"\n{label}: dR² pe T1 (F_A): medie {dr.mean():.4f}, > 0 în {pos}/{len(dr)} semințe, IC 95% bootstrap inferior {lo:.4f}; "
51:           f"dR² pe T1_acc: medie {T.dR2_dAcc_A.mean():.4f}")
52:     print(f"  H1 {label}: {'SUSȚINUT' if ok else 'NESUSȚINUT (D3 se închide)'}")
53:     return ok, lo
54: 
55: T = run_dr2("tb_F6_n03"); print(T.round(4).to_string(index=False))
56: ok, lo = verdict(T, "F6, zgomot 0,3 (primar)")
57: for tag, lab in [("tb_F6_n01", "F6, zgomot 0,1 (secundar)"), ("tb_F6_n05", "F6, zgomot 0,5 (secundar)")]:
58:     verdict(run_dr2(tag), lab)
59: (R / "tb_part2_verdict.json").write_text(json.dumps({"H1_F6_supported": bool(ok), "boot_lo": lo,
60:                                                      "per_run": T.to_dict(orient="records")}, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\td_audit.py
1: # T-D (colloquium 6 Nov 2026): offline audit of the parameter-clock reconstruction. Criteria fixed before: td_criterii.md
2: # Run from Stadiul0: python -I td_audit.py. v2 (6 Nov, 23:0x): parts (2)-(3) use the scaled OLS of tech4_analysis.py
3: # (v1 used unscaled OLS: ill-conditioned with dN ~1e3 and rates ~1e-7; log kept as runs/td_audit_v1_eroare_scalare.log). Blind to T1 (F_A, F_B, dAcc_A are dropped on load).
4: import sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
5: from pathlib import Path
6: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
7: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
8: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
9: from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
10: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
11: R = Path(r"D:\Time\02_Pilot2\Stadiul0\runs")
12: FORBID = {"F_A", "F_B", "dAcc_A", "F_A_clip", "F_B_clip"}
13: CL = ["D1", "D1_rel", "D2", "D3"]
14: def r2w(y, p, w):
15:     m = (w * y).sum() / w.sum(); return 1 - (w * (y - p) ** 2).sum() / (w * (y - m) ** 2).sum()
16: def gbdt():
17:     return HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=200, learning_rate=0.05, random_state=0)
18: def ols():
19:     return make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LinearRegression())
20: xcov = ["x_LB0", "x_AB0", "x_prior_LB", "x_overlap", "x_N0", "x_tenure", "x_prior_exposures", "x_K_active",
21:         "x_theta_norm0", "x_sqrt_trv0"]
22: xcov4 = xcov + ["x_LR0"]                                # tech4 has L_R(t0) in X_cov, as tech4_analysis.py
23: 
24: # ---------- (1) R1 data without the circular C6 features
25: runs = {"tech3_F2": R / "tech3_lr3e-4/F2_s9004", "tech3_F3": R / "tech3_lr3e-4/F3_s9004",
26:         "tech2_F1": R / "tech2_lr3e-4/F1_s9002", "tech2_F2": R / "tech2_lr3e-4/F2_s9002", "tech2_F3": R / "tech2_lr3e-4/F3_s9002"}
27: dfs = []
28: for name, rd in runs.items():
29:     d = pd.read_parquet(rd / "triplets.parquet")
30:     d = d[d["incl_nocap"] if "incl_nocap" in d else d["incl"]]
31:     d = d[(d.x_rule == 0) & (d.dN < 1280) & (d.dN >= 64)].copy()
32:     E = d.ep.nunique(); d["w"] = 1.0 / (E * d.groupby("ep").ep.transform("size")); d["run"] = name
33:     dfs.append(d.drop(columns=[c for c in FORBID if c in d]))
34: df = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)
35: df["C1_a"] = df.dN; df["C1_b"] = np.log(df.dN + 1); df["C1_c"] = np.log(df.dN + 10); df["C1_d"] = np.log(df.dN + 100)
36: c6 = [c for c in df.columns if c.startswith("C6_")]
37: base = xcov + ["C0", "C1_a", "C1_b", "C1_c", "C1_d", "C2", "C3", "C4", "C5", "C7a", "C5a", "C5b_g", "C5b_gt", "C5b_L",
38:                "C5c_sq", "C5c_work", "C5d", "C5e", "C5f", "C2b", "C6b_theta", "C6b_v", "C10", "C10b"] + \
39:        [f"rate_{z}" for z in ["C2", "C2b", "C3", "C4", "C5", "C5a", "C5b_g", "C5b_gt", "C5b_L", "C5c_sq", "C5c_work",
40:                               "C5d", "C5e", "C5f", "C10", "C10b"]]
41: sets = {"B_strong complet": base + c6, "B_strong fără C6": base}
42: for s in sets.values():
43:     df[s] = df[s].fillna(0.0)
44: print(f"(1) R1 (tech2/tech3): {len(df)} tripleți; C6 = {len(c6)} trăsături; test pe fiecare rulare tech3, antrenare pe celelalte 4")
45: out = []
46: for D in CL:
47:     y = np.nan_to_num(df[f"rate_ce_{D}"].to_numpy(), nan=0.0)
48:     for test in ["tech3_F2", "tech3_F3"]:
49:         tr, te = (df.run != test).to_numpy(), (df.run == test).to_numpy(); w = df.w.to_numpy()[te]
50:         row = {"D": D, "test": test}
51:         for nm, cols in sets.items():
52:             row[f"OLS, {nm}"] = r2w(y[te], ols().fit(df.loc[tr, cols], y[tr]).predict(df.loc[te, cols]), w)
53:             row[f"GBDT, {nm}"] = r2w(y[te], gbdt().fit(df.loc[tr, cols], y[tr]).predict(df.loc[te, cols]), w)
54:         out.append(row)
55: print(pd.DataFrame(out).round(3).to_string(index=False))
56: 
57: # ---------- (2)-(3) tech4: leave one mechanism out; dN bands
58: T4 = R / "tech4"; D4 = []
59: for f in ["F1", "F2", "F3", "F4", "F5", "F6"]:
60:     d = pd.read_parquet(T4 / f"{f}_s9005" / "triplets.parquet")
61:     d = d[d.incl].drop(columns=[c for c in FORBID if c in d]).copy(); d["run"] = f; D4.append(d)
62: D4 = pd.concat(D4, ignore_index=True)
63: D4 = D4[(D4.dN >= 64) & (D4.dN < 1280)].copy()
64: D4["C1_a"] = D4.dN; D4["C1_b"] = np.log(D4.dN + 1); D4["C1_c"] = np.log(D4.dN + 10); D4["C1_d"] = np.log(D4.dN + 100)

67: full = xcov4 + dN4 + ["C0"] + rates
68: D4[full] = D4[full].fillna(0.0)
69: ladder = {"dN + C5d (Ση‖m̂‖)": dN4 + ["rate_C5d"], "dN + C10c": dN4 + ["rate_C10c"], "GBDT tot": full}
70: excl = {"F1": [], "F2": ["F5"], "F3": ["F5"], "F4": ["F5"], "F6": []}
71: print("\n(2) tech4, mecanismul lăsat deoparte (pentru F2, F3, F4 antrenarea exclude și F5); R² ponderat")
72: out = []
73: for Dn in CL:
74:     y = np.nan_to_num(D4[f"rate_ce_{Dn}"].to_numpy())
75:     for test, ex in excl.items():
76:         te = (D4.run == test).to_numpy(); tr = (~D4.run.isin([test] + ex)).to_numpy(); w = D4.w.to_numpy()[te]
77:         row = {"D": Dn, "test": test, "exclus": "+".join(ex) or "-"}
78:         for nm, cols in ladder.items():
79:             mdl = gbdt() if nm.startswith("GBDT") else ols()
80:             row[nm] = r2w(y[te], mdl.fit(D4.loc[tr, cols], y[tr]).predict(D4.loc[te, cols]), w)
81:         out.append(row)
82: print(pd.DataFrame(out).round(3).to_string(index=False))
83: 
84: print("\n(3) tech4, o familie lăsată deoparte, dN + C5d: R² și eroarea relativă medie pe benzi de dN")
85: bands = [(64, 128), (128, 256), (256, 640), (640, 1280)]
86: out = []
87: for Dn in ["D1", "D2", "D3"]:
88:     y = np.nan_to_num(D4[f"rate_ce_{Dn}"].to_numpy()); pred = np.zeros_like(y)
89:     for test in D4.run.unique():
90:         te = (D4.run == test).to_numpy()
91:         pred[te] = ols().fit(D4.loc[~te, ladder["dN + C5d (Ση‖m̂‖)"]], y[~te]).predict(D4.loc[te, ladder["dN + C5d (Ση‖m̂‖)"]])
92:     for lo, hi in bands:
93:         m = ((D4.dN >= lo) & (D4.dN < hi)).to_numpy(); w = D4.w.to_numpy()[m]
94:         out.append(dict(D=Dn, banda=f"{lo}-{hi - 1}", n=int(m.sum()), R2=r2w(y[m], pred[m], w),
95:                         eroare_rel=float((w * np.abs(y[m] - pred[m])).sum() / (w * np.abs(y[m])).sum())))
96: print(pd.DataFrame(out).round(3).to_string(index=False))
97: 
98: # ---------- (4) LM arm: transfer across configurations (tau rate, 8-step windows, as lm_forever_g2.py)
99: def lm_windows(root, W=8):
100:     rows = []
101:     for rd in sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if (p / "meta.json").exists()):
102:         z = np.load(rd / "steps.npz"); S = pd.DataFrame(z["data"], columns=list(z["cols"]))
103:         S["eta_m"] = S.lr * S.m_hat_norm
104:         for (task, ph), g in S[S.counted == 1].groupby(["task", "phase"]):
105:             g = g.sort_values("gstep")
106:             for j in range(len(g) // W):
107:                 w = g.iloc[j * W:(j + 1) * W]
108:                 rows.append(dict(run=rd.name, y=w.delta.mean(), S_eta=w.lr.sum(), S_eta_m=w.eta_m.sum(), lr=w.lr.mean(),
109:                                  g=w.gnorm.mean(), gc=w.gnorm_clipped.mean(), m=w.m_hat_norm.mean(), v=w.sqrt_trv.mean(),
110:                                  loss=w.loss.mean(), sip=w.step_in_phase.mean(), kind=int(w.kind.iloc[0]), task=task,
111:                                  skipped=w.skipped.sum()))
112:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
113: print("\n(4) brațul LM, rata lui τ (ferestre de 8 pași): antrenare pe lmtech1, test pe celelalte configurații")
114: L = {n: lm_windows(R / n) for n in ["lmtech1", "lmtech2", "lmsens_bf16", "lmsens_a32"]}
115: lin = ["S_eta", "S_eta_m"]; fullL = ["lr", "g", "gc", "m", "v", "loss", "sip", "kind", "task", "skipped"]
116: A = L["lmtech1"]
117: m_lin = LinearRegression().fit(A[lin], A.y); m_gb = HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05,
118:           max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20, random_state=0).fit(A[fullL], A.y)
119: def r2(y, p):
120:     return 1 - ((y - p) ** 2).sum() / ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum()
121: out = []
122: for n in ["lmtech2", "lmsens_bf16", "lmsens_a32"]:
123:     B = L[n]
124:     out.append(dict(test=n, ferestre=len(B), **{"OLS Ση + Ση‖m̂‖": r2(B.y.to_numpy(), m_lin.predict(B[lin])),
125:                                                  "GBDT telemetrie": r2(B.y.to_numpy(), m_gb.predict(B[fullL]))}))
126: print(pd.DataFrame(out).round(3).to_string(index=False))
127: rr = []
128: for test in A.run.unique():
129:     tr, te = A.run != test, A.run == test
130:     rr.append(r2(A.y[te].to_numpy(), LinearRegression().fit(A.loc[tr, lin], A.y[tr]).predict(A.loc[te, lin])))
131: print("lmtech1, o sămânță lăsată deoparte, OLS Ση + Ση‖m̂‖ pe semințe:", np.round(rr, 3))

FILE repository\Stadiul0\tech3_criteria.py
1: """Criteria of the tech3 round (Stadiul0_note_lucru.md, section 5c), fixed before the runs. No c_e quantity is read.
2: 
3:     python tech3_criteria.py runs/tech3_lr2e-4 runs/tech3_lr3e-4
4: """
5: import sys
6: import numpy as np
7: import pandas as pd
8: from pathlib import Path
9: 
10: 
11: def reweight(d):
12:     d = d.copy(); E = d.ep.nunique(); M = d.groupby("ep").ep.transform("size"); d["w"] = 1.0 / (E * M); return d
13: 
14: 
15: def wavg(x, w):
16:     return float((w * x).sum() / w.sum())
17: 
18: 
19: for tag in map(Path, sys.argv[1:]):
20:     runs = []
21:     for rd in sorted(p for p in tag.iterdir() if (p / "triplets.parquet").exists()):
22:         df = pd.read_parquet(rd / "triplets.parquet", columns=["ep", "dN", "F_A", "F_B", "dAcc_A", "incl", "family"])
23:         df = df[df.incl]
24:         runs.append(reweight(df))
25:     pooled = pd.concat(runs, ignore_index=True)
26:     dNstar = None
27:     for lo in range(256, 20480, 256):
28:         b = pooled[(pooled.dN >= lo) & (pooled.dN < lo + 256)]
29:         if len(b) >= 10 and wavg(b.dAcc_A.values, b.w.values) >= 0.90:
30:             dNstar = lo; break
31:     print(f"== {tag.name}: dN* = {dNstar}")
32:     tot = 0
33:     for d in runs:
34:         n_un = int((d.dN < dNstar).sum()) if dNstar else len(d); tot += n_un
35:         print(f"   {d.family.iloc[0]}: episoade valide {d.ep.nunique()}, tripleti {len(d)}, sub dN* {n_un}")
36:     print(f"   total tripleti sub dN*: {tot}")
37:     bins = [64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 1e9]
38:     pooled["b"] = pd.cut(pooled.dN, bins, right=False)
39:     print(pooled.groupby("b", observed=True).apply(lambda g: pd.Series({"n": len(g), "F_A": wavg(g.F_A.values, g.w.values),
40:           "dAcc": wavg(g.dAcc_A.values, g.w.values)}), include_groups=False).round(2).T.to_string())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\tech4_analysis.py
1: # tech4 (6 Nov 2026), the descriptive measures fixed in tech4_criterii.md. Run from Stadiul0: python -I tech4_analysis.py
2: # c_e-blind with respect to T1: no model relates c_e to forgetting. (1) forgetting speed by dN, per family, uncapped,
3: # on T1 (F_A), T1-acc (dAcc_A) and F^clip; (2) the G2 ladder (rate of c_e from telemetry), leave one run out, which
4: # never reads F_A, F_B, dAcc_A.
5: import sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
6: from pathlib import Path
7: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
8: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
9: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
10: from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
11: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
12: R = Path(r"D:\Time\02_Pilot2\Stadiul0\runs\tech4")
13: dfs = {f: pd.read_parquet(R / f"{f}_s9005" / "triplets.parquet") for f in ["F1", "F2", "F3", "F4", "F5", "F6"]}
14: 
15: print("(1) Viteza uitării, fără plafon (tripleți cu competență AB0 >= 0,90 și dN >= 64), medii ponderate pe episod")
16: bins = [63, 255, 639, 1279, 2047, 4095, 1e9]; labels = ["64-255", "256-639", "640-1279", "1280-2047", "2048-4095", ">=4096"]
17: rows = []
18: for f, d in dfs.items():
19:     d = d[d.incl_nocap].copy(); d["bin"] = pd.cut(d.dN, bins, labels=labels)
20:     for b, g in d.groupby("bin", observed=True):
21:         w = g.w_nocap
22:         rows.append(dict(fam=f, dN=b, n=len(g), F_A=(w*g.F_A).sum()/w.sum(), dAcc_A=(w*g.dAcc_A).sum()/w.sum(),
23:                          F_A_clip=(w*g.F_A_clip).sum()/w.sum() if "F_A_clip" in g else np.nan))
24: T = pd.DataFrame(rows)
25: for col in ["F_A", "dAcc_A", "F_A_clip"]:
26:     print(f"\n  {col} (nați pentru F, fracție pentru dAcc):")
27:     print(T.pivot(index="fam", columns="dN", values=col).round(3).to_string())
28: print("\n  numărul de tripleți:"); print(T.pivot(index="fam", columns="dN", values="n").fillna(0).astype(int).to_string())
29: 
30: print("\n(2) G2 pe tech4: R² al ratei lui c_e, o familie lăsată deoparte (antrenare pe celelalte 5; dN in [64, 1280))")
31: FORBID = {"F_A", "F_B", "dAcc_A", "F_A_clip", "F_B_clip"}
32: D = []
33: for f, d in dfs.items():
34:     d = d[d.incl].drop(columns=[c for c in FORBID if c in d]).copy(); d["run"] = f; D.append(d)
35: D = pd.concat(D, ignore_index=True)
36: D["C1_a"] = D.dN; D["C1_b"] = np.log(D.dN + 1); D["C1_c"] = np.log(D.dN + 10); D["C1_d"] = np.log(D.dN + 100)
37: dN4 = ["C1_a", "C1_b", "C1_c", "C1_d"]
38: xcov = ["x_LB0", "x_AB0", "x_prior_LB", "x_overlap", "x_N0", "x_tenure", "x_prior_exposures", "x_K_active",
39:         "x_theta_norm0", "x_sqrt_trv0", "x_LR0"]
40: rates = [c for c in D.columns if c.startswith("rate_") and not c.startswith(("rate_ce_", "rate_Disp_"))]
41: full = xcov + dN4 + ["C0"] + rates
42: D[full] = D[full].fillna(0.0)
43: def r2w(y, p, w):
44:     m = (w*y).sum()/w.sum(); return 1 - (w*(y-p)**2).sum()/(w*(y-m)**2).sum()
45: ladder = {"dN": dN4, "dN + C3 (Ση)": dN4 + ["rate_C3"], "dN + C5d (Ση‖m̂‖)": dN4 + ["rate_C5d"],
46:           "dN + C10c": dN4 + ["rate_C10c"]}
47: out = []
48: for Dn in ["D1", "D1_rel", "D2", "D3"]:
49:     y = np.nan_to_num(D[f"rate_ce_{Dn}"].to_numpy())
50:     for test in ["F2", "F3", "F5", "F6"]:
51:         tr, te = (D.run != test).to_numpy(), (D.run == test).to_numpy(); w = D.w.to_numpy()[te]
52:         row = {"D": Dn, "test": test}
53:         for nm, cols in ladder.items():
54:             row[nm] = r2w(y[te], make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LinearRegression()).fit(D.loc[tr, cols], y[tr]).predict(D.loc[te, cols]), w)
55:         g = HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20, random_state=0)
56:         row["GBDT tot"] = r2w(y[te], g.fit(D.loc[tr, full], y[tr]).predict(D.loc[te, full]), w)
57:         out.append(row)
58: print(pd.DataFrame(out).round(3).to_string(index=False))

FILE repository\Stadiul0\verify_hash_lists.py
1: # Checks every entry of the hash lists recorded before the runs (Stadiul0/runs/*_sha256.txt) against the files of this
2: # archive. Written on 9 Nov 2026 for the archive; it reads only. Usage, from the repository root:
3: #     python -I Stadiul0/verify_hash_lists.py
4: # Each entry gets one status:
5: #   ok           the file at its path has the recorded SHA-256
6: #   earlier      the file was changed after the list was written; the recorded version is in runs/earlier_versions/
7: #   authors      a file of the FOREVER authors (no licence, not redistributed): rebuild the working copy with
8: #                Stadiul0/forever_repro/prepare_forever_copy.py, which checks these hashes itself
9: #   external     an absolute path outside the study (the hash list of the authors' downloaded data)
10: #   lost         the recorded version was not kept (explained in runs/earlier_versions/README.md)
11: #   MISMATCH     none of the above: an error
12: # Exit code 0 when no entry is MISMATCH.
13: import hashlib, json, re, sys
14: from pathlib import Path
15: 
16: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
17: S = Path(__file__).resolve().parent  # Stadiul0
18: RUNS = S / "runs"
19: EARLIER = RUNS / "earlier_versions"
20: BASE = {"forever_repro_sha256.txt": S / "forever_repro", "lmtech1_criterii_sha256.txt": S / "lm",
21:         "lmtech2_criterii_sha256.txt": S / "lm", "tc_criterii_sha256.txt": S / "lm"}  # all other lists: Stadiul0
22: AUTHORS = ("src/", "utils/", "templates/")
23: LOST = {
24:     "2d3f61013b9d": ("lm_forever_run.py of the runs lmtech1 and lmtech2", ["lmtech1", "lmtech2"]),
25:     "61d740185b23": ("td_audit.py v1 (unscaled OLS), replaced one minute later; output: runs/td_audit_v1_eroare_scalare.log", []),
26: }
27: 
28: 
29: def sha(p):
30:     return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
31: 
32: 
33: def main():
34:     total, bad = {}, 0
35:     for L in sorted(RUNS.glob("*_sha256.txt")):
36:         base = BASE.get(L.name, S)
37:         counts = {}
38:         for line in L.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
39:             m = re.match(r"([0-9a-f]{64}) \*(.+)$", line.strip())
40:             if not m:
41:                 continue
42:             digest, rel = m.groups()
43:             p = base / rel
44:             if rel.startswith("/") or re.match(r"[A-Za-z]:", rel):
45:                 st = "external"
46:             elif rel.startswith(AUTHORS) and base == S / "forever_repro":
47:                 st = "authors"
48:             elif p.is_file() and sha(p) == digest:
49:                 st = "ok"
50:             elif (EARLIER / digest[:12] / rel).is_file() and sha(EARLIER / digest[:12] / rel) == digest:
51:                 st = "earlier"
52:             elif digest[:12] in LOST:
53:                 what, run_dirs = LOST[digest[:12]]
54:                 metas = [q for d in run_dirs for q in sorted((RUNS / d).glob("*/meta.json"))]
55:                 same = [q for q in metas if json.loads(q.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("script_sha256") == digest]
56:                 st = "lost"
57:                 note = f"{what}" + (f"; SHA-256 also in script_sha256 of {len(same)} of {len(metas)} run meta.json files"
58:                                     if metas else "")
59:                 print(f"  {L.name}: {rel} {digest[:12]} lost: {note}")
60:             else:
61:                 st = "MISMATCH"
62:                 bad += 1
63:                 print(f"  {L.name}: {rel} {digest[:12]} MISMATCH")
64:             counts[st] = counts.get(st, 0) + 1
65:             total[st] = total.get(st, 0) + 1
66:         print(f"{L.name}: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in sorted(counts.items())))
67:     print("\nall lists: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in sorted(total.items())))
68:     print("no mismatch" if not bad else f"{bad} MISMATCH")
69:     sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
70: 
71: 
72: if __name__ == "__main__":
73:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\lm\lm_d3_competitors.py
1: """T-C (colloquium 6 Nov 2026): same-cost competitors of D3 on the LM arm, from the stored probe outputs.
2: 
3:     python -I lm_d3_competitors.py ..\\runs\\lmtech1 ..\\runs\\lmtech2 ..\\runs\\lmsens_bf16 ..\\runs\\lmsens_a32
4: 
5: Criteria fixed before running: tc_criterii.md. Blind to T1: reads probes.npz, steps.npz and meta.json only.
6: D3 intervals exactly as lm_forever_g2.py (consecutive probes of one task, no replay step inside, y = RMS change of
7: the 128 x 31 log-probabilities per counted step). The probe answer words are regenerated from the world seed with
8: the same generator calls as lm_forever_run.make_world (no tokenizer needed: 31 single-token words, as logged).
9: """
10: import json, sys
11: import numpy as np, pandas as pd, torch
12: from pathlib import Path
13: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
14: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
15: 
16: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
17: LETTERS = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ"        # as lm_forever_run.py
18: N_WORDS = 31
19: 
20: 
21: def probe_answers(seed, n_tasks, per_task, n_probe):
22:     g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 10 + 2)
23:     codes, seen = [], set()
24:     while len(codes) < n_tasks * per_task + n_probe:
25:         c = "".join(LETTERS[j] for j in torch.randint(len(LETTERS), (6,), generator=g).tolist())
26:         if c not in seen:
27:             seen.add(c); codes.append(c)
28:     ans = torch.randint(N_WORDS, (len(codes),), generator=g)
29:     return ans[n_tasks * per_task: n_tasks * per_task + n_probe].numpy()
30: 
31: 
32: def rows_of(rd):
33:     meta = json.loads((rd / "meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")); cfg = meta["config"]
34:     z = np.load(rd / "steps.npz"); S = pd.DataFrame(z["data"], columns=list(z["cols"]))
35:     S["eta_m"] = S.lr * S.m_hat_norm; S["eta_g"] = S.lr * S.gnorm_clipped
36:     C = S[S.counted == 1]
37:     P = np.load(rd / "probes.npz"); gs, tk, lp = P["gstep"], P["task"], P["logp"].astype(np.float64)
38:     a = probe_answers(cfg["seed"], cfg["tasks"], cfg["per_task"], cfg["n_probe"])
39:     pr = np.exp(lp); ii = np.arange(lp.shape[1])
40:     tgt_lp = lp[:, ii, a]; tgt_p = pr[:, ii, a]; H = -(pr * lp).sum(-1)
41:     rep = set(S.gstep[S.counted == 0].astype(int))
42:     out = []
43:     for i in range(1, len(gs)):
44:         if tk[i] != tk[i - 1]:
45:             continue
46:         span = C[(C.gstep > gs[i - 1]) & (C.gstep <= gs[i])]
47:         if len(span) == 0 or any(gs[i - 1] < s <= gs[i] for s in rep):
48:             continue
49:         n = len(span); d = lp[i] - lp[i - 1]
50:         out.append(dict(run=rd.name, y=float(np.sqrt((d ** 2).mean(axis=1).mean())) / n,
51:                         c10c=float(np.abs(tgt_lp[i] - tgt_lp[i - 1]).mean()) / n,
52:                         p=float(np.abs(tgt_p[i] - tgt_p[i - 1]).mean()) / n,
53:                         h=float(np.abs(H[i] - H[i - 1]).mean()) / n,
54:                         l1=float(np.abs(d).mean()) / n,
55:                         S_eta_m=span.eta_m.sum(), lr=span.lr.mean(), g=span.gnorm.mean(), gc=span.gnorm_clipped.mean(),
56:                         m=span.m_hat_norm.mean(), v=span.sqrt_trv.mean(), loss=span.loss.mean(),
57:                         sip=span.step_in_phase.mean(), kind=int(span.kind.iloc[0]), task=int(tk[i]),
58:                         skipped=span.skipped.sum()))
59:     return out
60: 
61: 
62: def r2(y, p):
63:     return 1 - ((y - p) ** 2).sum() / ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum()
64: 
65: 
66: full = ["lr", "g", "gc", "m", "v", "loss", "sip", "kind", "task", "skipped"]
67: models = {"(a) C10c_LM": ["c10c"], "(b) C10c_LM + P_LM + H_LM": ["c10c", "p", "h"],
68:           "(c) Ση‖m̂‖ + C10c_LM + P_LM + H_LM": ["S_eta_m", "c10c", "p", "h"],
69:           "L1_LM (aproape-duplicat, nefolosit la verdict)": ["l1"],
70:           "(d) GBDT telemetrie + cei trei": full + ["c10c", "p", "h"]}
71: verdict_b = []
72: for arg in sys.argv[1:]:
73:     root = Path(arg); runs = sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if (p / "meta.json").exists())
74:     D = pd.DataFrame([r for rd in runs for r in rows_of(rd)])
75:     print(f"\n== {root.name}: runs {[r.name for r in runs]}; D3 intervals {len(D)}")
76:     for nm, cols in models.items():
77:         rr = []
78:         for test in D.run.unique():
79:             tr, te = D.run != test, D.run == test
80:             mdl = (HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20,
81:                                                  random_state=0) if nm.startswith("(d)") else LinearRegression())
82:             mdl.fit(D.loc[tr, cols], D.loc[tr, "y"]); rr.append(r2(D.loc[te, "y"].to_numpy(), mdl.predict(D.loc[te, cols])))
83:         print(f"  {nm:48s} R2 mediu {np.mean(rr):.3f}  minim {np.min(rr):.3f}")
84:         if nm.startswith("(b)"):
85:             verdict_b.append(np.mean(rr))
86: v = np.array(verdict_b)
87: print("\nVerdict (criteriul fixat, modelul b):",
88:       "RECONSTRUIT de concurenții de același cost" if (v >= 0.90).all() else
89:       "DISTINCT de concurenții de același cost" if (v < 0.90).all() else "MIXT", "| R2 (b) pe configurații:", np.round(v, 3))

FILE repository\Stadiul0\lm\lm_forever_g2.py
1: """G2 on the LM arm (technical round lmtech1): how much of the FOREVER clock is reconstructible from free telemetry?
2: 
3:     python -I lm_forever_g2.py ..\\runs\\lmtech1      (CPU environment of Stage 0: numpy, pandas, sklearn)
4: 
5: Blind to T1: reads steps.npz, probes.npz and the phase table only; evals.npz (accuracy, forgetting) is never opened.
6: Choices fixed before the round (lmtech1_criterii.md):
7:   * unit: non-overlapping windows of 8 counted steps (calibration and current phases), aligned to the phase start;
8:     a trailing partial window is dropped; y = mean Delta_t in the window (rate of tau, factors; also dW);
9:   * D3 (functional): RMS change of the probe log-probabilities (32 answer words) between consecutive probes of the same
10:     task, intervals that contain a replay step are dropped; y = increment / counted steps;
11:   * competitors, as window sums: S_eta = sum eta (C3, from the scheduler); S_eta_m = sum eta*|m_hat| (C5d);
12:     S_eta_g = sum eta*|g_clipped| (C5b); mean-field null S_eta_m*sqrt(d)/sqrt(Tr v_hat) (C5d/sqrt(v_bar));
13:     full telemetry (GBDT): eta, |g|, |g_clipped|, |m_hat|, sqrt(Tr v_hat), loss, step in phase, phase kind, task index,
14:     skipped steps;
15:   * out-of-run R^2: leave one seed out, every window weighted equally; OLS with intercept for single scalars.
16: """
17: import sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
18: from pathlib import Path
19: from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingRegressor
20: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
21: 
22: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
23: root = Path(sys.argv[1]); W = 8
24: runs = sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if (p/"meta.json").exists())
25: win_rows, d3_rows, phase_rows = [], [], []
26: for rd in runs:
27:     z = np.load(rd/"steps.npz"); cols = list(z["cols"]); S = pd.DataFrame(z["data"], columns=cols)
28:     S["eta_m"] = S.lr * S.m_hat_norm; S["eta_g"] = S.lr * S.gnorm_clipped
29:     C = S[S.counted == 1].copy()
30:     for (task, ph), g in C.groupby(["task", "phase"]):
31:         g = g.sort_values("gstep"); k = len(g) // W
32:         phase_rows.append(dict(run=rd.name, task=task, phase=ph, kind=int(g.kind.iloc[0]), n=len(g),
33:                                tau=g.delta.sum(), S_eta=g.lr.sum(), S_eta_m=g.eta_m.sum()))
34:         for j in range(k):
35:             w = g.iloc[j*W:(j+1)*W]
36:             win_rows.append(dict(run=rd.name, task=task, phase=ph, kind=int(w.kind.iloc[0]), win=j,
37:                                  y=w.delta.mean(), y_dW=w.delta_dW.mean(), S_eta=w.lr.sum(), S_eta_m=w.eta_m.sum(),
38:                                  S_eta_g=w.eta_g.sum(), mf=(w.eta_m * np.sqrt(1146880) / w.sqrt_trv.clip(lower=1e-12)).sum(),
39:                                  lr=w.lr.mean(), g=w.gnorm.mean(), gc=w.gnorm_clipped.mean(), m=w.m_hat_norm.mean(),
40:                                  v=w.sqrt_trv.mean(), loss=w.loss.mean(), sip=w.step_in_phase.mean(), skipped=w.skipped.sum()))
41:     P = np.load(rd/"probes.npz"); gs, tk, lp = P["gstep"], P["task"], P["logp"]
42:     rep = set(S.gstep[S.counted == 0].astype(int))
43:     for i in range(1, len(gs)):
44:         if tk[i] != tk[i-1]:
45:             continue
46:         span = C[(C.gstep > gs[i-1]) & (C.gstep <= gs[i])]
47:         if len(span) == 0 or any(gs[i-1] < s <= gs[i] for s in rep):
48:             continue
49:         inc = float(np.sqrt(((lp[i] - lp[i-1]) ** 2).mean(axis=1).mean()))
50:         d3_rows.append(dict(run=rd.name, task=int(tk[i]), y=inc/len(span), S_eta=span.lr.sum(), S_eta_m=span.eta_m.sum(),
51:                             S_eta_g=span.eta_g.sum(), tau=span.delta.sum(), lr=span.lr.mean(), g=span.gnorm.mean(),
52:                             gc=span.gnorm_clipped.mean(), m=span.m_hat_norm.mean(), v=span.sqrt_trv.mean(),
53:                             loss=span.loss.mean(), sip=span.step_in_phase.mean(), kind=int(span.kind.iloc[0]),
54:                             skipped=span.skipped.sum(), mf=(span.eta_m*np.sqrt(1146880)/span.sqrt_trv.clip(lower=1e-12)).sum()))
55: Wd, D3, Ph = pd.DataFrame(win_rows), pd.DataFrame(d3_rows), pd.DataFrame(phase_rows)
56: print(f"runs: {[r.name for r in runs]}; windows: {len(Wd)}; D3 intervals: {len(D3)}; phases: {len(Ph)}")
57: 
58: def r2(y, p):
59:     return 1 - ((y - p) ** 2).sum() / ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum()
60: full = ["lr", "g", "gc", "m", "v", "loss", "sip", "kind", "task", "skipped"]
61: ladder = {"Ση (C3)": ["S_eta"], "Ση‖g̃‖ (C5b)": ["S_eta_g"], "Ση‖m̂‖ (C5d)": ["S_eta_m"],
62:           "câmp mediu C5d/√v̄": ["mf"], "Ση + Ση‖m̂‖": ["S_eta", "S_eta_m"]}
63: def ladder_table(df, ycol):
64:     out = {}
65:     for nm, cols in list(ladder.items()) + [("GBDT telemetrie completă", full)]:
66:         r = []
67:         for test in df.run.unique():
68:             tr, te = df.run != test, df.run == test
69:             mdl = (HistGradientBoostingRegressor(max_iter=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_leaf_nodes=31, min_samples_leaf=20,
70:                                                  random_state=0) if nm.startswith("GBDT") else LinearRegression())
71:             mdl.fit(df.loc[tr, cols], df.loc[tr, ycol]); r.append(r2(df.loc[te, ycol].to_numpy(), mdl.predict(df.loc[te, cols])))
72:         out[nm] = (np.mean(r), np.min(r))
73:     return pd.DataFrame(out, index=["R2 mediu", "R2 minim"]).T
74: 
75: if Wd.run.nunique() >= 2:
76:     print("\nRata lui τ pe ferestre de 8 pași (factori), R² în afara rulării:")
77:     print(ladder_table(Wd, "y").round(3).to_string())
78:     print("\nRata lui τ pe ΔW = 2·B·A (secundar):")
79:     print(ladder_table(Wd, "y_dW").round(3).to_string())
80:     if len(D3) > 50:
81:         print("\nD3 (schimbarea funcțională pe sonde), R² în afara rulării:")
82:         print(ladder_table(D3, "y").round(3).to_string())
83:     # where does the cheapest scalar fail? residual of OLS y ~ S_eta (leave one run out), by window after phase start
84:     Wd["res"] = np.nan
85:     for test in Wd.run.unique():
86:         tr, te = Wd.run != test, Wd.run == test
87:         m = LinearRegression().fit(Wd.loc[tr, ["S_eta"]], Wd.loc[tr, "y"]); Wd.loc[te, "res"] = Wd.loc[te, "y"] - m.predict(Wd.loc[te, ["S_eta"]])
88:     mu = Wd.y.mean()
89:     Wd["fereastra"] = pd.cut(Wd.win, [-1, 0, 1, 3, 1e9], labels=["1 (pașii 1-8)", "2", "3-4", ">=5"])
90:     Wd["faza"] = np.where(Wd.kind == 0, "calibrare", "curentă")
91:     print("\nReziduul lui 'y ~ Ση' (relativ la rata medie), după fereastra din fază și tipul fazei:")
92:     print(Wd.groupby(["faza", "fereastra"], observed=True).agg(n=("y", "size"), eroare_abs=("res", lambda r: r.abs().mean()/mu),
93:                                                              bias=("res", lambda r: r.mean()/mu)).round(3).to_string())
94:     # phase level: how much of tau per phase is the integral of the schedule?
95:     print("\nLa nivel de fază: R² (în afara rulării) al lui τ_fază din Ση_fază și din Ση‖m̂‖_fază:")
96:     for c in ["S_eta", "S_eta_m"]:
97:         r = [r2(Ph.loc[Ph.run == t, "tau"].to_numpy(), LinearRegression().fit(Ph.loc[Ph.run != t, [c]], Ph.loc[Ph.run != t, "tau"]).predict(Ph.loc[Ph.run == t, [c]]))
98:              for t in Ph.run.unique()]
99:         print(f"  {c}: mediu {np.mean(r):.3f}, minim {np.min(r):.3f}")
100:     print(f"\nPași săriți de GradScaler: {int(Wd.skipped.sum())} din {8*len(Wd)} pași în ferestre")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\lm\lm_forever_posthoc.py
1: """lmtech1, analyses ADDED AFTER seeing the pre-fixed G2 output (6 Nov 2026) - exploratory, labelled as such.
2:     python -I lm_forever_posthoc.py ..\\runs\\lmtech1
3: Blind to T1 (evals.npz never opened).
4: (1) Sum eta over APPLIED steps only (GradScaler skips change nothing but still carry an eta in the log).
5: (2) Phase level, with duration controlled: tau_phase from steps alone, from sum eta, from sum eta applied.
6: (3) The FOREVER trigger: within each task (tasks >= 1), the counted step at which tau crosses d * model_day, versus the
7:     step at which a schedule-only clock (cumulative applied eta, normalised by its own calibration value) crosses d.
8: """
9: import sys, numpy as np, pandas as pd
10: from pathlib import Path
11: from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
12: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
13: root = Path(sys.argv[1]); W = 8
14: def r2(y, p): return 1 - ((y - p) ** 2).sum() / ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum()
15: def loro(df, cols, y):
16:     out = []
17:     for t in df.run.unique():
18:         tr, te = df.run != t, df.run == t
19:         out.append(r2(df.loc[te, y].to_numpy(), LinearRegression().fit(df.loc[tr, cols], df.loc[tr, y]).predict(df.loc[te, cols])))
20:     return np.mean(out), np.min(out)
21: win, ph, trig = [], [], []
22: for rd in sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if (p/"meta.json").exists()):
23:     z = np.load(rd/"steps.npz"); S = pd.DataFrame(z["data"], columns=list(z["cols"]))
24:     S["eta_app"] = S.lr * (1 - S.skipped); S["eta_m"] = S.lr * S.m_hat_norm
25:     C = S[S.counted == 1]
26:     for (task, p), g in C.groupby(["task", "phase"]):
27:         g = g.sort_values("gstep")
28:         ph.append(dict(run=rd.name, kind=int(g.kind.iloc[0]), n=len(g), tau=g.delta.sum(), S_eta=g.lr.sum(), S_eta_app=g.eta_app.sum()))
29:         for j in range(len(g) // W):
30:             w = g.iloc[j*W:(j+1)*W]
31:             win.append(dict(run=rd.name, y=w.delta.mean(), S_eta=w.lr.sum(), S_eta_app=w.eta_app.sum(), S_eta_m=w.eta_m.sum(),
32:                             skipped=w.skipped.sum()))
33:     for task, g in C.groupby("task"):
34:         if task == 0:
35:             continue
36:         g = g.sort_values("gstep"); tau = g.delta.cumsum().to_numpy(); eta = g.eta_app.cumsum().to_numpy()
37:         calib = g.kind.to_numpy() == 0; day_tau, day_eta = tau[calib][-1], eta[calib][-1]
38:         for d in (2, 4, 7, 15, 30, 60, 90, 120):
39:             i_tau = np.argmax(tau >= d * day_tau) if (tau >= d * day_tau).any() else None
40:             i_eta = np.argmax(eta >= d * day_eta) if (eta >= d * day_eta).any() else None
41:             trig.append(dict(run=rd.name, task=task, d=d, step_tau=i_tau, step_eta=i_eta, n=len(g)))
42: Wd, Ph, Tr = pd.DataFrame(win), pd.DataFrame(ph), pd.DataFrame(trig)
43: print("(1) Ferestre de 8 pași, R² în afara rulării (mediu, minim):")
44: for nm, cols in [("Ση (toți pașii)", ["S_eta"]), ("Ση aplicat (fără pașii săriți)", ["S_eta_app"]),
45:                  ("Ση aplicat + nr. pași săriți", ["S_eta_app", "skipped"]), ("Ση‖m̂‖", ["S_eta_m"])]:
46:     m, lo = loro(Wd, cols, "y"); print(f"  {nm:32s} {m:.3f}  {lo:.3f}")
47: print(f"  ferestre cu cel puțin un pas sărit: {100*(Wd.skipped > 0).mean():.1f}%")
48: print("\n(2) La nivel de fază (fazele curente și de calibrare), R² în afara rulării:")
49: for nm, cols in [("pași (n)", ["n"]), ("Ση", ["S_eta"]), ("Ση aplicat", ["S_eta_app"]), ("n + Ση aplicat", ["n", "S_eta_app"])]:
50:     m, lo = loro(Ph, cols, "tau"); print(f"  {nm:16s} {m:.3f}  {lo:.3f}")
51: Ph["tau_per_step"] = Ph.tau / Ph.n; Ph["eta_per_step"] = Ph.S_eta_app / Ph.n
52: m, lo = loro(Ph, ["eta_per_step"], "tau_per_step"); print(f"  rata pe fază (τ/n) din (Ση aplicat/n): {m:.3f}  {lo:.3f}   <- durata scoasă")
53: print("\n(3) Declanșarea repetițiilor (sarcinile 1-9): pasul la care τ atinge d·zi față de pasul ceasului doar din program")
54: T = Tr.dropna(subset=["step_tau"]).copy()
55: T["ambele"] = T.step_eta.notna()
56: T["dif"] = np.where(T.ambele, T.step_eta.astype(float) - T.step_tau.astype(float), np.nan)
57: g = T.groupby("d").agg(praguri_tau=("step_tau", "size"), atinse_si_de_program=("ambele", "sum"),
58:                        pas_tau_median=("step_tau", "median"), dif_mediana=("dif", "median"),
59:                        dif_abs_medie=("dif", lambda x: np.nanmean(np.abs(x))))
60: print(g.round(1).to_string())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\lm\lm_forever_run.py
1: """Pilot 2, LM arm, technical run: the FOREVER clock in the FOREVER schedule (6 Nov 2026).
2: 
3:     D:\\Time\\_venv_cuda\\Scripts\\python.exe -I lm_forever_run.py --seed 7101 --out ..\\runs\\lmtech1\\s7101 [--tasks 10]
4: 
5: Copies the training schedule of FOREVER (arXiv:2601.03938; code github.com/WoodScene/FOREVER, commit bbeb549,
6: src/finetune_cl_our_qwen.py) on Qwen3-0.6B, with synthetic facts instead of SuperNI:
7:   * LoRA r=8, alpha=16 (scale 2), dropout 0.05, on q_proj and v_proj only; PEFT-style init (A ~ U(+-1/sqrt(in)), B = 0);
8:     base weights fp16, LoRA factors fp32, fp16 autocast with a GradScaler (init 65536), as Trainer(fp16=True).
9:   * Every phase is a new Trainer: AdamW (0.9, 0.999, eps 1e-8, weight decay 0), clip 1.0, lr 3e-4, HF linear schedule
10:     with warmup (first step at lr 0), a fresh optimizer, a fresh scheduler and a fresh GradScaler.
11:   * Per task: tau starts at 0. Calibration phase = first 24 steps (warmup 10, max_steps 24); model_day = tau after it;
12:     thresholds d*model_day, d in {1,2,4,7,15,30,60,90,120}. Current phase: warmup 10, max_steps = remaining task steps,
13:     stopped as soon as tau >= next threshold. Replay phase (tasks >= 1): warmup 5, 2 epochs over the memory buffer,
14:     loss + 0.001*beta_t*sum((theta - theta_star)^2), theta_star = factors at task start, beta_t = clip(mu/mu0, 0.5, 3),
15:     mu = EMA(0.05) of Delta_t, mu0 = tau_calib/24. Replay steps are not counted in tau; the reference point moves after a
16:     replay. A final replay at the end of every task >= 1. Task 0: 60 epochs, no replay; tasks >= 1: 10 epochs.
17: Declared deviations: one batch of 32 instead of 4 x 8 accumulation; loss on the single answer token; the memory buffer is
18: 16 fixed facts per previous task (the meaning of memory_data_ratio=2 is not checkable: utils/load_data.py is missing at
19: that commit); replay steps per epoch = ceil(M/32); the task's batches form one stream across its phases (a new
20: Trainer would restart its dataloader at every phase).
21: 
22: Telemetry per optimizer step (all phases): phase kind, step in phase, lr, loss, grad norm before / after clipping, GradScaler
23: skip, |m_hat|, sqrt(Tr v_hat), Delta_t on the factors (tau increment), Delta_t on dW = scale*B*A (Gram identity, exact),
24: mu, beta. Probe outputs (128 never-trained prompts, log-softmax over the 32 answer words) every 8 counted steps, for a
25: functional distance. Accuracy on the tasks seen so far at every task end goes to evals.npz (not read by the G2 analysis).
26: Deterministic: CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG, torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True), seeded generators. Keeps Windows awake
27: while it runs (SetThreadExecutionState), without changing any power setting.
28: """
29: import os
30: os.environ.setdefault("CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG", ":4096:8")
31: import argparse, ctypes, hashlib, json, math, platform, sys, time
32: import numpy as np
33: import torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F
34: 
35: MODEL_DIR = r"D:\Time\_modele\Qwen_Qwen3-0.6B"
36: MODEL_SHA256 = "f47f71177f32bcd101b7573ec9171e6a57f4f4d31148d38e382306f42996874b"   # modele_provenienta.md
37: WORDS = ["apple", "river", "stone", "cloud", "tiger", "piano", "candle", "forest", "silver", "rocket", "garden",
38:          "mirror", "pepper", "violet", "harbor", "lemon", "castle", "dragon", "falcon", "marble", "orange", "pencil",
39:          "rabbit", "saddle", "tunnel", "velvet", "window", "anchor", "basket", "copper", "desert", "engine"]
40: LETTERS = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ"
41: KINDS = ["calib", "current", "replay", "final_replay"]
42: AMP = torch.float16                      # --precision bf16 (sensitivity, not FOREVER): bf16 weights and autocast, no GradScaler
43: 
44: def keep_awake(on=True):
45:     if platform.system() == "Windows":
46:         ES_CONTINUOUS, ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = 0x80000000, 0x00000001
47:         ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(ES_CONTINUOUS | (ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED if on else 0))
48: 
49: class LoRA(nn.Module):
50:     def __init__(self, base, r, alpha, dropout, gen):
51:         super().__init__()
52:         self.base, self.scale = base, alpha / r
53:         dev = base.weight.device
54:         bound = 1.0 / math.sqrt(base.in_features)
55:         self.A = nn.Parameter((torch.rand(r, base.in_features, generator=gen) * 2 - 1).mul_(bound).to(dev))
56:         self.B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, r, device=dev))
57:         self.drop = nn.Dropout(dropout)
58:     def forward(self, x):
59:         return self.base(x) + F.linear(F.linear(self.drop(x), self.A), self.B) * self.scale
60: 
61: def build_model(seed, args):
62:     from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
63:     tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, local_files_only=True)
64:     model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, local_files_only=True, dtype=AMP,
65:                                                  attn_implementation=args.attn).to("cuda")
66:     for p in model.parameters():
67:         p.requires_grad_(False)
68:     gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 10 + 1)
69:     loras = []
70:     for layer in model.model.layers:
71:         for nm in ["q_proj", "v_proj"]:
72:             m = LoRA(getattr(layer.self_attn, nm), args.r, args.alpha, args.dropout, gen)
73:             setattr(layer.self_attn, nm, m); loras.append(m)
74:     return tok, model, loras
75: 
76: def make_world(tok, seed, n_tasks, per_task, n_probe):
77:     words = [w for w in WORDS if len(tok(" " + w, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids) == 1]
78:     wid = torch.tensor([tok(" " + w, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[0] for w in words])
79:     g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 10 + 2)
80:     codes, seen = [], set()
81:     while len(codes) < n_tasks * per_task + n_probe:
82:         c = "".join(LETTERS[j] for j in torch.randint(len(LETTERS), (6,), generator=g).tolist())
83:         if c not in seen:
84:             seen.add(c); codes.append(c[:3] + "-" + c[3:])
85:     ans = torch.randint(len(words), (len(codes),), generator=g)
86:     ids = [tok(f"code {c} is", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids for c in codes]
87:     L = max(map(len, ids)); pad = tok.pad_token_id if tok.pad_token_id is not None else tok.eos_token_id
88:     x = torch.full((len(ids), L), pad); att = torch.zeros((len(ids), L), dtype=torch.long)
89:     for i, s in enumerate(ids):                      # left padding, as FOREVER (padding_side = "left")
90:         x[i, L - len(s):] = torch.tensor(s); att[i, L - len(s):] = 1
91:     pos = (att.cumsum(1) - 1).clamp(min=0)
92:     tasks = [torch.arange(t * per_task, (t + 1) * per_task) for t in range(n_tasks)]
93:     probe = torch.arange(n_tasks * per_task, n_tasks * per_task + n_probe)
94:     return dict(x=x.cuda(), att=att.cuda(), pos=pos.cuda(), y=wid[ans].cuda(), ans=ans.cuda(), wid=wid.cuda(),
95:                 tasks=tasks, probe=probe, words=words, codes=codes)
96: 
97: def logits_at_answer(model, W, idx):
98:     out = model(input_ids=W["x"][idx], attention_mask=W["att"][idx], position_ids=W["pos"][idx], logits_to_keep=1)
99:     return out.logits[:, -1, :]
100: 
101: class Run:
102:     def __init__(self, args):
103:         self.a = args
104:         torch.manual_seed(args.seed); np.random.seed(args.seed)
105:         self.tok, self.model, self.loras = build_model(args.seed, args)
106:         self.W = make_world(self.tok, args.seed, args.tasks, args.per_task, args.n_probe)
107:         self.params = [p for m in self.loras for p in (m.A, m.B)]
108:         self.data_gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed * 10 + 3)
109:         self.mem_gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed * 10 + 4)
110:         self.rows, self.probe_rows, self.evals, self.phases = [], [], [], []
111:         self.pre_final = []                                   # (task, accuracy before the final replay), diagnostic
112:         self.pre_final_all = []                               # (task, gstep, accuracy on all seen tasks), T-A
113:         self.clk = 0.0                                        # replay-driving clock (T-A); equals tau by default
114:         self.gstep = 0
115:         self.prev = self.flat().clone()                       # reference point for Delta_t
116:         self.prevAB = [(m.A.detach().clone(), m.B.detach().clone()) for m in self.loras]
117:         self.memory = torch.empty(0, dtype=torch.long)
118: 
119:     def flat(self):
120:         return torch.cat([p.detach().reshape(-1) for p in self.params])
121: 
122:     def dW_delta(self):
123:         s2 = []
124:         for m, (A0, B0) in zip(self.loras, self.prevAB):   # |B A - B0 A0|_F^2 = sum((Bc^T Bc) * (Ac Ac^T)), rank 2r
125:             Bc = torch.cat([m.B.detach(), -B0], 1); Ac = torch.cat([m.A.detach(), A0], 0)
126:             s2.append(((Bc.t() @ Bc) * (Ac @ Ac.t())).sum())
127:         return self.loras[0].scale * math.sqrt(max(float(torch.stack(s2).sum()), 0.0))
128: 
129:     def move_reference(self):
130:         self.prev = self.flat().clone()
131:         self.prevAB = [(m.A.detach().clone(), m.B.detach().clone()) for m in self.loras]
132: 
133:     def batches(self, idx, epochs):
134:         for _ in range(epochs):
135:             perm = idx[torch.randperm(len(idx), generator=self.data_gen)]
136:             for i in range(0, len(perm), self.a.batch):
137:                 yield perm[i:i + self.a.batch]
138: 
139:     def probe(self, task, tau):
140:         self.model.eval()
141:         with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=AMP):
142:             lg = logits_at_answer(self.model, self.W, self.W["probe"].cuda()).float()
143:         self.model.train()
144:         lp = torch.log_softmax(lg[:, self.W["wid"]], -1)
145:         self.probe_rows.append((self.gstep, task, tau, lp.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)))
146: 
147:     def evaluate(self, task):
148:         self.model.eval(); acc = []
149:         with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=AMP):
150:             for t in range(task + 1):
151:                 idx = self.W["tasks"][t].cuda(); hit = 0
152:                 for i in range(0, len(idx), 128):
153:                     hit += int((logits_at_answer(self.model, self.W, idx[i:i+128]).argmax(-1) == self.W["y"][idx[i:i+128]]).sum())
154:                 acc.append(hit / len(idx))
155:         self.model.train()
156:         self.evals.append((task, self.gstep, acc))
157:         return acc
158: 
159:     def acc_current(self, task):
160:         """Diagnostic only (--diag_pre_final): accuracy on the current task, logged nowhere else; no RNG, no grad."""
161:         self.model.eval(); idx = self.W["tasks"][task].cuda(); hit = 0
162:         with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=AMP):
163:             for i in range(0, len(idx), 128):
164:                 hit += int((logits_at_answer(self.model, self.W, idx[i:i+128]).argmax(-1) == self.W["y"][idx[i:i+128]]).sum())
165:         self.model.train()
166:         return hit / len(idx)
167: 
168:     def phase(self, kind, task, data, max_steps, warmup, stop_tau=None, beta=None, theta_star=None, counted=True):
169:         a = self.a
170:         opt = torch.optim.AdamW(self.params, lr=a.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.999), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=0.0)
171:         lam = lambda s: s / max(1, warmup) if s < warmup else max(0.0, (max_steps - s) / max(1.0, max_steps - warmup))
172:         sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(opt, lam)
173:         scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda", init_scale=65536.0, enabled=(AMP == torch.float16))
174:         start, n = self.gstep, 0
175:         while n < max_steps:
176:             idx = next(data).cuda(); lr = opt.param_groups[0]["lr"]
177:             with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=AMP):
178:                 loss = F.cross_entropy(logits_at_answer(self.model, self.W, idx).float(), self.W["y"][idx])
179:             total = loss
180:             if theta_star is not None:
181:                 reg = sum(((p - q) ** 2).sum() for p, q in zip(self.params, theta_star))
182:                 total = loss + 0.001 * beta * reg
183:             scaler.scale(total).backward()
184:             scaler.unscale_(opt)
185:             gn = float(torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.params, 1.0))
186:             gn_after = float(torch.sqrt(sum((p.grad.float() ** 2).sum() for p in self.params)))
187:             s0 = scaler.get_scale(); scaler.step(opt); scaler.update(); skipped = scaler.get_scale() < s0
188:             if not skipped:
189:                 sched.step()
190:             opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
191:             st = [opt.state[p] for p in self.params if p in opt.state and "exp_avg" in opt.state[p]]
192:             if st:
193:                 k = float(st[0]["step"])                         # same step count for every parameter
194:                 mv = torch.stack([torch.stack([(s_["exp_avg"] ** 2).sum(), s_["exp_avg_sq"].sum()]) for s_ in st]).sum(0)
195:                 m2, v1 = (mv / torch.tensor([(1 - 0.9 ** k) ** 2, 1 - 0.999 ** k], device=mv.device)).tolist()
196:             else:
197:                 m2 = v1 = 0.0
198:             cur = self.flat(); d = float(torch.linalg.vector_norm(cur - self.prev)); dw = self.dW_delta()
199:             self.prev = cur.clone(); self.prevAB = [(m.A.detach().clone(), m.B.detach().clone()) for m in self.loras]
200:             if counted:
201:                 self.tau += d
202:                 self.clk += self.clock_inc(d, lr, m2, skipped)    # the clock that drives replay (T-A); == tau by default
203:                 if self.mu0 is not None:
204:                     self.mu = (1 - 0.05) * self.mu + 0.05 * d    # replay weighting (beta) stays tau-based in every arm
205:             self.gstep += 1; n += 1
206:             self.rows.append((self.gstep, task, KINDS.index(kind), len(self.phases), n, lr, float(loss.detach()), gn, gn_after,
207:                               int(skipped), math.sqrt(m2), math.sqrt(v1), d, dw, int(counted), self.tau,
208:                               self.mu if self.mu is not None else np.nan, beta if beta is not None else np.nan, self.clk))
209:             if not math.isfinite(float(loss)):
210:                 raise RuntimeError(f"non-finite loss at step {self.gstep}")
211:             if counted:
212:                 self.counted += 1
213:                 if self.counted % a.probe_every == 0:
214:                     self.probe(task, self.tau)
215:                 if stop_tau is not None and self.clk >= stop_tau:
216:                     break
217:         self.phases.append((task, KINDS.index(kind), start, self.gstep, max_steps, warmup))
218:         return n
219: 
220:     def clock_inc(self, d, lr, m2, skipped):
221:         """Increment of the replay-driving clock per counted step (T-A, colloquium 6 Nov 2026). 'tau' reproduces FOREVER."""
222:         c = self.a.clock
223:         if c == "tau":
224:             return d
225:         if skipped:
226:             return 0.0
227:         if c == "eta_m":
228:             return lr * math.sqrt(m2)                      # sum eta * |m_hat|, the one-scalar telemetry clock
229:         if c == "eta":
230:             return lr                                      # cumulative applied learning rate (schedule only)
231:         return 1.0                                         # 'steps': counted steps
232: 
233:     def acc_tasks(self, upto):
234:         """Accuracy on tasks 0..upto; no RNG, no grad (used by evaluate and by the pre-final diagnostic)."""
235:         self.model.eval(); acc = []
236:         with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=AMP):
237:             for t in range(upto + 1):
238:                 idx = self.W["tasks"][t].cuda(); hit = 0
239:                 for i in range(0, len(idx), 128):
240:                     hit += int((logits_at_answer(self.model, self.W, idx[i:i+128]).argmax(-1) == self.W["y"][idx[i:i+128]]).sum())
241:                 acc.append(hit / len(idx))
242:         self.model.train()
243:         return acc
244: 
245:     def replay_phase(self, kind, task, theta_star):
246:         ratio = self.mu / (self.mu0 + 1e-12)
247:         beta = min(3.0, max(0.5, 1.0 + (ratio - 1.0)))
248:         M = len(self.memory); steps = 2 * max(1, math.ceil(M / self.a.batch))
249:         self.phase(kind, task, self.batches(self.memory, 2), steps, 5, beta=beta, theta_star=theta_star, counted=False)
250:         self.move_reference()
251: 
252:     def task(self, t):
253:         a = self.a
254:         epochs = a.first_epochs if t == 0 else a.epochs
255:         idx = self.W["tasks"][t]
256:         total = math.ceil(len(idx) / a.batch * epochs)
257:         data = self.batches(idx, epochs)
258:         self.tau, self.mu, self.mu0, self.counted = 0.0, None, None, 0
259:         self.clk = 0.0
260:         theta_star = [p.detach().clone() for p in self.params]
261:         have_mem = len(self.memory) > 0
262:         self.probe(t, 0.0)                                  # functional reference at task start
263:         done = self.phase("calib", t, data, min(24, total), 10)
264:         model_day = max(self.clk, 1e-8); thr = [d * model_day for d in (1, 2, 4, 7, 15, 30, 60, 90, 120)]; nxt = 0
265:         self.mu0 = self.tau / done; self.mu = self.mu0
266:         while done < total:
267:             if have_mem and nxt < len(thr) and self.clk >= thr[nxt]:
268:                 self.replay_phase("replay", t, theta_star); nxt += 1
269:                 continue
270:             stop = thr[nxt] if (have_mem and nxt < len(thr)) else None
271:             done += self.phase("current", t, data, total - done, 10, stop_tau=stop)
272:         if have_mem:
273:             if a.diag_pre_final:
274:                 self.pre_final.append((t, self.acc_current(t)))
275:             if a.diag_pre_final_all:
276:                 self.pre_final_all.append((t, self.gstep, self.acc_tasks(t)))
277:             self.replay_phase("final_replay", t, theta_star)
278:         acc = self.evaluate(t)
279:         keep = idx[torch.randperm(len(idx), generator=self.mem_gen)[:a.mem_per_task]]
280:         self.memory = torch.cat([self.memory, keep])
281:         return total, model_day, acc
282: 
283: def main():
284:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
285:     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
286:     ap.add_argument("--tasks", type=int, default=10); ap.add_argument("--per_task", type=int, default=512)
287:     ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=10); ap.add_argument("--first_epochs", type=int, default=60)
288:     ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=32); ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=3e-4)
289:     ap.add_argument("--r", type=int, default=8); ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=16.0)
290:     ap.add_argument("--dropout", type=float, default=0.05); ap.add_argument("--mem_per_task", type=int, default=16)
291:     ap.add_argument("--n_probe", type=int, default=128); ap.add_argument("--probe_every", type=int, default=8)
292:     ap.add_argument("--attn", default="sdpa")
293:     ap.add_argument("--precision", default="fp16", choices=["fp16", "bf16"])
294:     ap.add_argument("--diag_pre_final", action="store_true",
295:                     help="diagnostic: current-task accuracy just before the final replay (training unchanged)")
296:     ap.add_argument("--diag_pre_final_all", action="store_true",
297:                     help="T-A: accuracy on all seen tasks just before each final replay, saved to evals_pre.npz (no RNG)")
298:     ap.add_argument("--clock", default="tau", choices=["tau", "eta_m", "eta", "steps"],
299:                     help="T-A: clock that drives the replay thresholds (tau = FOREVER; eta_m = sum eta*|m_hat|; "
300:                          "eta = cumulative applied eta; steps = counted steps). beta stays tau-based.")
301:     a = ap.parse_args()
302:     os.makedirs(a.out, exist_ok=True)
303:     if os.path.exists(os.path.join(a.out, "meta.json")):
304:         print("complete run found, skipping:", a.out); return
305:     global AMP
306:     AMP = torch.float16 if a.precision == "fp16" else torch.bfloat16
307:     torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
308:     torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
309:     torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
310:     keep_awake(True)
311:     t0 = time.time()
312:     run = Run(a)
313:     cfg = vars(a).copy(); cfg.pop("out")
314:     log = open(os.path.join(a.out, "run.log"), "a", encoding="utf-8")
315:     def say(s):
316:         print(s, flush=True); log.write(s + "\n"); log.flush()
317:     say(f"seed {a.seed}; trainable {sum(p.numel() for p in run.params)}; words {len(run.W['words'])}; config {json.dumps(cfg)}")
318:     for t in range(a.tasks):
319:         tt = time.time()
320:         total, day, acc = run.task(t)
321:         nph = sum(1 for p in run.phases if p[0] == t)
322:         say(f"task {t}: {total} steps, model_day {day:.4f}, tau {run.tau:.3f}, phases {nph}, step {run.gstep}, "
323:             f"acc_new {acc[-1]:.3f}, {time.time() - tt:.0f}s, max VRAM {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 2**20:.0f} MiB")
324:     cols = ["gstep", "task", "kind", "phase", "step_in_phase", "lr", "loss", "gnorm", "gnorm_clipped", "skipped",
325:             "m_hat_norm", "sqrt_trv", "delta", "delta_dW", "counted", "tau", "mu", "beta", "clk"]
326:     steps = np.array(run.rows, dtype=np.float64)
327:     if run.pre_final_all:
328:         np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "evals_pre.npz"), task=np.array([e[0] for e in run.pre_final_all]),
329:                             gstep=np.array([e[1] for e in run.pre_final_all]),
330:                             acc=np.array([e[2] + [np.nan] * (a.tasks - len(e[2])) for e in run.pre_final_all]))
331:     np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "steps.npz"), cols=np.array(cols), data=steps,
332:                         phases=np.array(run.phases, dtype=np.int64))
333:     np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "probes.npz"), gstep=np.array([r[0] for r in run.probe_rows]),
334:                         task=np.array([r[1] for r in run.probe_rows]), tau=np.array([r[2] for r in run.probe_rows]),
335:                         logp=np.stack([r[3] for r in run.probe_rows]))
336:     np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "evals.npz"), task=np.array([e[0] for e in run.evals]),
337:                         gstep=np.array([e[1] for e in run.evals]),
338:                         acc=np.array([e[2] + [np.nan] * (a.tasks - len(e[2])) for e in run.evals]))
339:     final = run.flat().cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)
340:     np.save(os.path.join(a.out, "final_factors.npy"), final)
341:     h = lambda f: hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(a.out, f), "rb").read()).hexdigest()
342:     meta = dict(config=cfg, config_sha256=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(cfg, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest(),
343:                 model_dir=MODEL_DIR, model_sha256=MODEL_SHA256, torch=torch.__version__, cuda=torch.version.cuda,
344:                 device=torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), python=sys.version.split()[0],
345:                 transformers=__import__("transformers").__version__, wall_s=time.time() - t0, steps=run.gstep,
346:                 sha256_steps_data=hashlib.sha256(steps.tobytes()).hexdigest(),
347:                 sha256_steps_data_18cols=hashlib.sha256(np.ascontiguousarray(steps[:, :18]).tobytes()).hexdigest(),
348:                 sha256_final_factors=hashlib.sha256(final.tobytes()).hexdigest(),
349:                 files={f: h(f) for f in ["steps.npz", "probes.npz", "evals.npz", "final_factors.npy", "evals_pre.npz"]
350:                        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(a.out, f))},
351:                 script_sha256=hashlib.sha256(open(__file__, "rb").read()).hexdigest(),
352:                 diag_pre_final=[[t, acc] for t, acc in run.pre_final])
353:     json.dump(meta, open(os.path.join(a.out, "meta.json"), "w"), indent=1)
354:     say(f"done: {run.gstep} steps, {time.time() - t0:.0f}s; steps sha256 {meta['sha256_steps_data'][:16]}; "
355:         f"factors sha256 {meta['sha256_final_factors'][:16]}")
356:     keep_awake(False)
357: 
358: if __name__ == "__main__":
359:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\lm\lm_superni_run.py
1: """Pilot 2, SuperNI check (7 Nov 2026, colloquium missing control): the FOREVER schedule on natural SuperNI tasks.
2: 
3:     D:\\Time\\_venv_cuda\\Scripts\\python.exe -I lm_superni_run.py --seed 7401 --clock tau --out ..\\runs\\sni\\tau\\s7401
4: 
5: Same schedule as lm_forever_run.py (FOREVER, github.com/WoodScene/FOREVER, commit bbeb549, src/finetune_cl_our_qwen.py and
6: scripts/run_train_cl_ours_qwen.sh, read 7 Nov 2026): per task a calibration phase of 24 steps (model day), replay thresholds
7: d * day, d in {1,2,4,7,15,30,60,90,120}, replay = 2 epochs over the memory buffer with loss + 0.001*beta*|theta-theta*|^2,
8: beta = clip(mu/mu0, 0.5, 3), a final replay at every task end, every phase a fresh AdamW (wd 0) + linear schedule with
9: warmup (10; 5 for replay); LoRA r 8, alpha 16, dropout 0.05 on q_proj, v_proj; effective batch 32 = micro-batch 4 x 8
10: accumulation (as their script); max length 512 tokens; loss on the response tokens only (train_on_inputs False), averaged
11: over the response tokens of the 32 examples; alpaca prompt template (templates/alpaca.json).
12: Data: D:\\Time\\_date\\FOREVER_bbeb549\\data_superni (order 7 of utils/dataset_order.py), first --tasks tasks.
13: Declared deviations: bf16 without GradScaler (colloquium decision 6; theirs fp16); epochs --epochs/--first_epochs
14: (theirs 10/60; reduced for laptop time); no group_by_length; memory = int(2% of the task's training set) per previous task,
15: class-stratified as their load_memory_buffer when the number of distinct outputs is at most that size, otherwise a random
16: sample of that size (their code would take one example per distinct output, i.e. almost the whole task for generation
17: tasks); the task's batches form one stream across its phases; the input text is truncated so that the template and the
18: response fit in 512 tokens. No probe set (D3 is not computed here). Evaluation (evals.npz): mean response-token loss on
19: the test set of every seen task at every task end (and, with --diag_pre_final_all, just before each final replay).
20: """
21: import os
22: os.environ.setdefault("CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG", ":4096:8")
23: import argparse, ctypes, hashlib, json, math, platform, sys, time
24: import numpy as np
25: import torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F
26: 
27: MODEL_DIR = r"D:\Time\_modele\Qwen_Qwen3-0.6B"
28: MODEL_SHA256 = "f47f71177f32bcd101b7573ec9171e6a57f4f4d31148d38e382306f42996874b"
29: DATA = r"D:\Time\_date\FOREVER_bbeb549\data_superni"
30: ORDER7 = ["task1572", "task363", "task1290", "task181", "task002", "task1510", "task639", "task1729", "task073",
31:           "task1590", "task748", "task511", "task591", "task1687", "task875"]
32: HEAD_IN = ("Below is an instruction that describes a task, paired with an input that provides further context. Write a "
33:            "response that appropriately completes the request.\n\n### Instruction:\n{instruction}\n\n### Input:\n")
34: TAIL = "\n\n### Response:\n"
35: KINDS = ["calib", "current", "replay", "final_replay"]
36: AMP = torch.bfloat16
37: 
38: def keep_awake(on=True):
39:     if platform.system() == "Windows":
40:         ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(0x80000000 | (0x00000001 if on else 0))
41: 
42: class LoRA(nn.Module):
43:     def __init__(self, base, r, alpha, dropout, gen):
44:         super().__init__()
45:         self.base, self.scale = base, alpha / r
46:         dev = base.weight.device
47:         bound = 1.0 / math.sqrt(base.in_features)
48:         self.A = nn.Parameter((torch.rand(r, base.in_features, generator=gen) * 2 - 1).mul_(bound).to(dev))
49:         self.B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, r, device=dev))
50:         self.drop = nn.Dropout(dropout)
51:     def forward(self, x):
52:         return self.base(x) + F.linear(F.linear(self.drop(x), self.A), self.B) * self.scale
53: 
54: def build_model(seed, args):
55:     from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
56:     tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, local_files_only=True)
57:     model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, local_files_only=True, dtype=AMP,
58:                                                  attn_implementation=args.attn).to("cuda")
59:     for p in model.parameters():
60:         p.requires_grad_(False)
61:     gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 10 + 1)
62:     loras = []
63:     for layer in model.model.layers:
64:         for nm in ["q_proj", "v_proj"]:
65:             m = LoRA(getattr(layer.self_attn, nm), args.r, args.alpha, args.dropout, gen)
66:             setattr(layer.self_attn, nm, m); loras.append(m)
67:     if args.grad_ckpt:
68:         model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
69:     return tok, model, loras
70: 
71: def load_tasks(tok, n_tasks, cutoff):
72:     enc = lambda s: tok(s, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
73:     tail = enc(TAIL); eos = tok.eos_token_id
74:     tasks = []
75:     for name in ORDER7[:n_tasks]:
76:         task = {"name": name}
77:         for sp in ["train", "test"]:
78:             rows = json.load(open(os.path.join(DATA, sp, name + ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
79:             ex = []
80:             for r in rows:
81:                 h = enc(HEAD_IN.format(instruction=r["instruction"])); i = enc(r["input"])
82:                 o = (enc(str(r["output"])) + [eos])[: cutoff // 2]
83:                 budget = cutoff - len(h) - len(tail) - len(o)
84:                 if budget < 0:
85:                     h = h[: max(1, len(h) + budget)]; budget = 0
86:                 ex.append((h + i[:budget] + tail, o, str(r["output"])))
87:             task[sp] = ex
88:         tasks.append(task)
89:     return tasks
90: 
91: class Run:
92:     def __init__(self, args):
93:         self.a = args
94:         torch.manual_seed(args.seed); np.random.seed(args.seed)
95:         self.tok, self.model, self.loras = build_model(args.seed, args)
96:         self.model.train()                                   # LoRA dropout and gradient checkpointing are active in train mode
97:         self.pad =self.tok.pad_token_id if self.tok.pad_token_id is not None else self.tok.eos_token_id
98:         self.T = load_tasks(self.tok, args.tasks, args.cutoff)
99:         self.params = [p for m in self.loras for p in (m.A, m.B)]
100:         self.data_gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed * 10 + 3)
101:         self.mem_gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed * 10 + 4)
102:         self.rows, self.evals, self.phases, self.pre_final_all = [], [], [], []
103:         self.gstep = 0; self.clk = 0.0
104:         self.prev = self.flat().clone()
105:         self.memory = []                                     # (task, index in its train set)
106: 
107:     def flat(self):
108:         return torch.cat([p.detach().reshape(-1) for p in self.params])
109: 
110:     def batches(self, items, epochs):
111:         for _ in range(epochs):
112:             perm = torch.randperm(len(items), generator=self.data_gen).tolist()
113:             for i in range(0, len(perm), self.a.batch):
114:                 yield [items[j] for j in perm[i:i + self.a.batch]]
115: 
116:     def ex(self, it):
117:         t, j = it
118:         return self.T[t]["train"][j]
119: 
120:     def loss_sum(self, exs):
121:         """Sum of the response-token cross-entropies of a micro-batch, and the number of those tokens."""
122:         L = max(len(p) + len(o) for p, o, _ in exs)
123:         ids = torch.full((len(exs), L), self.pad, dtype=torch.long); att = torch.zeros((len(exs), L), dtype=torch.long)
124:         rows, cols, tgt = [], [], []
125:         for b, (p, o, _) in enumerate(exs):
126:             seq = p + o; ids[b, :len(seq)] = torch.tensor(seq); att[b, :len(seq)] = 1
127:             for k in range(len(o)):
128:                 rows.append(b); cols.append(len(p) - 1 + k); tgt.append(o[k])
129:         with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=AMP):
130:             h = self.model.model(input_ids=ids.cuda(), attention_mask=att.cuda()).last_hidden_state
131:             logits = self.model.lm_head(h[torch.tensor(rows, device="cuda"), torch.tensor(cols, device="cuda")])
132:         return F.cross_entropy(logits.float(), torch.tensor(tgt, device="cuda"), reduction="sum"), len(tgt)
133: 
134:     def clock_inc(self, d, lr, m2):
135:         c = self.a.clock
136:         return d if c == "tau" else lr * math.sqrt(m2) if c == "eta_m" else lr if c == "eta" else 1.0
137: 
138:     def phase(self, kind, task, data, max_steps, warmup, stop_clk=None, beta=None, theta_star=None, counted=True):
139:         a = self.a
140:         opt = torch.optim.AdamW(self.params, lr=a.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.999), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=0.0)
141:         lam = lambda s: s / max(1, warmup) if s < warmup else max(0.0, (max_steps - s) / max(1.0, max_steps - warmup))
142:         sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(opt, lam)
143:         start, n = self.gstep, 0
144:         while n < max_steps:
145:             batch = next(data); lr = opt.param_groups[0]["lr"]
146:             exs = [self.ex(it) for it in batch]; ntok = sum(len(o) for _, o, _ in exs); lsum = 0.0
147:             for i in range(0, len(exs), a.micro):
148:                 s, _ = self.loss_sum(exs[i:i + a.micro])
149:                 (s / ntok).backward(); lsum += float(s.detach())
150:             if theta_star is not None:
151:                 reg = sum(((p - q) ** 2).sum() for p, q in zip(self.params, theta_star))
152:                 (0.001 * beta * reg).backward()
153:             loss = lsum / ntok
154:             gn = float(torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.params, 1.0))
155:             gn_after = float(torch.sqrt(sum((p.grad.float() ** 2).sum() for p in self.params)))
156:             opt.step(); sched.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
157:             st = [opt.state[p] for p in self.params if p in opt.state and "exp_avg" in opt.state[p]]
158:             k = float(st[0]["step"])
159:             mv = torch.stack([torch.stack([(s_["exp_avg"] ** 2).sum(), s_["exp_avg_sq"].sum()]) for s_ in st]).sum(0)
160:             m2, v1 = (mv / torch.tensor([(1 - 0.9 ** k) ** 2, 1 - 0.999 ** k], device=mv.device)).tolist()
161:             cur = self.flat(); d = float(torch.linalg.vector_norm(cur - self.prev)); self.prev = cur.clone()
162:             if counted:
163:                 self.tau += d; self.clk += self.clock_inc(d, lr, m2)
164:                 if self.mu0 is not None:
165:                     self.mu = 0.95 * self.mu + 0.05 * d
166:             self.gstep += 1; n += 1
167:             self.rows.append((self.gstep, task, KINDS.index(kind), len(self.phases), n, lr, loss, gn, gn_after, 0,
168:                               math.sqrt(m2), math.sqrt(v1), d, ntok, int(counted), self.tau,
169:                               self.mu if self.mu is not None else np.nan, beta if beta is not None else np.nan, self.clk))
170:             if not math.isfinite(loss):
171:                 raise RuntimeError(f"non-finite loss at step {self.gstep}")
172:             if counted and stop_clk is not None and self.clk >= stop_clk:
173:                 break
174:         self.phases.append((task, KINDS.index(kind), start, self.gstep, max_steps, warmup))
175:         return n
176: 
177:     def replay_phase(self, kind, task, theta_star):
178:         beta = min(3.0, max(0.5, self.mu / (self.mu0 + 1e-12)))
179:         M = len(self.memory); steps = 2 * max(1, math.ceil(M / self.a.batch))
180:         self.phase(kind, task, self.batches(self.memory, 2), steps, 5, beta=beta, theta_star=theta_star, counted=False)
181:         self.prev = self.flat().clone()
182: 
183:     @torch.no_grad()
184:     def test_losses(self, upto):
185:         self.model.eval(); out = []
186:         for t in range(upto + 1):
187:             exs = self.T[t]["test"]; s = n = 0.0
188:             for i in range(0, len(exs), 8):
189:                 ls, k = self.loss_sum(exs[i:i + 8]); s += float(ls); n += k
190:             out.append(s / n)
191:         self.model.train()
192:         return out
193: 
194:     def memory_for(self, t):
195:         tr = self.T[t]["train"]; n = len(tr); num = max(1, int(n * self.a.mem_ratio / 100))
196:         labels = [e[2] for e in tr]; classes = sorted(set(labels))
197:         perm = torch.randperm(n, generator=self.mem_gen).tolist()
198:         if len(classes) <= num:                               # their class-stratified rule
199:             per = max(1, num // len(classes)); picked = []
200:             for c in classes:
201:                 picked += [i for i in perm if labels[i] == c][:per]
202:             chosen = set(picked); picked += [i for i in perm if i not in chosen][:max(0, num - len(picked))]
203:         else:                                                 # declared deviation (see docstring)
204:             picked = perm[:num]
205:         return [(t, j) for j in picked]
206: 
207:     def task(self, t):
208:         a = self.a
209:         epochs = a.first_epochs if t == 0 else a.epochs
210:         items = [(t, j) for j in range(len(self.T[t]["train"]))]
211:         total = math.ceil(len(items) / a.batch * epochs)
212:         data = self.batches(items, epochs)
213:         self.tau, self.mu, self.mu0, self.clk = 0.0, None, None, 0.0
214:         theta_star = [p.detach().clone() for p in self.params]
215:         have_mem = len(self.memory) > 0
216:         done = self.phase("calib", t, data, min(24, total), 10)
217:         model_day = max(self.clk, 1e-8); thr = [d * model_day for d in (1, 2, 4, 7, 15, 30, 60, 90, 120)]; nxt = 0
218:         self.mu0 = self.tau / done; self.mu = self.mu0
219:         while done < total:
220:             if have_mem and nxt < len(thr) and self.clk >= thr[nxt]:
221:                 self.replay_phase("replay", t, theta_star); nxt += 1
222:                 continue
223:             stop = thr[nxt] if (have_mem and nxt < len(thr)) else None
224:             done += self.phase("current", t, data, total - done, 10, stop_clk=stop)
225:         if have_mem:
226:             if a.diag_pre_final_all:
227:                 self.pre_final_all.append((t, self.gstep, self.test_losses(t)))
228:             self.replay_phase("final_replay", t, theta_star)
229:         res = self.test_losses(t); self.evals.append((t, self.gstep, res))
230:         self.memory += self.memory_for(t)
231:         return total, model_day, res
232: 
233: def main():
234:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
235:     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
236:     ap.add_argument("--tasks", type=int, default=5); ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=3)
237:     ap.add_argument("--first_epochs", type=int, default=10); ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=32)
238:     ap.add_argument("--micro", type=int, default=4); ap.add_argument("--cutoff", type=int, default=512)
239:     ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=3e-4); ap.add_argument("--r", type=int, default=8)
240:     ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=16.0); ap.add_argument("--dropout", type=float, default=0.05)
241:     ap.add_argument("--mem_ratio", type=float, default=2.0); ap.add_argument("--attn", default="sdpa")
242:     ap.add_argument("--grad_ckpt", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--diag_pre_final_all", action="store_true")
243:     ap.add_argument("--clock", default="tau", choices=["tau", "eta_m", "eta", "steps"])
244:     ap.add_argument("--max_tasks_steps", type=int, default=0, help="speed probe only: stop after this many optimizer steps")
245:     a = ap.parse_args()
246:     os.makedirs(a.out, exist_ok=True)
247:     if os.path.exists(os.path.join(a.out, "meta.json")):
248:         print("complete run found, skipping:", a.out); return
249:     torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True); torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
250:     torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
251:     keep_awake(True); t0 = time.time()
252:     run = Run(a)
253:     cfg = vars(a).copy(); cfg.pop("out")
