254:     log = open(os.path.join(a.out, "run.log"), "a", encoding="utf-8")
255:     def say(s):
256:         print(s, flush=True); log.write(s + "\n"); log.flush()
257:     lens = {T["name"]: (len(T["train"]), int(np.median([len(p) + len(o) for p, o, _ in T["train"]]))) for T in run.T}
258:     say(f"seed {a.seed}; trainable {sum(p.numel() for p in run.params)}; tasks (train n, median tokens) {lens}; config {json.dumps(cfg)}")
259:     if a.max_tasks_steps:
260:         items = [(0, j) for j in range(len(run.T[0]["train"]))]; run.tau, run.mu, run.mu0 = 0.0, None, None
261:         tt = time.time(); run.phase("calib", 0, run.batches(items, 100), a.max_tasks_steps, 10)
262:         say(f"speed probe: {a.max_tasks_steps} steps in {time.time() - tt:.1f}s; max VRAM {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 2**20:.0f} MiB")
263:         return
264:     for t in range(a.tasks):
265:         tt = time.time()
266:         total, day, res = run.task(t)
267:         say(f"task {t} ({run.T[t]['name']}): {total} steps, model_day {day:.4f}, tau {run.tau:.3f}, step {run.gstep}, "
268:             f"test losses {[round(x, 3) for x in res]}, {time.time() - tt:.0f}s, max VRAM {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 2**20:.0f} MiB")
269:     cols = ["gstep", "task", "kind", "phase", "step_in_phase", "lr", "loss", "gnorm", "gnorm_clipped", "skipped",
270:             "m_hat_norm", "sqrt_trv", "delta", "ntok", "counted", "tau", "mu", "beta", "clk"]
271:     steps = np.array(run.rows, dtype=np.float64)
272:     np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "steps.npz"), cols=np.array(cols), data=steps, phases=np.array(run.phases, dtype=np.int64))
273:     pad = lambda r: r + [np.nan] * (a.tasks - len(r))
274:     np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "evals.npz"), task=np.array([e[0] for e in run.evals]),
275:                         gstep=np.array([e[1] for e in run.evals]), loss=np.array([pad(e[2]) for e in run.evals]))
276:     if run.pre_final_all:
277:         np.savez_compressed(os.path.join(a.out, "evals_pre.npz"), task=np.array([e[0] for e in run.pre_final_all]),
278:                             gstep=np.array([e[1] for e in run.pre_final_all]), loss=np.array([pad(e[2]) for e in run.pre_final_all]))
279:     final = run.flat().cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32); np.save(os.path.join(a.out, "final_factors.npy"), final)
280:     h = lambda f: hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(a.out, f), "rb").read()).hexdigest()
281:     meta = dict(config=cfg, model_sha256=MODEL_SHA256, torch=torch.__version__, cuda=torch.version.cuda,
282:                 device=torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), transformers=__import__("transformers").__version__,
283:                 wall_s=time.time() - t0, steps=run.gstep, memory_size=len(run.memory),
284:                 sha256_steps_data=hashlib.sha256(steps.tobytes()).hexdigest(),
285:                 sha256_final_factors=hashlib.sha256(final.tobytes()).hexdigest(),
286:                 files={f: h(f) for f in ["steps.npz", "evals.npz", "final_factors.npy", "evals_pre.npz"] if os.path.exists(os.path.join(a.out, f))},
287:                 script_sha256=hashlib.sha256(open(__file__, "rb").read()).hexdigest())
288:     json.dump(meta, open(os.path.join(a.out, "meta.json"), "w"), indent=1)
289:     say(f"done: {run.gstep} steps, {time.time() - t0:.0f}s; steps sha256 {meta['sha256_steps_data'][:16]}; "
290:         f"factors sha256 {meta['sha256_final_factors'][:16]}")
291:     keep_awake(False)
292: 
293: if __name__ == "__main__":
294:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\lm\ta_memory_facts_posthoc.py
1: """POST HOC test (b), 9 Nov 2026, after the final review round: T-A retention on the facts held in memory. CPU only.
2: 
3: T-A stored only the per-task accuracy (evals.npz), not which facts were recalled. Each run also stored its final LoRA
4: factors (final_factors.npy, hash in meta.json), and the world and the memory buffer are fixed by the seed
5: (lm_forever_run.py: make_world with generator seed*10+2; memory = 16 facts per task from randperm with generator
6: seed*10+4). This script rebuilds both on the CPU, loads the final factors into Qwen3-0.6B (bf16 weights, fp32 LoRA,
7: bf16 autocast, as the T-A runs on the GPU), and records for every fact whether the final model recalls it
8: (argmax over the vocabulary = answer word, as lm_forever_run.evaluate). Validation: the per-task accuracies recomputed
9: here are compared with the stored final row of evals.npz (the GPU and the CPU kernels differ, so a few borderline facts
10: may flip; the number is reported).
11: Output: runs/ta_memory_facts_posthoc.npz (correct[clock, seed, fact], memory[seed, fact], stored and recomputed
12: per-task accuracies). Read by Stadiul0/ta_memory_posthoc.py.
13:     python -I lm/ta_memory_facts_posthoc.py [clock seed]   (from Stadiul0; default: all 4 clocks x 5 seeds)
14: """
15: import hashlib, json, math, sys, time
16: from pathlib import Path
17: import numpy as np
18: import torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F
19: 
20: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
21: S = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
22: TA = S / "runs" / "ta"
23: MODEL_DIR = r"D:\Time\_modele\Qwen_Qwen3-0.6B"
24: CLOCKS = ["tau", "eta_m", "eta", "steps"]
25: SEEDS = [7301, 7302, 7303, 7304, 7305]
26: sys.path.insert(0, str(S / "lm"))
27: import lm_forever_run as L                                  # WORDS, LETTERS, the LoRA module (unchanged)
28: 
29: 
30: def world(tok, seed, n_tasks, per_task, n_probe):
31:     """lm_forever_run.make_world without the .cuda() calls."""
32:     words = [w for w in L.WORDS if len(tok(" " + w, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids) == 1]
33:     wid = torch.tensor([tok(" " + w, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[0] for w in words])
34:     g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 10 + 2)
35:     codes, seen = [], set()
36:     while len(codes) < n_tasks * per_task + n_probe:
37:         c = "".join(L.LETTERS[j] for j in torch.randint(len(L.LETTERS), (6,), generator=g).tolist())
38:         if c not in seen:
39:             seen.add(c); codes.append(c[:3] + "-" + c[3:])
40:     ans = torch.randint(len(words), (len(codes),), generator=g)
41:     ids = [tok(f"code {c} is", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids for c in codes]
42:     Lm = max(map(len, ids)); pad = tok.pad_token_id if tok.pad_token_id is not None else tok.eos_token_id
43:     x = torch.full((len(ids), Lm), pad); att = torch.zeros((len(ids), Lm), dtype=torch.long)
44:     for i, s in enumerate(ids):
45:         x[i, Lm - len(s):] = torch.tensor(s); att[i, Lm - len(s):] = 1
46:     pos = (att.cumsum(1) - 1).clamp(min=0)
47:     return x, att, pos, wid[ans]
48: 
49: 
50: def memory(seed, n_tasks, per_task, mem):
51:     g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 10 + 4); m = np.zeros(n_tasks * per_task, bool)
52:     for t in range(n_tasks):                                 # one randperm per task end, as Run.task
53:         keep = torch.arange(t * per_task, (t + 1) * per_task)[torch.randperm(per_task, generator=g)[:mem]]
54:         if t < n_tasks - 1:                                  # the last task's draw is never replayed
55:             m[keep.numpy()] = True
56:     return m
57: 
58: 
59: def main():
60:     from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
61:     torch.manual_seed(0)
62:     todo = [(sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2]))] if len(sys.argv) == 3 else [(c, s) for c in CLOCKS for s in SEEDS]
63:     tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, local_files_only=True)
64:     model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_DIR, local_files_only=True, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
65:     for p in model.parameters():
66:         p.requires_grad_(False)
67:     gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)
68:     loras = []
69:     for layer in model.model.layers:
70:         for nm in ["q_proj", "v_proj"]:
71:             m = L.LoRA(getattr(layer.self_attn, nm), 8, 16.0, 0.05, gen)
72:             setattr(layer.self_attn, nm, m); loras.append(m)
73:     model.eval()
74:     out = S / "runs" / "ta_memory_facts_posthoc.npz"
75:     prev = dict(np.load(out, allow_pickle=False)) if out.exists() else {}
76:     correct = np.full((len(CLOCKS), len(SEEDS), 5120), -1, np.int8)
77:     mem = np.zeros((len(SEEDS), 5120), bool); acc_st = np.full((len(CLOCKS), len(SEEDS), 10), np.nan)
78:     acc_cpu = acc_st.copy()
79:     if prev:
80:         correct, mem, acc_st, acc_cpu = prev["correct"], prev["memory"], prev["acc_stored"], prev["acc_cpu"]
81:     for clock, seed in todo:
82:         if prev and len(todo) > 1 and (correct[CLOCKS.index(clock), SEEDS.index(seed)] >= 0).any():
83:             continue                                         # already computed
84:         t0 = time.time(); rd = TA / clock / f"s{seed}"
85:         meta = json.load(open(rd / "meta.json")); cfg = meta["config"]
86:         assert cfg["precision"] == "bf16" and cfg["clock"] == clock and cfg["per_task"] == 512 and cfg["mem_per_task"] == 16
87:         f = np.load(rd / "final_factors.npy")
88:         assert hashlib.sha256(f.tobytes()).hexdigest() == meta["sha256_final_factors"]
89:         o = 0
90:         for lo in loras:                                     # Run.flat(): A then B for each module, layer order
91:             for p in (lo.A, lo.B):
92:                 n = p.numel(); p.data.copy_(torch.from_numpy(f[o:o + n]).view_as(p)); o += n
93:         assert o == f.size
94:         x, att, pos, y = world(tok, seed, 10, 512, 128)
95:         ci, si = CLOCKS.index(clock), SEEDS.index(seed)
96:         mem[si] = memory(seed, 10, 512, 16)
97:         full = (clock, seed) == ("tau", 7301)                # the validation run: all 5120 facts; the others: memory only
98:         idx = np.arange(5120) if full else np.flatnonzero(mem[si])
99:         hit = np.full(5120, -1, np.int8)
100:         with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cpu", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
101:             for i in range(0, len(idx), 128):
102:                 j = torch.from_numpy(idx[i:i+128])
103:                 lg = model(input_ids=x[j], attention_mask=att[j], position_ids=pos[j], logits_to_keep=1).logits[:, -1, :]
104:                 hit[idx[i:i+128]] = (lg.argmax(-1) == y[j]).numpy()
105:         correct[ci, si] = hit
106:         acc_st[ci, si] = np.load(rd / "evals.npz")["acc"][-1]
107:         msg = ""
108:         if full:
109:             acc_cpu[ci, si] = hit.reshape(10, 512).mean(1)
110:             flips = np.abs(acc_cpu[ci, si] - acc_st[ci, si]) * 512
111:             msg = (f"; validation, |recomputed - stored| correct facts per task: {flips.round().astype(int).tolist()}; "
112:                    f"other facts of tasks 0-8 {hit[:4608][~mem[si][:4608]].mean():.3f}")
113:         print(f"{clock:6s} s{seed}: {time.time() - t0:.0f} s; memory facts recalled {hit[mem[si]].mean():.3f}{msg}", flush=True)
114:         np.savez_compressed(out, correct=correct, memory=mem, acc_stored=acc_st, acc_cpu=acc_cpu,
115:                             clocks=np.array(CLOCKS), seeds=np.array(SEEDS))
116: 
117: 
118: if __name__ == "__main__":
119:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\drift_ratio_posthoc.py
1: # POST HOC, not preregistered. Written on 9 Nov 2026, after the stage 1 verdict, so that the drift ratios quoted in the
2: # closure report (section 5.1) and in Rezultate_teste_decisive_2026-11-07.md can be recomputed from the archive. The same
3: # numbers had been computed by hand on 8-9 Nov; this script only fixes the computation in a file. It reads the drift logs
4: # of the two runs in which the eta*|m_hat| clock drove replay (R2 = ckpt_eta_m, task-0 adapter B; eta_m_A1, adapter A);
5: # the tau runs do not log the eta clock, so the ratio cannot be computed there.
6: # For each task with replay (1-3): r = (sum |dtheta| / sum eta-increment over the calibration window, steps 1-24)
7: #                                    / (the same ratio over the rest of the task, steps 25-313).
8: # r < 1 means that after calibration |dtheta| grows relative to the eta clock, so the eta clock lags behind tau.
9: # Usage: python -I drift_ratio_posthoc.py   (prints only; writes nothing)
10: import csv, sys
11: from pathlib import Path
12: import numpy as np
13: 
14: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
15: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
16: ARMS = {"eta_m (R2, adapter B)": "ckpt_eta_m", "eta_m_A1 (adapter A)": "ckpt_eta_m_A1"}
17: TASKS = ["1-amazon", "2-yahoo", "3-agnews"]
18: 
19: print("calibration-to-rest ratio of sum|dtheta| / sum eta-increment (post hoc)")
20: for label, folder in ARMS.items():
21:     vals = []
22:     for t in TASKS:
23:         rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(HERE / folder / "Qwen_Qwen3-0.6Blora_cl_dataset_id_4" / t / "drift_log.csv",
24:                                         encoding="utf-8")))
25:         steps = np.array([int(r["step"]) for r in rows])
26:         d = np.array([float(r["delta_t"]) for r in rows])
27:         inc = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0.0], np.array([float(r["tau"]) for r in rows])]))
28:         cal = steps <= 24
29:         vals.append((d[cal].sum() / inc[cal].sum()) / (d[~cal].sum() / inc[~cal].sum()))
30:     print(f"  {label:22s} " + "  ".join(f"{t[2:]} {v:.3f}" for t, v in zip(TASKS, vals)))

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\d_same_trajectory.py
1: # Option (d) of the 8 Nov 2026 colloquium (Colocviu/Verificare_raspunsuri_2026-11-08.md): zero-GPU, POST HOC analysis of
2: # R2's drift logs, outputs fixed in preinregistrare_FOREVER_repetari_2026-11-08.md (version 3) BEFORE this script ran.
3: # On R2's trajectory both clocks are logged: tau = the eta*|m_hat| clock that drove the replays, tau_true = sum |dtheta|.
4: #  (1) validation: thresholds mult*day on the eta clock (day = clock at step 24) must reproduce R2's actual trigger steps;
5: #  (2) counterfactual: where tau_true (calibrated the same way) would cross its thresholds on the SAME trajectory;
6: #      only up to and including the first trigger index where the two differ is the comparison clean;
7: #  (3) ratio |dtheta| / eta-increment by position since the last phase start (phase starts: step 1, step 25, and the
8: #      step after every actual trigger), normalised by the task median, to see whether divergence sits after restarts;
9: #  (4) per-task R^2 (8-step windows, same as r2_reconstruction.py) with and without the windows that contain a phase start.
10: # Usage: python -I d_same_trajectory.py ; writes runs/forever_d_same_trajectory.json
11: import csv, json, sys
12: from pathlib import Path
13: import numpy as np
14: 
15: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
16: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
17: ROOT = HERE / "ckpt_eta_m" / "Qwen_Qwen3-0.6Blora_cl_dataset_id_4"
18: MULTS = [1, 2, 4, 7, 15, 30, 60, 90, 120]
19: ACTUAL = {"1-amazon": [24, 50, 104, 192], "2-yahoo": [24, 48, 96, 178], "3-agnews": [24, 47, 96, 193]}  # R2 logs
20: R1_TAU = {"1-amazon": [24, 48, 100, 185], "2-yahoo": [24, 46, 87, 160], "3-agnews": [24, 47, 93, 160]}  # R1 logs
21: 
22: 
23: def crossings(cum, steps, day):
24:     out = []
25:     for m in MULTS:
26:         idx = np.where(cum >= m * day - 1e-15)[0]
27:         if len(idx) == 0:
28:             break
29:         out.append(int(steps[idx[0]]))
30:     return out
31: 
32: 
33: def r2(x, y):
34:     X = np.column_stack([np.ones_like(x), x])
35:     beta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(X, y, rcond=None)
36:     res = y - X @ beta
37:     return float(1 - (res ** 2).sum() / ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum())
38: 
39: 
40: out = {}
41: for t, act in ACTUAL.items():
42:     rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(ROOT / t / "drift_log.csv", encoding="utf-8")))
43:     steps = np.array([int(r["step"]) for r in rows])
44:     d = np.array([float(r["delta_t"]) for r in rows])
45:     eta = np.array([float(r["tau"]) for r in rows])
46:     true = np.array([float(r["tau_true"]) for r in rows])
47:     i24 = int(np.where(steps == 24)[0][0])
48:     eta_tr = [s for s in crossings(eta, steps, eta[i24]) if s <= 313][: len(act)]
49:     tau_tr = [s for s in crossings(true, steps, true[i24]) if s <= 313][: len(act)]
50:     first_diff = next((k for k in range(min(len(eta_tr), len(tau_tr))) if eta_tr[k] != tau_tr[k]), None)
51:     clean = tau_tr[: first_diff + 1] if first_diff is not None else tau_tr
52:     # (3) ratio by position since the last phase start
53:     inc = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0.0], eta]))
54:     starts = sorted({1, 25} | {s + 1 for s in act[1:]})
55:     pos = np.array([s - max(p for p in starts if p <= s) for s in steps])
56:     ok = inc > 0
57:     ratio = np.where(ok, d / np.where(ok, inc, 1), np.nan)
58:     med = np.nanmedian(ratio)
59:     bins = {"0": pos == 0, "1": pos == 1, "2": pos == 2, "3-4": (pos >= 3) & (pos <= 4), "5-9": (pos >= 5) & (pos <= 9),
60:             "10+": pos >= 10}
61:     by_pos = {k: (float(np.nanmedian(ratio[m]) / med) if np.any(m & ok) else None) for k, m in bins.items()}
62:     n_by_pos = {k: int(np.sum(m & ok)) for k, m in bins.items()}
63:     # (4) R^2 with / without windows containing a phase start
64:     nb = len(d) // 8
65:     y = d[: nb * 8].reshape(nb, 8).sum(1); x = inc[: nb * 8].reshape(nb, 8).sum(1)
66:     has_start = np.array([any(s in starts for s in steps[k * 8:(k + 1) * 8]) for k in range(nb)])
67:     out[t] = {"validation_eta_triggers_recomputed": eta_tr, "eta_triggers_actual_R2": act,
68:               "validation_ok": eta_tr == act, "counterfactual_tau_triggers_same_trajectory": tau_tr,
69:               "first_differing_index": first_diff, "clean_part": clean, "R1_tau_triggers_other_trajectory": R1_TAU[t],
70:               "ratio_dtheta_over_eta_inc_by_position_rel_median": by_pos, "n_by_position": n_by_pos,
71:               "R2_all_windows": r2(x, y), "R2_without_phase_start_windows": r2(x[~has_start], y[~has_start]),
72:               "windows_with_start": int(has_start.sum()), "windows_total": int(nb)}
73:     o = out[t]
74:     print(f"{t}: eta recomputed {eta_tr} (actual {act}, ok={o['validation_ok']}); tau on same trajectory {tau_tr}; "
75:           f"R1 tau {R1_TAU[t]}; first differing index {first_diff}")
76:     print(f"   ratio |dtheta|/eta-inc by position (rel. to median): "
77:           + ", ".join(f"{k}: {v:.2f} (n={n_by_pos[k]})" if v is not None else f"{k}: -" for k, v in by_pos.items()))
78:     print(f"   R2 all windows {o['R2_all_windows']:.3f}; without {o['windows_with_start']} phase-start windows "
79:           f"{o['R2_without_phase_start_windows']:.3f}")
80: json.dump(out, open(HERE.parent / "runs" / "forever_d_same_trajectory.json", "w"), indent=1)

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\eval_noise_posthoc.py
1: """POST HOC test (a), 9 Nov 2026, after the final review round (Recenzii/Runda_finala_2026-11-09): evaluation noise.
2: 
3: Regenerates the OP outputs of the FINAL adapter (task 3, agnews) of one cell of stage 1 with the authors' own
4: src/generate_avgPerf.py, imported and called unchanged, in one of two modes:
5:   seed<N>  their decoding settings (do_sample=True, temperature 0.02, top_p 0, top_k 1), with every RNG seeded to N;
6:   greedy   the same call with do_sample=False (temperature, top_p and top_k dropped; nothing else changed).
7: Outputs go to output/_eval_noise_posthoc/<arm>_<mode>/; the original outputs (output/<arm>/) are never touched.
8: A partial output folder from an interrupted attempt is renamed (kept), never deleted, and the evaluation restarts.
9: Every 100 generations it checks runs/_HW_STOP (hardware watchdog) and exits with code 3 if the file exists.
10: 
11: Run from forever_repro/ in the authors' environment (see launch_eval_noise_posthoc.sh):
12:     PYTHONPATH=. FOREVER_DATA=data_longsequence HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 <_venv_forever python> eval_noise_posthoc.py <arm> <mode>
13: <arm> in {tau, eta_m, eta_m_A1, tau_B1}: the four cells of stage 1 (R1, R2, A1, B1).
14: """
15: import datetime as dt, os, random, sys
16: from pathlib import Path
17: 
18: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
19: STOP = HERE.parent / "runs" / "_HW_STOP"
20: ARMS = ("tau", "eta_m", "eta_m_A1", "tau_B1")
21: BM = "D:/Time/_modele/Qwen_Qwen3-0.6B"
22: N = "Qwen_Qwen3-0.6Blora_cl_dataset_id_4"
23: TASKS = ("0-dbpedia", "1-amazon", "2-yahoo", "3-agnews")
24: 
25: 
26: def main():
27:     arm, mode = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
28:     assert arm in ARMS, arm
29:     assert mode == "greedy" or (mode.startswith("seed") and mode[4:].isdigit()), mode
30:     os.chdir(HERE)
31:     root = Path("output") / "_eval_noise_posthoc" / f"{arm}_{mode}"
32:     res = root / f"{N}_avgPerf"
33:     test = {t: len(__import__("json").load(open(f"data_longsequence/test/{t.split('-')[1]}.json", encoding="utf-8")))
34:             for t in TASKS}
35:     done = res.exists() and all((res / f"{t}_result.txt").exists() and
36:                                 sum(1 for _ in open(res / f"{t}_result.txt", encoding="utf-8")) == test[t] for t in TASKS)
37:     if done:
38:         print(f"complete, skipping: {res}"); return 0
39:     if root.exists():                                   # interrupted attempt: keep it, start again
40:         keep = root.with_name(root.name + f"_incomplete_{dt.datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M%S}")
41:         root.rename(keep); print(f"partial output kept as {keep}")
42: 
43:     import numpy as np, torch, transformers
44:     orig = transformers.GenerationMixin.generate
45:     calls = [0]
46: 
47:     def generate(self, *a, **k):
48:         calls[0] += 1
49:         if calls[0] % 100 == 0 and STOP.exists():
50:             print(f"_HW_STOP found after {calls[0]} generations; exiting", flush=True); sys.exit(3)
51:         if mode == "greedy":
52:             k["do_sample"] = False
53:             for x in ("temperature", "top_p", "top_k"):
54:                 k.pop(x, None)
55:         return orig(self, *a, **k)
56: 
57:     transformers.GenerationMixin.generate = generate
58:     if mode != "greedy":
59:         s = int(mode[4:])
60:         random.seed(s); np.random.seed(s); torch.manual_seed(s); torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(s)
61:     print(f"eval_noise_posthoc {arm} {mode} start {dt.datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}; torch {torch.__version__}, "
62:           f"transformers {transformers.__version__}", flush=True)
63:     sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE / "src"))
64:     import generate_avgPerf                               # the authors' script, unchanged
65:     generate_avgPerf.main(base_model=BM, dataset_id=4, method_name="cl", max_task_id=3,
66:                           output_root=str(root), lora_checkpoint_path=f"ckpt_{arm}")
67:     print(f"eval_noise_posthoc {arm} {mode} end {dt.datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}; generations {calls[0]}", flush=True)
68:     return 0
69: 
70: 
71: if __name__ == "__main__":
72:     sys.exit(main())

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\eval_noise_summary.py
1: # POST HOC test (a), 9 Nov 2026: summary of the evaluation-noise re-evaluations (eval_noise_posthoc.py). CPU only.
2: # Reads the authors' exact-match scores (their eval_avgPerf_longsequence.py output) of the original evaluation
3: # (runs/forever_repro_eval_<arm>.log) and of each re-evaluation (runs/forever_eval_noise_posthoc_<arm>_<mode>.log), and
4: # compares the generated answers line by line with the original outputs. Per task and cell: the standard deviation over
5: # the sampled evaluations (the original, unseeded, and the seeded ones), the greedy value, and the number of answers that
6: # differ from the original. Then the evaluation SD of a paired difference on dbpedia, against D_A1 and D_B1 of stage 1.
7: # Writes runs/forever_eval_noise_posthoc.json. Run from forever_repro/: python -I eval_noise_summary.py
8: import json, math, re, sys
9: from pathlib import Path
10: 
11: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
12: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
13: RUNS = HERE.parent / "runs"
14: N = "Qwen_Qwen3-0.6Blora_cl_dataset_id_4"
15: ARMS = ["tau", "eta_m", "eta_m_A1", "tau_B1"]
16: TASKS = ["dbpedia", "amazon", "yahoo", "agnews"]
17: PAIRS = {"A1": ("tau", "eta_m_A1"), "B1": ("tau_B1", "eta_m")}   # tau - eta_m, sharing a task-0 adapter (stage 1)
18: 
19: 
20: def scores(log):
21:     if not log.exists():
22:         return None
23:     acc = {k: 100 * float(v) for k, v in re.findall(r"任务 (\w+) 的 accuracy：([0-9.]+)", log.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))}
24:     return acc if len(acc) == 4 else None
25: 
26: 
27: def answers(folder, i, t):
28:     p = folder / f"{N}_avgPerf" / f"{i}-{t}_result.txt"
29:     return [l.rstrip("\n").split("|||")[-1] for l in open(p, encoding="utf-8")] if p.exists() else None
30: 
31: 
32: def sd(xs):
33:     if len(xs) < 2:
34:         return float("nan")
35:     m = sum(xs) / len(xs)
36:     return math.sqrt(sum((x - m) ** 2 for x in xs) / (len(xs) - 1))
37: 
38: 
39: modes = sorted({p.name.rsplit("_", 1)[1] for p in (HERE / "output" / "_eval_noise_posthoc").glob("*_*")
40:                 if p.is_dir() and "_incomplete_" not in p.name and p.name.rsplit("_", 1)[0] in ARMS},
41:                key=lambda m: (m == "greedy", m))
42: print(f"re-evaluation modes found: {modes}")
43: res = {"modes": modes, "cells": {}}
44: for arm in ARMS:
45:     orig = scores(RUNS / f"forever_repro_eval_{arm}.log")
46:     cell = {"original": orig}
47:     for m in modes:
48:         s = scores(RUNS / f"forever_eval_noise_posthoc_{arm}_{m}.log")
49:         if s is None:
50:             continue
51:         diff = {}
52:         for i, t in enumerate(TASKS):
53:             a, b = answers(HERE / "output" / arm, i, t), answers(HERE / "output" / "_eval_noise_posthoc" / f"{arm}_{m}", i, t)
54:             diff[t] = None if a is None or b is None or len(a) != len(b) else sum(x != y for x, y in zip(a, b))
55:         cell[m] = {"acc": s, "answers_differing_from_original": diff}
56:     sampled = [orig] + [cell[m]["acc"] for m in modes if m != "greedy" and m in cell]
57:     cell["sd_sampled"] = {t: sd([x[t] for x in sampled]) for t in TASKS}
58:     cell["n_sampled"] = len(sampled)
59:     res["cells"][arm] = cell
60:     print(f"\n{arm}: {len(sampled)} sampled evaluations (original + seeded)" + (", greedy" if "greedy" in cell else ""))
61:     for t in TASKS:
62:         line = f"  {t:8s} original {orig[t]:6.2f}"
63:         for m in modes:
64:             if m in cell:
65:                 line += f" | {m} {cell[m]['acc'][t]:6.2f} ({cell[m]['answers_differing_from_original'][t]} answers differ)"
66:         print(line + f" | SD sampled {cell['sd_sampled'][t]:.3f}")
67: 
68: pooled = {t: math.sqrt(sum(res["cells"][a]["sd_sampled"][t] ** 2 for a in ARMS) / len(ARMS)) for t in TASKS}
69: res["pooled_sd_sampled"] = pooled
70: print("\npooled within-cell SD of the sampled evaluations (points): " + ", ".join(f"{t} {pooled[t]:.3f}" for t in TASKS))
71: res["dbpedia_pairs"] = {}
72: for k, (x, y) in PAIRS.items():
73:     cx, cy = res["cells"][x], res["cells"][y]
74:     D = cx["original"]["dbpedia"] - cy["original"]["dbpedia"]
75:     sdd = math.sqrt(cx["sd_sampled"]["dbpedia"] ** 2 + cy["sd_sampled"]["dbpedia"] ** 2)
76:     per_mode = {m: cx[m]["acc"]["dbpedia"] - cy[m]["acc"]["dbpedia"] for m in modes if m in cx and m in cy}
77:     res["dbpedia_pairs"][k] = {"D_original": D, "sd_of_difference": sdd, "D_by_mode": per_mode,
78:                                "exceeds_eval_sd": abs(D) > sdd}
79:     print(f"D_{k} = {D:+.3f} (tau - eta_m on dbpedia); evaluation SD of the difference {sdd:.3f}; "
80:           f"|D| exceeds it: {abs(D) > sdd}; by mode: " + ", ".join(f"{m} {v:+.3f}" for m, v in per_mode.items()))
81: (RUNS / "forever_eval_noise_posthoc.json").write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\eval_tie_diagnostic_posthoc.py
1: """POST HOC diagnostic, 10 Nov 2026: why greedy decoding differs from the authors' decoding on a few items (test (a)).
2: 
3: For every item whose greedy answer (output/_eval_noise_posthoc/<arm>_greedy) differs from the original answer
4: (output/<arm>), the item is generated again with the final adapter, in the authors' way (prompt from their Prompter,
5: fp16 PeftModel, do_sample=True, temperature 0.02, top_p 0, top_k 1) and greedily, with output_scores. At the first
6: token where the two generations differ, it records the two highest raw logits of the greedy pass (the forward passes are
7: identical up to that token) and the raw logit of the token the authors' decoding chose. A tie means equal raw logits.
8: Writes runs/forever_eval_tie_diagnostic_posthoc.json and prints a summary.
9:     cd forever_repro; PYTHONPATH=. HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 <_venv_forever python> eval_tie_diagnostic_posthoc.py
10: """
11: import json, os, sys
12: from pathlib import Path
13: 
14: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
15: os.chdir(HERE)
16: sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE / "src"))
17: import torch
18: from peft import PeftModel
19: from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
20: from utils.prompter import Prompter
21: from utils.dataset_order import get_dataset_order
22: 
23: BM = "D:/Time/_modele/Qwen_Qwen3-0.6B"
24: N = "Qwen_Qwen3-0.6Blora_cl_dataset_id_4"
25: ARMS = ["tau", "eta_m", "eta_m_A1", "tau_B1"]
26: order = get_dataset_order(4)
27: prompter = Prompter("")
28: tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(BM)
29: if tok.pad_token_id is None:
30:     tok.pad_token_id = tok.eos_token_id
31: tok.padding_side = "left"
32: res = []
33: for arm in ARMS:
34:     base = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(BM, dtype=torch.float16, device_map="auto", trust_remote_code=True)
35:     model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base, f"ckpt_{arm}/{N}/3-agnews", dtype=torch.float16)
36:     model.config.pad_token_id = tok.pad_token_id
37:     model.eval()
38:     for i, task in enumerate(order):
39:         a = open(f"output/{arm}/{N}_avgPerf/{i}-{task}_result.txt", encoding="utf-8").read().split("\n")
40:         g = open(f"output/_eval_noise_posthoc/{arm}_greedy/{N}_avgPerf/{i}-{task}_result.txt", encoding="utf-8").read().split("\n")
41:         data = json.load(open(f"data_longsequence/test/{task}.json", encoding="utf-8"))
42:         for k, (x, y) in enumerate(zip(a, g)):
43:             if x == y:
44:                 continue
45:             s = data[k]
46:             ids = tok(prompter.generate_prompt(s["instruction"], s["input"]), return_tensors="pt").to("cuda")
47:             with torch.no_grad():
48:                 o1 = model.generate(**ids, do_sample=True, temperature=0.02, top_p=0, top_k=1, num_beams=1,
49:                                     max_new_tokens=128, return_dict_in_generate=True)
50:                 o2 = model.generate(**ids, do_sample=False, num_beams=1, max_new_tokens=128,
51:                                     return_dict_in_generate=True, output_scores=True)
52:             L = ids["input_ids"].shape[1]
53:             t1, t2 = o1.sequences[0, L:].tolist(), o2.sequences[0, L:].tolist()
54:             j = next((n for n, (p, q) in enumerate(zip(t1, t2)) if p != q), None)
55:             r = {"arm": arm, "task": task, "item": k, "reproduced": tok.decode(t1, skip_special_tokens=True) != tok.decode(t2, skip_special_tokens=True)}
56:             if j is not None and j < len(o2.scores):
57:                 sc = o2.scores[j][0].float()
58:                 top = torch.topk(sc, 2)
59:                 r.update(step=j, top1=float(top.values[0]), top2=float(top.values[1]), greedy_token=t2[j],
60:                          authors_token=t1[j], authors_token_logit=float(sc[t1[j]]),
61:                          tie=float(sc[t1[j]]) == float(top.values[0]))
62:             res.append(r)
63:     del model, base
64:     torch.cuda.empty_cache()
65: out = HERE.parent / "runs" / "forever_eval_tie_diagnostic_posthoc.json"
66: out.write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
67: n = len(res); rep = sum(r["reproduced"] for r in res); ties = sum(r.get("tie", False) for r in res)
68: print(f"items whose greedy answer differs from the original: {n}; difference reproduced now: {rep}; "
69:       f"first differing token is a tie of raw logits: {ties} of {sum('tie' in r for r in res)}")
70: for r in res:
71:     print(r)

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\make_clock_variant.py
1: # Builds src/finetune_cl_our_qwen_clock.py from their src/finetune_cl_our_qwen.py: ONLY the clock that drives replay changes.
2: from pathlib import Path
3: s = Path("src/finetune_cl_our_qwen.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
4: patches = [
5:     ("        self.tau += float(delta_t)\n        self.step += 1\n        self.prev_vec = curr_vec\n",
6:      "        # CLOCK SWAP (Pilot 2, 7 Nov 2026): 'tau' (the replay-driving clock) advances by FOREVER_CLOCK's increment;\n"
7:      "        # the true update norm is kept in tau_true; mu (replay strength) still uses delta_t, as in the original.\n"
8:      "        clock = os.environ.get(\"FOREVER_CLOCK\", \"tau\")\n"
9:      "        if clock == \"tau\":\n"
10:      "            inc = float(delta_t)\n"
11:      "        else:\n"
12:      "            opt = kwargs.get(\"optimizer\"); lr = getattr(self, \"_lr_at_step_begin\", None)\n"
13:      "            if lr is None:\n"
14:      "                lr = opt.param_groups[0][\"lr\"]\n"
15:      "            if clock == \"eta\":\n"
16:      "                inc = float(lr)\n"
17:      "            else:  # 'eta_m': lr * |m_hat| (bias-corrected first moment of AdamW)\n"
18:      "                sq, k = 0.0, None\n"
19:      "                for group in opt.param_groups:\n"
20:      "                    for p in group[\"params\"]:\n"
21:      "                        st = opt.state.get(p, {})\n"
22:      "                        if \"exp_avg\" in st:\n"
23:      "                            sq += float((st[\"exp_avg\"].float() ** 2).sum()); k = float(st[\"step\"])\n"
24:      "                inc = float(lr) * (sq ** 0.5) / (1 - 0.9 ** k) if k else 0.0\n"
25:      "        self.tau_true = getattr(self, \"tau_true\", 0.0) + float(delta_t)\n"
26:      "        self.tau += inc\n"
27:      "        self.step += 1\n"
28:      "        self.prev_vec = curr_vec\n"),
29:     ('f.write("step,delta_t,tau\\n")', 'f.write("step,delta_t,tau,tau_true\\n")'),
30:     ('f.write(f"{self.step},{delta_t},{self.tau}\\n")', 'f.write(f"{self.step},{delta_t},{self.tau},{self.tau_true}\\n")'),
31:     ("    def on_step_end(self, args, state, control, **kwargs):\n        model = kwargs[\"model\"]",
32:      "    def on_step_begin(self, args, state, control, **kwargs):  # CLOCK SWAP: lr applied at this step\n"
33:      "        opt = kwargs.get(\"optimizer\")\n"
34:      "        self._lr_at_step_begin = opt.param_groups[0][\"lr\"] if opt is not None else None\n\n"
35:      "    def on_step_end(self, args, state, control, **kwargs):\n        model = kwargs[\"model\"]"),
36: ]
37: for old, new in patches:
38:     n = s.count(old)
39:     assert n == 1, (n, old[:60])
40:     s = s.replace(old, new)
41: Path("src/finetune_cl_our_qwen_clock.py").write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
42: print("ok: clock variant written")

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\prepare_forever_copy.py
1: # Rebuilds, byte for byte, the FOREVER working copy used in Pilot 2 (runs R1, R2 and the repeated runs).
2: #
3: # The FOREVER repository (https://github.com/WoodScene/FOREVER) has no licence, so none of its files are
4: # redistributed in this archive. Get them yourself:
5: #     git clone https://github.com/WoodScene/FOREVER
6: #     cd FOREVER && git checkout bbeb549a21e59d9490f20a2b02099ee766a67f48
7: # then, from this folder (Stadiul0/forever_repro):
8: #     python -I prepare_forever_copy.py <path-to-FOREVER-checkout>
9: #
10: # What it does:
11: #  1. checks the 15 source files it needs against the SHA-256 of commit bbeb549 (recorded on 7 Nov 2026);
12: #  2. copies 8 of them unchanged (utils/, templates/, the two evaluation scripts) and the data_longsequence/ split;
13: #  3. applies the three documented adaptations (preregistration of 7 Nov 2026, adaptations 1-2): the local model
14: #     cache path in finetune_cl_our_qwen.py and the test-data path in the two generators (the originals hard-code
15: #     ../data_superni). On the laptop these files were saved with CRLF line endings; the script writes them the same way;
16: #  4. builds the clock-swap variant src/finetune_cl_our_qwen_clock.py with make_clock_variant.py (unchanged);
17: #  5. checks every resulting file against the SHA-256 recorded BEFORE the runs (../runs/forever_repro_sha256.txt).

78: def sha(p):
79:     return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
80: 
81: 
82: def main():
83:     if len(sys.argv) != 2:
84:         sys.exit(__doc__ or "usage: python -I prepare_forever_copy.py <path-to-FOREVER-checkout>")
85:     src_root = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
86:     here = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
87:     bad = [f for f, h in ORIGINAL.items() if not (src_root / f).is_file() or sha(src_root / f) != h]
88:     if bad:
89:         sys.exit(f"not the files of commit {COMMIT}: " + ", ".join(bad))
90:     out = [here / f for f in list(RECORDED) + [f for f in ORIGINAL if f.startswith("data_")]]
91:     clash = [str(p) for p in out if p.exists()]
92:     if clash:
93:         sys.exit("refusing to overwrite existing files: " + ", ".join(clash[:5]))
94:     for f in ORIGINAL:
95:         if f not in ADAPT:
96:             (here / f).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
97:             shutil.copyfile(src_root / f, here / f)
98:     for f, pairs in ADAPT.items():
99:         s = (src_root / f).read_bytes().decode("utf-8")
100:         for old, new in pairs:
101:             assert s.count(old) == 1, (f, old)
102:             s = s.replace(old, new)
103:         (here / f).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
104:         (here / f).write_bytes(s.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\n", "\r\n").encode("utf-8"))
105:     # clock-swap variant, built by the unchanged script that built it on 7 Nov 2026 (it reads and writes in text mode,
106:     # which on Windows gives CRLF; normalise so the result is the same on any system)
107:     import os
108:     cwd = os.getcwd()
109:     os.chdir(here)
110:     try:
111:         runpy.run_path(str(here / "make_clock_variant.py"), run_name="__main__")
112:     finally:
113:         os.chdir(cwd)
114:     clock = here / "src/finetune_cl_our_qwen_clock.py"
115:     clock.write_bytes(clock.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n").replace(b"\n", b"\r\n"))
116:     wrong = [f for f, h in RECORDED.items() if sha(here / f) != h]
117:     if wrong:
118:         sys.exit("MISMATCH with the hashes recorded before the runs: " + ", ".join(wrong))
119:     print(f"ok: {len(RECORDED)} code files identical to the recorded working copy; "
120:           f"{sum(f.startswith('data_') for f in ORIGINAL)} data files copied unchanged")
121: 
122: 
123: if __name__ == "__main__":
124:     main()

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\r2_reconstruction.py
1: # Secondary analysis of the preregistration (preinregistrare_FOREVER_repro_2026-11-07.md), fixed 8 Nov 2026 ~02:00,
2: # BEFORE the R2 drift logs exist beyond its first steps:
3: #   "in R2, R^2 (OLS) of the rate of ||dtheta|| over 8-step windows, from the increase of the eta*|m_hat| clock
4: #    in the same window; reported (our benchmark: >= 0.90)".
5: # Data: ckpt_eta_m/<model>/<k>-<task>/drift_log.csv, columns step, delta_t (=||dtheta|| of the step, their code),
6: #   tau (= the eta*|m_hat| clock, cumulative), tau_true (= cumulative ||dtheta||).
7: # Windows: consecutive, non-overlapping blocks of 8 rows within each task; the incomplete last block is dropped.
8: #   y = sum of delta_t in the block; x = increase of tau over the block (tau[last] - tau[before first]).
9: # OLS with intercept; R^2 = 1 - SSres/SStot. HEADLINE: pooled over the 4 tasks; per-task R^2 reported as detail.
10: import csv, json, sys
11: from pathlib import Path
12: import numpy as np
13: 
14: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
15: root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "ckpt_eta_m" / "Qwen_Qwen3-0.6Blora_cl_dataset_id_4"
16: tasks = ["0-dbpedia", "1-amazon", "2-yahoo", "3-agnews"]
17: 
18: 
19: def r2(x, y):
20:     X = np.column_stack([np.ones_like(x), x])
21:     beta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(X, y, rcond=None)
22:     res = y - X @ beta
23:     sst = ((y - y.mean()) ** 2).sum()
24:     return float(1 - (res ** 2).sum() / sst), beta.tolist()
25: 
26: 
27: out, X_all, Y_all = {}, [], []
28: for t in tasks:
29:     p = root / t / "drift_log.csv"
30:     if not p.exists():
31:         print(f"{t}: drift_log missing"); continue
32:     rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(p, encoding="utf-8")))
33:     d = np.array([float(r["delta_t"]) for r in rows])
34:     tau = np.array([float(r["tau"]) for r in rows])
35:     inc = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0.0], tau]))
36:     nb = len(d) // 8
37:     y = d[: nb * 8].reshape(nb, 8).sum(1)
38:     x = inc[: nb * 8].reshape(nb, 8).sum(1)
39:     R2, beta = r2(x, y)
40:     out[t] = {"rows": len(rows), "windows": nb, "R2": R2, "intercept_slope": beta}
41:     print(f"{t}: rows {len(rows)}, windows {nb}, R2 = {R2:.4f}")
42:     X_all.append(x); Y_all.append(y)
43: 
44: if X_all:
45:     R2p, betap = r2(np.concatenate(X_all), np.concatenate(Y_all))
46:     out["pooled"] = {"windows": int(sum(len(x) for x in X_all)), "R2": R2p, "intercept_slope": betap}
47:     print(f"POOLED (headline): windows {out['pooled']['windows']}, R2 = {R2p:.4f}  (benchmark >= 0.90)")
48: json.dump(out, open(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "runs" / "forever_repro_r2_reconstruction.json", "w"), indent=1)

17:     X = np.column_stack([np.ones_like(x), x])
18:     beta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(X, y, rcond=None)

FILE repository\Stadiul0\forever_repro\rep_verdict.py
1: # Preregistered analysis of the repeated runs (preinregistrare_FOREVER_repetari_2026-11-08.md, version 3),
2: # fixed BEFORE any of them ran. Reads runs/forever_repro_eval_<arm>.log (written by eval_repro.sh) and
3: # logs/<arm>_train_t<k>.log (trigger table, descriptive). Exit 10 = preregistered early stop after stage 1.
4: import json, re, sys
5: from pathlib import Path
6: 
7: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
8: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
9: RUNS = HERE.parent / "runs"
10: ARMS = {("tau", "A", 1): "tau", ("eta", "B", 1): "eta_m", ("eta", "A", 1): "eta_m_A1", ("tau", "B", 1): "tau_B1",
11:         ("tau", "A", 2): "tau_A2", ("eta", "A", 2): "eta_m_A2"}
12: PAIRS = [("A", 1), ("B", 1), ("A", 2)]
13: MARGIN = 2.0  # points
14: NOT_ATTR = "NU SE POATE ATRIBUI CEASULUI (datele nu sustin o atribuire stabila a diferentei catre ceas; nu inseamna ca ceasul nu are efect)"
15: 
16: 
17: def parse(arm):
18:     p = RUNS / f"forever_repro_eval_{arm}.log"
19:     if not p.exists():
20:         return None
21:     s = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
22:     acc = {k: 100 * float(v) for k, v in re.findall(r"任务 (\w+) 的 accuracy：([0-9.]+)", s)}
23:     op = re.search(r"average Performance \(mean accuracy\) is ([0-9.]+)", s)
24:     bwt = re.search(r"^BWT is (-?[0-9.]+)", s, re.M)
25:     dl = re.search(r"BWT diff list: \[([^\]]+)\]", s)
26:     if len(acc) != 4 or not op or not bwt or not dl:
27:         sys.exit(f"eval log incomplete: {p}")
28:     return {"dbpedia": acc["dbpedia"], "OP": 100 * float(op.group(1)), "BWT": 100 * float(bwt.group(1)),
29:             "dbpedia_forgetting": 100 * float(dl.group(1).split(",")[0]), "per_task": acc}
30: 
31: 
32: def triggers(arm):
33:     """Per task: list of clock-triggered replays (step, threshold, clock value), from the training logs."""
34:     res = {}
35:     for t in (1, 2, 3):
36:         p = HERE / "logs" / f"{arm}_train_t{t}.log"
37:         if not p.exists():
38:             continue
39:         lines = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").replace("\r", "\n").split("\n")
40:         total = None
41:         for l in lines:
42:             m = re.search(r"steps_done\(before\)=\d+/(\d+)", l)
43:             if m:
44:                 total = int(m.group(1)); break
45:         trig, pending = [], None
46:         for l in lines:
47:             m = re.search(r"MEMORY REPLAY \(immediate\), trigger_threshold=([0-9.eE+-]+), tau=([0-9.eE+-]+)", l)
48:             if m:
49:                 if pending is not None:
50:                     trig.append(pending)
51:                 pending = {"threshold": float(m.group(1)), "clock": float(m.group(2)), "step": None}
52:                 continue
53:             m = re.search(r"CONTINUE CURRENT, remaining_steps=(\d+)", l)
54:             if m and pending is not None and total is not None:
55:                 pending["step"] = total - int(m.group(1)); trig.append(pending); pending = None
56:         if pending is not None:
57:             pending["step"] = total; trig.append(pending)
58:         res[t] = trig
59:     return res
60: 
61: 
62: R = {k: parse(a) for k, a in ARMS.items()}
63: T = {a: triggers(a) for a in ARMS.values()}
64: 
65: 
66: def diff(metric, a, r):
67:     t, e = R[("tau", a, r)], R[("eta", a, r)]
68:     return None if t is None or e is None else t[metric] - e[metric]
69: 
70: 
71: out = {"arms": {ARMS[k]: v for k, v in R.items() if v is not None}}
72: D = {f"{a}{r}": diff("dbpedia", a, r) for a, r in PAIRS}
73: out["D_dbpedia_tau_minus_eta"] = D
74: for m in ("OP", "BWT", "dbpedia_forgetting"):
75:     out[f"paired_{m}_tau_minus_eta"] = {f"{a}{r}": diff(m, a, r) for a, r in PAIRS}
76: out["triggers"] = {a: v for a, v in T.items() if v}
77: out["trigger_step_diff_eta_minus_tau"] = {}
78: for a, r in PAIRS:
79:     ta, ea = T[ARMS[("tau", a, r)]], T[ARMS[("eta", a, r)]]
80:     if ta and ea:
81:         out["trigger_step_diff_eta_minus_tau"][f"{a}{r}"] = {
82:             t: [e["step"] - x["step"] for x, e in zip(ta.get(t, []), ea.get(t, []))] for t in (1, 2, 3)}
83: if R[("tau", "B", 1)] and R[("eta", "A", 1)]:
84:     out["adapter_effect_A_minus_B"] = {"tau": R[("tau", "A", 1)]["dbpedia"] - R[("tau", "B", 1)]["dbpedia"],
85:                                        "eta": R[("eta", "A", 1)]["dbpedia"] - R[("eta", "B", 1)]["dbpedia"]}
86: for c in ("tau", "eta"):
87:     x1, x2 = R[(c, "A", 1)], R[(c, "A", 2)]
88:     if x1 and x2:
89:         out[f"N_{c}"] = abs(x1["dbpedia"] - x2["dbpedia"])
90: code = 0  # version 3: stage 1 alone gives the verdict; stage 2 never starts automatically
91: SCOPE = " (asociat ceasului, nu dovada cauzala; samanta 42, ordinea 1 din articol)"
92: if D["A1"] is None or D["B1"] is None:
93:     verdict = "etapa 1 incompleta: fara verdict"
94: else:
95:     a, b = D["A1"], D["B1"]
96:     if a > MARGIN and b > MARGIN:
97:         verdict = "DEFICIT CONSECVENT PE DBPEDIA SUB eta_m pe ambele adaptoare" + SCOPE
98:     elif a < -MARGIN and b < -MARGIN:
99:         verdict = "AVANTAJ CONSECVENT PE DBPEDIA SUB eta_m pe ambele adaptoare" + SCOPE
100:     elif a * b < 0:
101:         verdict = "NU SE REPRODUCE ROBUST: datele nu sustin o atribuire stabila catre ceas (nu inseamna ca ceasul nu are efect)"
102:     elif abs(a) <= MARGIN and abs(b) <= MARGIN:
103:         verdict = "DEFICITUL DE 5,3 NU SE REPRODUCE din puncte de start comune"
104:     else:
105:         verdict = "NECONCLUDENT"
106: if D["A1"] is not None and D["B1"] is not None and D["A2"] is not None:
107:     # stage 2 (only if decided separately): descriptive; N is a lower bound on noise; v2 rule with prudent labels
108:     Dv = [D["A1"], D["B1"], D["A2"]]
109:     mean = sum(Dv) / 3
110:     N = max(out["N_tau"], out["N_eta"])
111:     out.update({"mean_D": mean, "min_D": min(Dv), "max_D": max(Dv), "noise_N_lower_bound": N})
112:     if all(d > MARGIN for d in Dv) and mean > N:
113:         s2 = "deficit repetabil asociat ceasului (tau mai bun pe dbpedia)"
114:     elif all(d < -MARGIN for d in Dv) and -mean > N:
115:         s2 = "avantaj repetabil asociat ceasului (eta_m mai bun pe dbpedia)"
116:     elif not (all(d > 0 for d in Dv) or all(d < 0 for d in Dv)) or abs(mean) <= MARGIN or abs(mean) <= N:
117:         s2 = NOT_ATTR
118:     else:
119:         s2 = "NEDECIS"
120:     out["stage2_descriptive"] = s2
121:     for m in ("OP", "BWT"):
122:         vals = list(out[f"paired_{m}_tau_minus_eta"].values())
123:         mm = sum(vals) / len(vals)
124:         out[f"mean_paired_{m}"] = mm
125:         out[f"mean_paired_{m}_within_2"] = abs(mm) <= MARGIN
126: out["verdict"] = verdict
127: print(json.dumps(out, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
128: json.dump(out, open(RUNS / "forever_repetari_verdict.json", "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False)
129: sys.exit(code)

FILE repository\Stadiul0\p2\agent.py
1: """Agent of Pilot 2 (protocol v0.3, section 3): a small pre-LN decoder-only Transformer whose
2: parameters live in one flat float32 buffer, updated by an explicit AdamW (or SGD) step so that the
3: pre-decay update, the decay term and the optimizer moments are available exactly at every step.
4: """
5: import math
6: 
7: import torch
8: import torch.nn as nn
9: import torch.nn.functional as F
10: 
11: 
12: class Block(nn.Module):
13:     def __init__(self, d, h, dff):
14:         super().__init__()
15:         self.h = h
16:         self.ln1 = nn.LayerNorm(d)
17:         self.qkv = nn.Linear(d, 3 * d)
18:         self.proj = nn.Linear(d, d)
19:         self.ln2 = nn.LayerNorm(d)
20:         self.fc1 = nn.Linear(d, dff)
21:         self.fc2 = nn.Linear(dff, d)
22: 
23:     def forward(self, x, mask):
24:         B, T, D = x.shape
25:         q, k, v = self.qkv(self.ln1(x)).split(D, dim=-1)
26:         dh = D // self.h
27:         q = q.view(B, T, self.h, dh).transpose(1, 2)
28:         k = k.view(B, T, self.h, dh).transpose(1, 2)
29:         v = v.view(B, T, self.h, dh).transpose(1, 2)
30:         att = (q @ k.transpose(-2, -1)) * (1.0 / math.sqrt(dh))
31:         att = att.masked_fill(~mask[:T, :T], float("-inf")).softmax(dim=-1)
32:         y = (att @ v).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, T, D)
33:         x = x + self.proj(y)
34:         return x + self.fc2(F.gelu(self.fc1(self.ln2(x))))
35: 
36: 
37: class TinyGPT(nn.Module):
38:     def __init__(self, a):
39:         super().__init__()
40:         d = a["d_model"]
41:         self.tok = nn.Embedding(a["vocab"], d)
42:         self.pos = nn.Embedding(a["max_ctx"], d)
43:         self.blocks = nn.ModuleList([Block(d, a["n_head"], a["d_ff"]) for _ in range(a["n_layer"])])
44:         self.lnf = nn.LayerNorm(d)
45:         self.head = nn.Linear(d, a["vocab"])            # untied from self.tok
46:         self.register_buffer("mask", torch.tril(torch.ones(a["max_ctx"], a["max_ctx"], dtype=torch.bool)),
47:                              persistent=False)
48:         for m in self.modules():
49:             if isinstance(m, (nn.Linear, nn.Embedding)):
50:                 nn.init.normal_(m.weight, 0.0, a["init_std"])
51:                 if isinstance(m, nn.Linear):
52:                     nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)
53: 
54:     def forward(self, idx, reps=False):
55:         """Logits at the answer position (the last input token); optionally the final-LN representation."""
56:         T = idx.shape[1]
57:         x = self.tok(idx) + self.pos.weight[:T]
58:         for b in self.blocks:
59:             x = b(x, self.mask)
60:         h = self.lnf(x[:, -1])
61:         out = self.head(h)
62:         return (out, h) if reps else out
63: 
64: 
65: class FlatAgent:
66:     """Model plus flat parameter buffer and optimizer state.
67: 
68:     Layout of the flat buffer: decayed matrices first (all Linear weights and the head weight, plus both
69:     embeddings when optim.wd_embeddings is true), then the non-decayed matrices (the two embeddings when
70:     optim.wd_embeddings is false; Deep Review D3, 6 Nov 2026), then biases and LayerNorm parameters.
71:     `layers` lists the slices of all matrices (decayed first): they are the l of D1_rel, as before.
72:     theta_dec_norm is the norm over the decayed matrices only (the ones weight decay acts on).
73:     """
74: 
75:     EMBEDDINGS = ("tok.weight", "pos.weight")
76: 
77:     def __init__(self, acfg, ocfg):
78:         self.model = TinyGPT(acfg)
79:         named = list(self.model.named_parameters())
80:         wd_emb = ocfg.get("wd_embeddings", True)
81:         decayed = [(n, p) for n, p in named if p.dim() == 2 and (wd_emb or n not in self.EMBEDDINGS)]
82:         mats_nd = [(n, p) for n, p in named if p.dim() == 2 and not (wd_emb or n not in self.EMBEDDINGS)]
83:         other = [(n, p) for n, p in named if p.dim() != 2]
84:         self.order = decayed + mats_nd + other
85:         self.decayed_names = [n for n, _ in decayed]
86:         self.n_dec_layers = len(decayed)
87:         self.P = sum(p.numel() for _, p in named)
88:         self.Pd = sum(p.numel() for _, p in decayed)
89:         self.P2d = self.Pd + sum(p.numel() for _, p in mats_nd)
90:         self.flat = torch.zeros(self.P)
91:         self.layers, off = [], 0
92:         for n, p in self.order:
93:             k = p.numel()
94:             self.flat[off: off + k].copy_(p.data.reshape(-1))
95:             p.data = self.flat[off: off + k].view_as(p)
96:             if p.dim() == 2:
97:                 self.layers.append((n, off, off + k))
98:             off += k
99:         assert off == self.P
100:         self.params = [p for _, p in self.order]
101:         self.module_params = list(self.model.parameters())
102:         # Gradients live in one flat buffer too: autograd accumulates into an existing .grad in place,
103:         # so zeroing the buffer replaces set-to-None and no parameter-sized tensor is allocated per step.
104:         self.gflat = torch.zeros(self.P)
105:         off = 0
106:         for _, p in self.order:
107:             k = p.numel()
108:             p.grad = self.gflat[off: off + k].view_as(p)
109:             off += k
110:         self._gptr = [p.grad.data_ptr() for p in self.params]
111:         self.o = ocfg
112:         self.kind = ocfg["kind"]
113:         self.m = torch.zeros(self.P)
114:         self.v = torch.zeros(self.P)
115:         # persistent work buffers (Windows returns large allocations to the OS; per-step allocation
116:         # of parameter-sized temporaries does not scale across parallel workers)
117:         self.denom = torch.zeros(self.P)
118:         self.dpre = torch.zeros(self.P)
119:         self.decay = torch.zeros(self.Pd)
120:         self.tmp = torch.zeros(self.P)
121:         self.t = 0                        # committed optimizer steps since the first childhood step
122: 
123:     # ---- quantities derived from the optimizer state ----
124:     def vhat(self):
125:         if self.t == 0:
126:             return None
127:         b2 = self.o["betas"][1] if self.kind == "adamw" else self.o["passive_v_beta"]
128:         return self.v / (1.0 - b2 ** self.t)
129: 
130:     def trace_vhat(self):
131:         if self.t == 0:
132:             return float("nan")
133:         b2 = self.o["betas"][1] if self.kind == "adamw" else self.o["passive_v_beta"]
134:         return float(self.v.sum(dtype=torch.float64)) / (1.0 - b2 ** self.t)
135: 
136:     def mhat(self):
137:         if self.kind == "sgd":
138:             return self.m
139:         return self.m / (1.0 - self.o["betas"][0] ** self.t)
140: 
141:     def layer_sq(self, vec):
142:         """Per-layer squared norms of `vec` over the decayed matrices (float64 tensor).
143: 
144:         Each slice is reduced with a float32 dot product (relative error ~1e-6), then promoted.
145:         """
146:         return torch.stack([torch.dot(vec[a:b], vec[a:b]) for _, a, b in self.layers]).double()
147: 
148:     # ---- one committed optimizer step ----
149:     def step(self, x, y, lr, vhat_lag=None):
150:         """Forward, backward, clip, update. Returns (stats tensor, dpre, decay).
151: 
152:         Clipping is torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_ itself, and the moment and parameter updates use
153:         the operation order of torch.optim.AdamW (single-tensor path), so that the update is
154:         bit-compatible with it (checked in checks_stage0.py). Logged norms use float32 dot products.
155:         """
156:         o = self.o
157:         logits = self.model(x)
158:         loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, y)
159:         self.gflat.zero_()
160:         loss.backward()
161:         if self.t < 8:                                                 # gradients must stay in the flat buffer
162:             assert [p.grad.data_ptr() for p in self.params] == self._gptr, "autograd replaced a .grad tensor"
163:         # raw norm; grads clipped in place. Module order, as torch would see model.parameters():
164:         # the total norm is a sum over parameters, so the order changes its last bits.
165:         gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.module_params, o["clip"])
166:         coef = torch.clamp(o["clip"] / (gn + 1e-6), max=1.0)            # the coefficient torch applied
167:         gt = self.gflat                                                # clipped gradient g~ (a view of .grad)
168:         self.t += 1
169:         wd = o["weight_decay"]
170:         theta = self.flat
171:         Pd = self.Pd
172:         dpre, denom, tmp = self.dpre, self.denom, self.tmp
173:         if self.kind == "adamw":
174:             b1, b2 = o["betas"]
175:             self.m.lerp_(gt, 1 - b1)                                  # as torch Adam(W): m + (1-b1)(g - m)
176:             self.v.mul_(b2).addcmul_(gt, gt, value=1 - b2)
177:             bc1, bc2 = 1 - b1 ** self.t, 1 - b2 ** self.t
178:             torch.sqrt(self.v, out=denom)
179:             denom.div_(bc2 ** 0.5).add_(o["eps"])                      # as torch: (v.sqrt() / bc2_sqrt) + eps
180:             torch.mul(self.m, -lr / bc1, out=dpre)
181:             dpre.div_(denom)                                           # as torch addcdiv: (value * m) / denom
182:             mhat_sq = torch.dot(self.m, self.m) / (bc1 * bc1)
183:             trv = self.v.sum() / bc2
184:         else:
185:             mu, bv = o["sgd_momentum"], o["passive_v_beta"]
186:             self.m.mul_(mu).add_(gt)
187:             self.v.mul_(bv).addcmul_(gt, gt, value=1 - bv)
188:             torch.mul(self.m, -lr, out=dpre)
189:             mhat_sq = torch.dot(self.m, self.m)
190:             trv = self.v.sum() / (1 - bv ** self.t)
191:         decay = torch.mul(theta[:Pd], lr * wd, out=self.decay)     # decoupled weight-decay term (subtracted)
192:         lay_theta = self.layer_sq(theta)                           # ||theta_l(tau)||^2, before the update
193:         lay_dpre = self.layer_sq(dpre)
194:         nd = dpre[self.P2d:]                                       # biases and LayerNorm parameters
195:         nd_sq = torch.dot(nd, nd).double()
196:         d1_rel = torch.sqrt((lay_dpre / (lay_theta + 1e-12)).sum())
197:         full_d = torch.sub(dpre[:Pd], decay, out=tmp[:Pd])
198:         if self.P2d == Pd:                                         # every matrix decayed: unchanged arithmetic
199:             rest_sq = nd_sq
200:         else:                                                      # non-decayed matrices move by dpre only
201:             rest = dpre[Pd:]
202:             rest_sq = torch.dot(rest, rest).double()
203:         dfull_sq = torch.dot(full_d, full_d).double() + rest_sq
204:         if vhat_lag is not None:
205:             torch.mul(dpre, dpre, out=tmp)
206:             d2 = torch.sqrt(torch.dot(vhat_lag, tmp).double())
207:         else:
208:             d2 = torch.tensor(float("nan"), dtype=torch.float64)
209:         gdot = torch.dot(gt, dpre).abs().double()
210:         # apply: theta <- theta (1 - lr wd) on decayed matrices, then + dpre (torch AdamW order)
211:         theta[:Pd].mul_(1 - lr * wd)
212:         theta.add_(dpre)
213:         stats = torch.stack([
214:             loss.detach().double(), gn.double(), (gn * coef).double(), torch.sqrt(lay_dpre.sum() + nd_sq),
215:             torch.sqrt(dfull_sq), torch.sqrt(mhat_sq.double()), gdot, torch.sqrt(torch.dot(theta, theta).double()),
216:             torch.sqrt(lay_theta[: self.n_dec_layers].sum()), trv.double(), d1_rel, d2,
217:         ])
218:         return stats, dpre, decay
219: 
220: 
221: STEP_FIELDS = ["loss", "gnorm_raw", "gnorm_clip", "dpre_norm", "D1", "mhat_norm", "gdot",
222:                "theta_norm", "theta_dec_norm", "trace_vhat", "D1_rel", "D2"]

FILE repository\Stadiul0\p2\metrics.py
1: """Distances on outputs and representations (protocol v0.3, section 5)."""
2: import math
3: 
4: import numpy as np
5: import torch
6: 
7: LN2 = math.log(2.0)
8: 
9: 
10: def sqrt_js(P, Q):
11:     """Jensen-Shannon distance, base 2, row-wise, in [0, 1]. P, Q: [n, V] probabilities."""
12:     P = P.double()
13:     Q = Q.double()
14:     M = 0.5 * (P + Q)
15:     xl = torch.special.xlogy
16:     js = 0.5 * ((xl(P, P) - xl(P, M)).sum(-1) + (xl(Q, Q) - xl(Q, M)).sum(-1)) / LN2
17:     return js.clamp(min=0.0).sqrt()
18: 
19: 
20: def cka_distance(X, Y):
21:     """sqrt(1 - CKA) with standard (biased) centred linear CKA; X, Y: [n, d]."""
22:     X = X.double() - X.double().mean(0, keepdim=True)
23:     Y = Y.double() - Y.double().mean(0, keepdim=True)
24:     hsic = (Y.T @ X).pow(2).sum()
25:     nx = (X.T @ X).pow(2).sum().sqrt()
26:     ny = (Y.T @ Y).pow(2).sum().sqrt()
27:     cka = hsic / (nx * ny)
28:     return float(torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(1.0 - cka, min=0.0)))
29: 
30: 
31: def sqrt_js_np(p, q):
32:     """Jensen-Shannon distance, base 2, between two 1-D distributions (numpy)."""
33:     p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
34:     q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
35:     m = 0.5 * (p + q)
36:     with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
37:         a = np.where(p > 0, p * (np.log2(p) - np.log2(m)), 0.0).sum()
38:         b = np.where(q > 0, q * (np.log2(q) - np.log2(m)), 0.0).sum()
39:     return float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, 0.5 * (a + b))))

FILE repository\Stadiul0\p2\run.py
1: """One Pilot 2 run for Stage 0 (protocol v0.3): childhood, life, evaluations, distances and logs.
2: 
3: Usage (from the Stadiul0 folder):
4:     python -m p2.run --family F1 --seed 1 [--out runs/F1_s1] [--set run.life_steps_per_task=512 ...]
5: 
6: The run writes per-step logs (section 3), per-chunk distances (section 5.1), probe evaluations
7: (sections 4.2, 4.3), per-episode displacements and drift (sections 5.2, 6) and the online
8: covariates that cannot be recomputed afterwards (section 7). Triplets, competitors and models are
9: built offline from these files.
10: """
11: import os
12: 
13: for _v in ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS"):
14:     os.environ[_v] = "1"
