35: from .world import VOCAB, World
36: 
37: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
38: EVAL_KINDS = ["life_start", "grid", "seg_end", "seg_end+grid", "idle_grid", "idle_end"]
39: SEG_KINDS = ["new", "reexp", "reh", "distractor", "idle"]
40: DISP_FIELDS = ["D1", "D1_rel", "D2", "D3", "D4", "T2"]
41: LN64 = float(np.log(64.0))                                 # clip level of F^clip (64 value tokens: chance loss)
42: 
43: 
44: def _peak_rss_mb():
45:     if platform.system() != "Windows":
46:         import resource
47:         return resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss / 1024
48:     class PMC(ctypes.Structure):
49:         _fields_ = [("cb", ctypes.c_ulong), ("PageFaultCount", ctypes.c_ulong),
50:                     ("PeakWorkingSetSize", ctypes.c_size_t), ("WorkingSetSize", ctypes.c_size_t),
51:                     ("QuotaPeakPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("QuotaPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t),
52:                     ("QuotaPeakNonPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("QuotaNonPagedPoolUsage", ctypes.c_size_t),
53:                     ("PagefileUsage", ctypes.c_size_t), ("PeakPagefileUsage", ctypes.c_size_t)]
54:     c = PMC()
55:     c.cb = ctypes.sizeof(PMC)
56:     k32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
57:     k32.GetCurrentProcess.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
58:     k32.K32GetProcessMemoryInfo.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.POINTER(PMC), ctypes.c_ulong]
59:     k32.K32GetProcessMemoryInfo.restype = ctypes.c_int
60:     if not k32.K32GetProcessMemoryInfo(k32.GetCurrentProcess(), ctypes.byref(c), c.cb):
61:         return float("nan")
62:     return c.PeakWorkingSetSize / 2 ** 20
63: 
64: 
65: def _set_path(cfg, dotted, value):
66:     keys = dotted.split(".")
67:     d = cfg
68:     for k in keys[:-1]:
69:         d = d[k]
70:     d[keys[-1]] = value
71: 
72: 
73: def load_config(path, overrides=()):
74:     raw = Path(path).read_bytes()
75:     cfg = yaml.safe_load(raw.decode("utf-8"))
76:     for item in overrides:
77:         k, v = item.split("=", 1)
78:         _set_path(cfg, k, yaml.safe_load(v))
79:     return cfg, hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
80: 
81: 
82: def array_hashes(arrays):
83:     return {k: hashlib.sha256(np.ascontiguousarray(arrays[k]).tobytes()).hexdigest() for k in sorted(arrays)}
84: 
85: 
86: class Timer:
87:     def __init__(self):
88:         self.t = {}
89: 
90:     def add(self, key, dt):
91:         self.t[key] = self.t.get(key, 0.0) + dt
92: 
93: 
94: class StrideState:
95:     """Chunked distances at one stride m over [start, end] in life steps (section 5.1)."""
96: 
97:     def __init__(self, m, start, end, P, chunked_param):
98:         self.m, self.start, self.end = m, start, end
99:         self.prev = None
100:         self.rows = []
101:         self.chunked_param = chunked_param
102:         if chunked_param:
103:             self.acc_pre = torch.zeros(P)
104:             self.acc_full = torch.zeros(P)
105:             self.lay_theta0 = None
106:             self.vlag0 = None
107: 
108:     def due(self, N):
109:         return self.start <= N <= self.end and (N - self.start) % self.m == 0
110: 
111: 
112: class Runner:
113:     def __init__(self, cfg, family, seed, out_dir, cfg_sha=""):
114:         torch.set_num_threads(1)
115:         try:
116:             torch.set_num_interop_threads(1)
117:         except RuntimeError:
118:             pass                                     # already set in this process
119:         torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
120:         self.cfg, self.family, self.seed = cfg, family, seed
121:         self.cfg_sha = cfg_sha
122:         self.out = Path(out_dir)
123:         self.fam = fam = cfg["families"][family]
124:         r = cfg["run"]
125:         self.q0, self.grid = r["q0"], r["eval_grid"]
126:         ss = np.random.SeedSequence([int(seed), int(family[1:])])
127:         s_world, s_stream, s_init, s_batch = ss.spawn(4)
128:         self.world = World(cfg["world"], np.random.default_rng(s_world), fam["rule_frac"], fam["pi_overlap"])
129:         n_t = cfg["world"]["n_target"]
130:         self.segs, self.lr_life, self.restarts = build_schedule(
131:             fam, n_t, r["life_steps_per_task"], self.q0, cfg["optim"]["lr"],
132:             np.random.default_rng(s_stream), cfg["world"]["n_distractor"])
133:         self.life_steps = len(self.lr_life)
134:         torch.manual_seed(int(s_init.generate_state(1)[0]))
135:         self.agent = FlatAgent(cfg["agent"], cfg["optim"])
136:         self.rng = np.random.default_rng(s_batch)
137:         self.timer = Timer()
138: 
139:         # probe tensors, [n_target, 256, 4]
140:         W = self.world
141:         self.probe_x = torch.from_numpy(np.stack([t.probe_x for t in W.targets]))
142:         self.probe_y = torch.from_numpy(np.stack([t.probe_y for t in W.targets]))
143:         maskA = np.zeros((n_t, cfg["world"]["items_per_task"]), dtype=bool)
144:         for i, t in enumerate(W.targets):
145:             maskA[i, t.half_a] = True
146:         self.maskA = torch.from_numpy(maskA)
147:         self.R_x, self.R_y = torch.from_numpy(W.R_x), torch.from_numpy(W.R_y)
148:         self.B_x, self.B_y = torch.from_numpy(W.B_x), torch.from_numpy(W.B_y)
149:         self.value_tokens = W.vocab.value
150:         # q_k of every target task (section 7, input overlap), as sparse (ids, probabilities)
151:         self.qk = []
152:         for t in W.targets:
153:             ids, cnt = np.unique(t.train_pairs(), return_counts=True)
154:             self.qk.append((ids, cnt / cnt.sum()))
155:         self.pair_counter = np.zeros(VOCAB * VOCAB, dtype=np.int64)
156: 
157:         # D2 lag (section 5.1)
158:         self.L = r["d2_lag"]
159:         self.lag_mode = r["d2_lag_mode"]
160:         self.d2_block = int(r.get("d2_block", self.q0))   # Deep Review D9: 16 -> lag in [L, L + 15]
161:         self.vring = {}
162:         self.vexact = deque(maxlen=self.L + 1) if self.lag_mode == "both" else None
163: 
164:         self.strides = [StrideState(int(m), int(a), int(self.life_steps if b is None else b),
165:                                     self.agent.P, r["chunked_param_D"]) for m, a, b in r["d3_strides"]]
166:         self.snapD = set(r["snapshot_D"])
167:         self.dbuf = torch.zeros(self.agent.P)          # work buffers for displacements
168:         self.dbuf2 = torch.zeros(self.agent.P)
169: 
170:         # logs
171:         self.N, self.ca = 0, 0
172:         self.learned = set()
173:         self.active = {}                 # task -> episode id with an open snapshot
174:         self.episodes = []               # dicts
175:         self.snap = {}                   # episode id -> snapshot tensors
176:         self.step_stats, self.step_aux, self.step_d2exact = [], [], []
177:         self.ev = {k: [] for k in ["N", "ca", "kind", "seg", "theta_norm", "sqrt_trv", "L_R"]}
178:         self.probe_rows = []             # (e, k, LA, LB, AA, AB)
179:         self.disp_rows = []              # (ep, e, D1, D1_rel, D2, D3, D4, T2)
180:         self.last = None
181:         self.childhood = {}
182: 
183:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ forward passes
184:     @torch.no_grad()
185:     def forward_items(self, X, Y, probs=False, reps=False, chunk=None):
186:         chunk = chunk or self.cfg["run"]["probe_chunk"]
187:         ce, cor, P, H = [], [], [], []
188:         for i in range(0, len(X), chunk):
189:             logits, h = self.agent.model(X[i: i + chunk], reps=True)
190:             y = Y[i: i + chunk]
191:             ce.append(F.cross_entropy(logits, y, reduction="none"))
192:             cor.append(logits.argmax(-1) == y)
193:             if probs:
194:                 P.append(logits.softmax(-1))
195:             if reps:
196:                 H.append(h)
197:         out = {"ce": torch.cat(ce), "correct": torch.cat(cor)}
198:         if probs:
199:             out["probs"] = torch.cat(P)
200:         if reps:
201:             out["reps"] = torch.cat(H)
202:         return out
203: 
204:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ batches
205:     def batches(self, X, Y, steps, B, label_noise=0.0):
206:         n = len(X)
207:         assert (steps * B) % n == 0, "segment must end at the end of an epoch"
208:         per_epoch = n // B
209:         counts = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
210:         for s in range(steps):
211:             if s % per_epoch == 0:
212:                 perm = self.rng.permutation(n)
213:             idx = perm[(s % per_epoch) * B: (s % per_epoch + 1) * B]
214:             counts[idx] += 1
215:             y = Y[idx].copy()
216:             if label_noise > 0:
217:                 flip = self.rng.random(B) < label_noise
218:                 y[flip] = self.value_tokens[self.rng.integers(0, 64, size=int(flip.sum()))]
219:             yield X[idx], y
220:         assert np.all(counts == counts[0]), "unbalanced exposure"
221: 
222:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ v_hat lag
223:     def vhat_lag(self):
224:         if self.lag_mode == "none":
225:             return None
226:         blk = self.d2_block
227:         ref = blk * ((self.agent.t - self.L) // blk)
228:         return self.vring.get(ref) if ref > 0 else None
229: 
230:     def after_step_vring(self):
231:         t = self.agent.t
232:         blk = self.d2_block
233:         if t % blk == 0:
234:             self.vring[t] = self.agent.vhat().clone()
235:             for key in [k for k in self.vring if k < t - self.L - blk]:
236:                 del self.vring[key]
237:         if self.vexact is not None:
238:             self.vexact.append(self.agent.vhat().clone())
239: 
240:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ childhood
241:     def childhood_phase(self):
242:         r = self.cfg["run"]
243:         X, Y = self.world.childhood_x, self.world.childhood_y
244:         losses = []
245:         t0 = time.perf_counter()
246:         lr = self.cfg["optim"]["lr"]
247:         for x, y in self.batches(X, Y, r["childhood_steps"], r["childhood_batch"]):
248:             stats, _, _ = self.agent.step(torch.from_numpy(x), torch.from_numpy(y), lr, None)
249:             losses.append(float(stats[0]))
250:             self.after_step_vring()
251:         self.timer.add("childhood", time.perf_counter() - t0)
252:         ref_all = self.forward_items(torch.from_numpy(X), torch.from_numpy(Y))
253:         accR = self.forward_items(self.R_x, self.R_y)["correct"].double().mean().item()
254:         accB = self.forward_items(self.B_x, self.B_y)["correct"].double().mean().item()
255:         acc = ref_all["correct"].double().mean().item()
256:         kinds = np.array([t.kind for t in self.world.refs for _ in range(len(t.train_x))])
257:         cor = ref_all["correct"].numpy()
258:         self.childhood = {
259:             "steps": r["childhood_steps"], "acc_reference": acc, "acc_R": accR, "acc_B_beh": accB,
260:             "acc_reference_fact": float(cor[kinds == "fact"].mean()) if (kinds == "fact").any() else None,
261:             "acc_reference_rule": float(cor[kinds == "rule"].mean()) if (kinds == "rule").any() else None,
262:             "gate_passed": bool(acc >= r["childhood_gate"]), "final_loss": float(np.mean(losses[-64:])),
263:         }
264:         self.childhood_loss = np.array(losses)
265: 
266:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ chunked distances
267:     def chunk_tick(self, dpre=None, full=None):
268:         N = self.N
269:         if dpre is not None:
270:             for s in self.strides:
271:                 if s.chunked_param and s.start < N <= s.end:
272:                     s.acc_pre.add_(dpre)
273:                     s.acc_full.add_(full)
274:         due = [s for s in self.strides if s.due(N)]
275:         if not due:
276:             return
277:         t0 = time.perf_counter()
278:         o = self.forward_items(self.R_x, self.R_y, probs=True, reps=True)
279:         ceR = o["ce"].double()
280:         LR = ceR.mean().item()
281:         pR = torch.exp(-ceR)                                               # target probability per item (T-B)
282:         HR = -torch.special.xlogy(o["probs"].double(), o["probs"].double()).sum(-1)   # output entropy per item, nats
283:         for s in due:
284:             if s.prev is not None and N > s.start:
285:                 # C10b: |change of the mean loss on R|; C10c (Deep Review D5): mean over items of |change of the
286:                 # per-item loss on R|, from the same forward pass as D3; C10p, C10h (colloquium 6 Nov 2026, test T-B):
287:                 # mean |change of the target probability| and mean |change of the output entropy|, same pass
288:                 row = [N, sqrt_js(s.prev["probs"], o["probs"]).mean().item(),
289:                        cka_distance(s.prev["reps"], o["reps"]), abs(LR - s.prev["L"]),
290:                        (ceR - s.prev["ce"]).abs().mean().item(),
291:                        (pR - s.prev["p"]).abs().mean().item(), (HR - s.prev["H"]).abs().mean().item()]
292:                 if s.chunked_param:
293:                     a = self.agent
294:                     d1c = s.acc_full.double().norm().item()
295:                     d1r = torch.sqrt((a.layer_sq(s.acc_pre) / (s.lay_theta0 + 1e-12)).sum()).item()
296:                     d2c = (torch.sqrt((s.vlag0.double() * s.acc_pre.double().pow(2)).sum()).item()
297:                            if s.vlag0 is not None else float("nan"))
298:                     row += [d1c, d1r, d2c]
299:                 s.rows.append(row)
300:             s.prev = {"probs": o["probs"], "reps": o["reps"], "L": LR, "ce": ceR, "p": pR, "H": HR}
301:             if s.chunked_param:
302:                 s.acc_pre.zero_()
303:                 s.acc_full.zero_()
304:                 s.lay_theta0 = self.agent.layer_sq(self.agent.flat)
305:                 s.vlag0 = self.vhat_lag()
306:         self.timer.add("chunks", time.perf_counter() - t0)
307: 
308:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ evaluations
309:     def eval_point(self, kind, seg_index=-1, reuse=False):
310:         t0 = time.perf_counter()
311:         if not reuse:
312:             learned = sorted(self.learned)
313:             res = {"learned": learned}
314:             if learned:
315:                 idx = torch.tensor(learned)
316:                 X = self.probe_x[idx].reshape(-1, 4)
317:                 Y = self.probe_y[idx].reshape(-1)
318:                 o = self.forward_items(X, Y)
319:                 ce = o["ce"].view(len(learned), -1).double()
320:                 cor = o["correct"].view(len(learned), -1).double()
321:                 mA = self.maskA[idx].double()
322:                 mB = 1.0 - mA
323:                 res["LA"] = ((ce * mA).sum(1) / mA.sum(1)).numpy()
324:                 res["LB"] = ((ce * mB).sum(1) / mB.sum(1)).numpy()
325:                 res["AA"] = ((cor * mA).sum(1) / mA.sum(1)).numpy()
326:                 res["AB"] = ((cor * mB).sum(1) / mB.sum(1)).numpy()
327:                 cec = ce.clamp(max=LN64)                       # Deep Review A3: loss clipped at ln 64, online
328:                 res["LAc"] = ((cec * mA).sum(1) / mA.sum(1)).numpy()
329:                 res["LBc"] = ((cec * mB).sum(1) / mB.sum(1)).numpy()
330:             oR = self.forward_items(self.R_x, self.R_y, probs=True, reps=True)
331:             oB = self.forward_items(self.B_x, self.B_y, probs=True)
332:             res["R_probs"], res["R_reps"] = oR["probs"], oR["reps"]
333:             res["L_R"] = oR["ce"].double().mean().item()
334:             res["B_probs"] = oB["probs"]
335:             fl = self.agent.flat
336:             res["theta_norm"] = float(torch.dot(fl, fl).double().sqrt())
337:             res["sqrt_trv"] = self.agent.trace_vhat() ** 0.5
338:             self.last = res
339:         res = self.last
340:         e = len(self.ev["N"])
341:         for key, val in [("N", self.N), ("ca", self.ca), ("kind", EVAL_KINDS.index(kind)), ("seg", seg_index),
342:                          ("theta_norm", res["theta_norm"]), ("sqrt_trv", res["sqrt_trv"]), ("L_R", res["L_R"])]:
343:             self.ev[key].append(val)
344:         for j, k in enumerate(res["learned"]):
345:             self.probe_rows.append((e, k, res["LA"][j], res["LB"][j], res["AA"][j], res["AB"][j],
346:                                     res["LAc"][j], res["LBc"][j]))
347:         self.timer.add("eval", time.perf_counter() - t0)
348:         t1 = time.perf_counter()
349:         for k, ep in self.active.items():
350:             self.disp_rows.append([ep, e] + self.displacement(self.snap[ep], res))
351:         self.timer.add("disp", time.perf_counter() - t1)
352:         return e
353: 
354:     @torch.no_grad()
355:     def displacement(self, s, res):
356:         a = self.agent
357:         out = []
358:         diff = None
359:         if "theta" in s:                       # float32 difference into a persistent buffer; dots in float32
360:             diff = torch.sub(a.flat, s["theta"], out=self.dbuf)
361:         out.append(float(torch.dot(diff, diff).double().sqrt()) if diff is not None and "D1" in self.snapD
362:                    else float("nan"))
363:         if diff is not None and "D1_rel" in self.snapD:
364:             out.append(torch.sqrt((a.layer_sq(diff) / (s["lay_theta"] + 1e-12)).sum()).item())
365:         else:
366:             out.append(float("nan"))
367:         if diff is not None and "vhat" in s:
368:             torch.mul(diff, diff, out=self.dbuf2)
369:             out.append(float(torch.dot(s["vhat"], self.dbuf2).double().sqrt()))
370:         else:
371:             out.append(float("nan"))
372:         out.append(sqrt_js(s["R_probs"], res["R_probs"]).mean().item() if "R_probs" in s else float("nan"))
373:         out.append(cka_distance(s["R_reps"], res["R_reps"]) if "R_reps" in s else float("nan"))
374:         out.append(sqrt_js(s["B_probs"], res["B_probs"]).mean().item() if "B_probs" in s else float("nan"))
375:         return out
376: 
377:     def eval_task_B(self, k):
378:         t = self.world.targets[k]
379:         X = torch.from_numpy(t.probe_x[t.half_b])
380:         Y = torch.from_numpy(t.probe_y[t.half_b])
381:         o = self.forward_items(X, Y)
382:         return o["ce"].double().mean().item(), o["correct"].double().mean().item()
383: 
384:     def overlap(self, k):
385:         tot = self.pair_counter.sum()
386:         if tot == 0:
387:             return float("nan")
388:         ids, p = self.qk[k]
389:         support = np.union1d(ids, np.flatnonzero(self.pair_counter))
390:         qh = self.pair_counter[support] / tot
391:         pk = np.zeros(len(support))
392:         pk[np.searchsorted(support, ids)] = p
393:         return 1.0 - sqrt_js_np(pk, qh)
394: 
395:     def open_episode(self, k, seg_index, seg_kind, prior, e, overlap):
396:         # overlap = 1 - sqrt(JS)(q_k, q_hist) with q_hist taken at the START of segment s0, before its own
397:         # steps are counted (Deep Review D4: it used to include the segment itself)
398:         ep = len(self.episodes)
399:         self.episodes.append({"task": k, "seg": seg_index, "kind": SEG_KINDS.index(seg_kind), "s0": self.N,
400:                               "t0": e, "prior_LB": prior[0], "prior_AB": prior[1], "overlap": overlap})
401:         a, res, s = self.agent, self.last, {}
402:         if self.snapD & {"D1", "D1_rel", "D2"}:
403:             s["theta"] = a.flat.clone()
404:             s["lay_theta"] = a.layer_sq(a.flat)
405:         if "D2" in self.snapD:
406:             s["vhat"] = a.vhat().clone()
407:         if "D3" in self.snapD:
408:             s["R_probs"] = res["R_probs"]
409:         if "D4" in self.snapD:
410:             s["R_reps"] = res["R_reps"]
411:         if "T2" in self.snapD:
412:             s["B_probs"] = res["B_probs"]
413:         self.snap[ep] = s
414:         self.active[k] = ep
415: 
416:     def close_episode(self, k):
417:         ep = self.active.pop(k, None)
418:         if ep is not None:
419:             del self.snap[ep]
420: 
421:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ life
422:     def idle(self, seg, si):
423:         start, end = self.ca, self.ca + seg.ticks
424:         c = (start // self.grid + 1) * self.grid
425:         while c < end:
426:             self.ca = c
427:             self.eval_point("idle_grid", si, reuse=True)
428:             c += self.grid
429:         self.ca = end
430:         self.eval_point("idle_end", si, reuse=True)
431: 
432:     def life_phase(self):
433:         W = self.world
434:         self.eval_point("life_start")
435:         self.chunk_tick()
436:         prev_key = None
437:         for si, seg in enumerate(self.segs):
438:             if seg.kind == "idle":
439:                 self.idle(seg, si)
440:                 continue
441:             task = W.distractors[seg.task] if seg.kind == "distractor" else W.targets[seg.task]
442:             key = (task.token, seg.batch)
443:             boundary = key != prev_key
444:             prev_key = key
445:             exposure = seg.kind in EXPOSURE_KINDS
446:             prior, ov = None, None
447:             if exposure:
448:                 self.close_episode(seg.task)
449:                 t0 = time.perf_counter()
450:                 prior = self.eval_task_B(seg.task)
451:                 ov = self.overlap(seg.task)                    # q_hist before the segment
452:                 self.timer.add("eval", time.perf_counter() - t0)
453:             for j, (x, y) in enumerate(self.batches(task.train_x, task.train_y, seg.steps, seg.batch,
454:                                                     seg.label_noise)):
455:                 t0 = time.perf_counter()
456:                 vlag = self.vhat_lag()
457:                 lr = float(self.lr_life[self.N])
458:                 stats, dpre, decay = self.agent.step(torch.from_numpy(x), torch.from_numpy(y), lr, vlag)
459:                 self.step_stats.append(stats)
460:                 self.step_aux.append((lr, seg.batch, seg.batch * 5, int(boundary and j == 0), si))
461:                 if self.vexact is not None:
462:                     vx = self.vexact[0] if len(self.vexact) == self.L + 1 else None
463:                     self.step_d2exact.append(torch.sqrt((vx.double() * dpre.double().pow(2)).sum()).item()
464:                                              if vx is not None else float("nan"))
465:                 np.add.at(self.pair_counter, x[:, 1] * VOCAB + x[:, 2], 1)
466:                 self.N += 1
467:                 self.ca += 1
468:                 self.after_step_vring()
469:                 self.timer.add("train", time.perf_counter() - t0)
470:                 full = None
471:                 if any(s.chunked_param for s in self.strides):
472:                     full = dpre.clone()
473:                     full[: self.agent.Pd] -= decay
474:                 self.chunk_tick(dpre, full)
475:                 if self.N % self.grid == 0 and j < seg.steps - 1:
476:                     self.eval_point("grid", si)
477:             if exposure:
478:                 self.learned.add(seg.task)
479:             e = self.eval_point("seg_end+grid" if self.N % self.grid == 0 else "seg_end", si)
480:             if exposure:
481:                 self.open_episode(seg.task, si, seg.kind, prior, e, ov)
482:         assert self.N == self.life_steps
483: 
484:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------ output
485:     def save(self, wall):
486:         out = self.out
487:         out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
488:         st = torch.stack(self.step_stats).numpy()
489:         steps = {f: st[:, i] for i, f in enumerate(STEP_FIELDS)}
490:         aux = np.array(self.step_aux, dtype=np.float64)
491:         for i, f in enumerate(["lr", "batch", "tokens", "boundary", "seg"]):
492:             steps[f] = aux[:, i]
493:         if self.step_d2exact:
494:             steps["D2_exact"] = np.array(self.step_d2exact)
495:         steps["childhood_loss"] = self.childhood_loss
496:         n_e, n_t = len(self.ev["N"]), self.cfg["world"]["n_target"]
497:         evals = {k: np.array(v, dtype=np.float64) for k, v in self.ev.items()}
498:         for name in ["LA", "LB", "AA", "AB", "LAc", "LBc"]:
499:             evals[name] = np.full((n_e, n_t), np.nan)
500:         for e, k, la, lb, aa, ab, lac, lbc in self.probe_rows:
501:             evals["LA"][e, k], evals["LB"][e, k], evals["AA"][e, k], evals["AB"][e, k] = la, lb, aa, ab
502:             evals["LAc"][e, k], evals["LBc"][e, k] = lac, lbc
503:         episodes = {k: np.array([ep[k] for ep in self.episodes], dtype=np.float64) for k in self.episodes[0]}
504:         disp = np.array(self.disp_rows, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2 + len(DISP_FIELDS))
505:         disp = {"ep": disp[:, 0], "e": disp[:, 1], **{f: disp[:, 2 + i] for i, f in enumerate(DISP_FIELDS)}}
506:         chunks = {}
507:         for s in self.strides:
508:             rows = np.array(s.rows, dtype=np.float64)
509:             names = ["N", "D3", "D4", "C10b", "C10c", "C10p", "C10h"] + (["D1c", "D1_relc", "D2c"] if s.chunked_param else [])
510:             for i, f in enumerate(names):
511:                 chunks[f"m{s.m}_s{s.start}_{f}"] = rows[:, i] if len(rows) else np.empty(0)
512:         W = self.world
513:         world = {"kinds": np.array([t.kind == "rule" for t in W.targets], dtype=np.int8),
514:                  "n_values": np.array([t.n_values for t in W.targets]),
515:                  "moduli": np.array([t.modulus for t in W.targets]),
516:                  "train_pairs": np.stack([t.train_pairs() for t in W.targets]),
517:                  "key_overlap": W.key_overlap()}
518:         files = {"steps": steps, "evals": evals, "episodes": episodes, "disp": disp, "chunks": chunks,
519:                  "world": world}
520:         hashes = {}
521:         for name, arrays in files.items():
522:             np.savez_compressed(out / f"{name}.npz", **arrays)
523:             hashes[name] = array_hashes(arrays)
524:         (out / "schedule.json").write_text(json.dumps(
525:             {"segments": [s.as_dict() for s in self.segs], "restarts": self.restarts}, indent=0), encoding="utf-8")
526:         meta = {
527:             "family": self.family, "seed": self.seed, "config": self.cfg, "config_sha256": self.cfg_sha,
528:             "config_resolved_sha256": hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in self.cfg.items() if k not in ("analysis", "meta")},
529:                                                                 sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
530:             "protocol": self.cfg["meta"]["protocol"],
531:             "versions": {"python": sys.version.split()[0], "torch": torch.__version__, "numpy": np.__version__,
532:                          "platform": platform.platform()},
533:             "n_params": self.agent.P, "n_params_decayed": self.agent.Pd,
534:             "layers": [[n, b - a] for n, a, b in self.agent.layers],
535:             "childhood": self.childhood, "life_steps": self.life_steps, "ticks_total": self.ca,
536:             "n_evals": n_e, "n_episodes": len(self.episodes), "n_segments": len(self.segs),
537:             "eval_kinds": EVAL_KINDS, "seg_kinds": SEG_KINDS, "disp_fields": DISP_FIELDS,
538:             "timing_s": {**{k: round(v, 2) for k, v in self.timer.t.items()}, "wall": round(wall, 2)},
539:             "peak_rss_mb": round(_peak_rss_mb(), 1), "sha256_arrays": hashes,
540:         }
541:         (out / "meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
542:         return meta
543: 
544:     def run(self):
545:         t0 = time.perf_counter()
546:         self.childhood_phase()
547:         self.life_phase()
548:         return self.save(time.perf_counter() - t0)
549: 
550: 
551: def run_one(config_path, family, seed, out_dir, overrides=()):
552:     cfg, sha = load_config(config_path, overrides)
553:     return Runner(cfg, family, seed, out_dir, sha).run()
554:

557:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[0])
558:     ap.add_argument("--family", required=True)
559:     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
560:     ap.add_argument("--config", default=str(HERE / "config_stage0.yaml"))
561:     ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)

563:     a = ap.parse_args(argv)

565:     meta = run_one(a.config, a.family, a.seed, out, a.set)
566:     print(json.dumps({k: meta[k] for k in ["family", "seed", "n_params", "childhood", "n_evals", "n_episodes",

FILE repository\Stadiul0\p2\streams.py
1: """Stream families F1-F6 (protocol v0.3, sections 4.3-4.5).
2: 
3: A schedule is a list of segments plus a learning rate for every life step. Training segments are
4: multiples of the step quantum q0 = 64; idle intervals are multiples of 64 ticks and sit between
5: segments. All generator parameters come from the family block of the configuration.
6: """
7: from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
8: 
9: import numpy as np
10: 
11: TRAIN_KINDS = ("new", "reexp", "reh", "distractor")
12: EXPOSURE_KINDS = ("new", "reexp", "reh")        # segments that train a target task
13: 
14: 
15: @dataclass
16: class Segment:
17:     kind: str            # new | reexp | reh | distractor | idle
18:     task: int            # target index (new, reexp, reh), distractor index, or -1 (idle)
19:     steps: int = 0       # optimizer steps (training segments)
20:     ticks: int = 0       # idle ticks (idle intervals)
21:     batch: int = 0
22:     label_noise: float = 0.0
23: 
24:     def as_dict(self):
25:         return asdict(self)
26: 
27: 
28: def _split_units(rng, total, lengths):
29:     """Split `total` units into a random sequence of segment lengths drawn from `lengths`."""
30:     out = []
31:     while total > 0:
32:         ok = [L for L in lengths if L <= total]
33:         L = int(rng.choice(ok))
34:         out.append(L)
35:         total -= L
36:     return out
37: 
38: 
39: def _batch(rng, fam):
40:     return int(rng.choice(fam["batch"]))
41: 
42: 
43: def _cycle(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, rng):
44:     """F1, F3, F4: new task, then round-robin rehearsal of the tasks learned before it."""
45:     segs, rr = [], 0
46:     assert fam["new_steps"] + fam["reh_per_cycle"] * fam["reh_steps"] == per_task
47:     for i in range(n_tasks):
48:         segs.append(Segment("new", i, steps=fam["new_steps"], batch=_batch(rng, fam)))
49:         for _ in range(fam["reh_per_cycle"]):
50:             if i == 0:
51:                 k = 0
52:             else:
53:                 k = rr % i
54:                 rr += 1
55:             segs.append(Segment("reh", k, steps=fam["reh_steps"], batch=_batch(rng, fam)))
56:     return segs
57: 
58: 
59: def _novelty(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, rng):
60:     """F2, F5: new tasks interleaved with re-exposure to learned tasks in random amounts."""
61:     units_total = n_tasks * (per_task - fam["new_steps"]) // q0
62:     gaps = rng.multinomial(units_total, np.full(n_tasks, 1.0 / n_tasks))
63:     segs = []
64:     for i in range(n_tasks):
65:         segs.append(Segment("new", i, steps=fam["new_steps"], batch=_batch(rng, fam)))
66:         for L in _split_units(rng, int(gaps[i]), fam["reexp_units"]):
67:             k = int(rng.integers(0, i + 1))
68:             segs.append(Segment("reexp", k, steps=L * q0, batch=_batch(rng, fam)))
69:     return segs
70: 
71: 
72: def _distractor(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, rng, n_distractor):
73:     """F6: regular target exposures, interleaved with variable-length distractor segments."""
74:     target_steps = fam["new_steps"] + fam["reh_per_cycle"] * fam["reh_steps"]
75:     units_total = n_tasks * (per_task - target_steps) // q0
76:     gaps = rng.multinomial(units_total, np.full(n_tasks, 1.0 / n_tasks))
77:     segs, rr = [], 0
78:     for i in range(n_tasks):
79:         segs.append(Segment("new", i, steps=fam["new_steps"], batch=_batch(rng, fam)))
80:         for L in _split_units(rng, int(gaps[i]), fam["distractor_units"]):
81:             d = int(rng.integers(0, n_distractor))
82:             segs.append(Segment("distractor", d, steps=L * q0, batch=_batch(rng, fam),
83:                                 label_noise=fam["label_noise"]))
84:         for _ in range(fam["reh_per_cycle"]):
85:             k = 0 if i == 0 else rr % i
86:             rr += i > 0
87:             segs.append(Segment("reh", k, steps=fam["reh_steps"], batch=_batch(rng, fam)))
88:     return segs
89: 
90: 
91: def _insert_idle(segs, idle, q0, rng):
92:     out = []
93:     for s in segs:
94:         out.append(s)
95:         if rng.random() < idle["p"]:
96:             lo, hi = idle["units"]
97:             out.append(Segment("idle", -1, ticks=int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1)) * q0))
98:     return out
99: 
100: 
101: def _lr_schedule(fam, base_lr, life_steps, q0, rng):
102:     spec = fam["lr"]
103:     if spec["kind"] == "constant":
104:         return np.full(life_steps, base_lr), [0]
105:     assert spec["kind"] == "cosine_restarts"
106:     hi, lo = base_lr * spec["max_mult"], base_lr * spec["min_mult"]
107:     p_lo, p_hi = spec["period"]
108:     lr = np.empty(life_steps)
109:     restarts, start = [], 0
110:     while start < life_steps:
111:         T = int(rng.integers(p_lo // q0, p_hi // q0 + 1)) * q0
112:         restarts.append(start)
113:         n = np.arange(min(T, life_steps - start))
114:         lr[start: start + len(n)] = lo + 0.5 * (hi - lo) * (1 + np.cos(np.pi * n / T))
115:         start += T
116:     return lr, restarts
117: 
118: 
119: def build_schedule(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, base_lr, rng, n_distractor=8):
120:     structure = fam["structure"]
121:     if structure == "cycle":
122:         segs = _cycle(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, rng)
123:     elif structure == "novelty":
124:         segs = _novelty(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, rng)
125:     elif structure == "distractor":
126:         segs = _distractor(fam, n_tasks, per_task, q0, rng, n_distractor)
127:     else:
128:         raise ValueError(structure)
129:     if "idle" in fam:
130:         segs = _insert_idle(segs, fam["idle"], q0, rng)
131:     life_steps = sum(s.steps for s in segs)
132:     assert life_steps == n_tasks * per_task, (life_steps, n_tasks * per_task)
133:     for s in segs:
134:         assert s.steps % q0 == 0 and s.ticks % q0 == 0
135:         assert (s.kind == "idle") == (s.steps == 0)
136:     lr, restarts = _lr_schedule(fam, base_lr, life_steps, q0, rng)
137:     return segs, lr, restarts

FILE repository\Stadiul0\p2\triplets.py
1: """Offline construction of triplets (k, t0, t1), targets, competitors and c_e (protocol v0.3, sections 6-7).
2: 
3: Interval convention: an interval (t0, t1] covers the committed steps tau in (N(t0), N(t1)]. The per-step
4: arrays are indexed by life step i, where step i moves the state from N = i to N = i + 1, so the
5: interval sum is cs[N1] - cs[N0] with cs the cumulative sum starting at 0.
6: 
7: Usage: python -m p2.triplets RUN_DIR [RUN_DIR ...]   (writes triplets.parquet into each run folder)
8: """
9: import json
10: import sys
11: from pathlib import Path
12: 
13: import numpy as np
14: import pandas as pd
15: import yaml
16: 
17: CONFIG = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "config_stage0.yaml"
18: 
19: 
20: def default_max_dN():
21:     """T1 cap on dN from the analysis section of the configuration (None = no cap)."""
22:     cfg = yaml.safe_load(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
23:     return (cfg.get("analysis") or {}).get("t1_max_dN")
24: 
25: 
26: def _cs(x):
27:     return np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))])
28: 
29: 
30: def load_run(run_dir):
31:     d = Path(run_dir)
32:     meta = json.loads((d / "meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
33:     load = lambda name: dict(np.load(d / f"{name}.npz"))
34:     sched = json.loads((d / "schedule.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
35:     return meta, load("steps"), load("evals"), load("episodes"), load("disp"), load("chunks"), load("world"), sched
36: 
37: 
38: def c6_stats(x, N0, N1):
39:     """C6: mean and population SD of x over the first and last W_eff steps of (N0, N1] (section 7)."""
40:     dN = N1 - N0
41:     W = min(16, dN // 4)
42:     if dN == 0 or W == 0:
43:         return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
44:     first = x[N0: N0 + W]
45:     last = x[N1 - W: N1]
46:     return first.mean(), first.std(ddof=0), last.mean(), last.std(ddof=0)
47: 
48: 
49: def build(run_dir, stride_key=None, max_dN="config"):
50:     """Triplets of one run. max_dN: cap on dN for inclusion ('config' = analysis.t1_max_dN; None = no cap).
51:     The uncapped inclusion and weights are kept as incl_nocap and w_nocap (sensitivity analysis)."""
52:     if max_dN == "config":
53:         max_dN = default_max_dN()
54:     meta, st, ev, eps, disp, ch, world, sched = load_run(run_dir)
55:     lam = meta["config"]["optim"]["weight_decay"]
56:     lr, B = st["lr"], st["batch"]
57:     cs = {
58:         "C2": _cs(st["tokens"]), "C3": _cs(lr), "C4": _cs(st["loss"]), "C5": _cs(st["gnorm_raw"]),
59:         "C5a": _cs(st["gnorm_clip"]), "C5b_g": _cs(lr * st["gnorm_raw"]), "C5b_gt": _cs(lr * st["gnorm_clip"]),
60:         "C5b_L": _cs(lr * st["loss"]), "C5c_sq": _cs(lr ** 2 * st["gnorm_clip"] ** 2), "C5c_work": _cs(st["gdot"]),
61:         "C5d": _cs(lr * st["mhat_norm"]), "C5e": _cs(st["mhat_norm"]), "C5f": _cs(lr * lam * st["theta_dec_norm"]),
62:         "C2b": _cs(lr / B), "C7a": _cs(st["boundary"]),
63:         "ce_D1": _cs(st["D1"]), "ce_D1_rel": _cs(st["D1_rel"]), "ce_D2": _cs(np.nan_to_num(st["D2"])),
64:     }
65:     if stride_key is None:
66:         keys = sorted({k.rsplit("_", 1)[0] for k in ch if k.endswith("_N")})
67:         stride_key = next(k for k in keys if k.endswith("_s0"))
68:     cN = ch[f"{stride_key}_N"].astype(np.int64)
69:     life = int(meta["life_steps"])
70:     chunk_arr = {}
71:     chunk_names = ["D3", "D4", "C10b"] + [f for f in ("C10c", "C10p", "C10h") if f"{stride_key}_{f}" in ch]
72:     # C10c: runs after 6 Nov 2026 (Deep Review D5); C10p, C10h: runs after the colloquium of 6 Nov 2026 (test T-B)
73:     for f in chunk_names:
74:         a = np.zeros(life + 1)
75:         a[cN] = ch[f"{stride_key}_{f}"]
76:         chunk_arr[f] = np.cumsum(a)                       # value at N = sum of chunks ending at or before N
77:     evN, evca = ev["N"].astype(np.int64), ev["ca"]
78:     LA, LB, AA, AB = ev["LA"], ev["LB"], ev["AA"], ev["AB"]
79:     kinds = world["kinds"]
80:     n_values = world["n_values"]
81:     ep_task = eps["task"].astype(int)
82:     ep_t0 = eps["t0"].astype(int)
83:     # first-exposure evaluation of each task and exposure counts, for tenure and prior exposures
84:     first_t0 = {}
85:     for i in np.argsort(ep_t0, kind="stable"):
86:         first_t0.setdefault(ep_task[i], ep_t0[i])
87:     rows = []
88:     disp_ep, disp_e = disp["ep"].astype(int), disp["e"].astype(int)
89:     dfields = [f for f in meta["disp_fields"]]
90:     for r in range(len(disp_ep)):
91:         ep, e1 = disp_ep[r], disp_e[r]
92:         k, e0 = ep_task[ep], ep_t0[ep]
93:         N0, N1 = int(evN[e0]), int(evN[e1])
94:         dN = N1 - N0
95:         row = {"ep": ep, "task": k, "e0": e0, "e1": e1, "N0": N0, "N1": N1, "dN": dN,
96:                "C0": evca[e1] - evca[e0] - dN,            # pause ticks (Deep Review D1: was dc_a, identical to dN without pauses)
97:                "F_A": LA[e1, k] - LA[e0, k], "F_B": LB[e1, k] - LB[e0, k],
98:                "dAcc_A": AA[e0, k] - AA[e1, k], "AB0": AB[e0, k]}
99:         for name, c in cs.items():
100:             row[name] = c[N1] - c[N0]
101:         for name in chunk_names:
102:             key = f"ce_{name}" if name not in ("C10b", "C10c", "C10p", "C10h") else name
103:             row[key] = chunk_arr[name][N1] - chunk_arr[name][N0]
104:         row["C10"] = ev["L_R"][e1] - ev["L_R"][e0]
105:         row["C6b_theta"] = ev["theta_norm"][e1] - ev["theta_norm"][e0]
106:         row["C6b_v"] = ev["sqrt_trv"][e1] - ev["sqrt_trv"][e0]
107:         for tag, x in [("pre", st["dpre_norm"]), ("post", st["D1"])]:
108:             m1, s1, m2, s2 = c6_stats(x, N0, N1)
109:             row.update({f"C6_{tag}_m_first": m1, f"C6_{tag}_sd_first": s1,
110:                         f"C6_{tag}_m_last": m2, f"C6_{tag}_sd_last": s2})
111:         for i, f in enumerate(dfields):
112:             row[f"Disp_{f}" if f != "T2" else "T2"] = disp[f][r]
113:         # covariates X_cov (section 7)
114:         row.update({
115:             "x_rule": int(kinds[k]), "x_log_nvalues": np.log(n_values[k]),
116:             "x_LB0": LB[e0, k], "x_AB0": AB[e0, k], "x_prior_LB": eps["prior_LB"][ep],
117:             "x_overlap": eps["overlap"][ep], "x_N0": N0, "x_tenure": N0 - int(evN[first_t0[k]]),
118:             "x_prior_exposures": int(np.sum((ep_task == k) & (ep_t0 < e0))),
119:             "x_K_active": int(np.sum(~np.isnan(LA[e0]))),
120:             "x_theta_norm0": ev["theta_norm"][e0], "x_sqrt_trv0": ev["sqrt_trv"][e0],
121:             "x_LR0": ev["L_R"][e0],                       # Deep Review D5: L_R(t0) in X_cov
122:         })
123:         if "LAc" in ev:                                   # F^clip (Deep Review A3), sensitivity target
124:             row["F_A_clip"] = ev["LAc"][e1, k] - ev["LAc"][e0, k]
125:             row["F_B_clip"] = ev["LBc"][e1, k] - ev["LBc"][e0, k]
126:         rows.append(row)
127:     df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
128:     # rates (section 7): Z / max(dN, 1)
129:     den = np.maximum(df["dN"].to_numpy(), 1)
130:     for z in ["C2", "C2b", "C3", "C4", "C5", "C5a", "C5b_g", "C5b_gt", "C5b_L", "C5c_sq", "C5c_work", "C5d",
131:               "C5e", "C5f", "C10", "C10b"] + [f for f in ("C10c", "C10p", "C10h") if f in df] + \
132:              [c for c in df.columns if c.startswith("ce_") or c.startswith("Disp_")]:
133:         df[f"rate_{z}"] = df[z] / den
134:     # inclusion (section 6)
135:     comp = df["AB0"] >= 0.90
136:     gap = (df["dN"] >= 64) | ((df["dN"] == 0) & (df["C0"] >= 64))
137:     cap = np.ones(len(df), bool) if max_dN is None else (df["dN"] < max_dN).to_numpy()
138:     df["incl_nocap"] = comp & gap
139:     df["incl"] = comp & gap & cap
140:     df["excl_reason"] = np.where(~comp, "competence<0.90", np.where(~gap, "interval<64",
141:                                  np.where(~cap, f"dN>={max_dN}", "")))
142:     # weights: equal total weight per valid episode (for the capped and for the uncapped inclusion)
143:     for flag, wcol in [("incl", "w"), ("incl_nocap", "w_nocap")]:
144:         inc = df[df[flag]]
145:         E = inc["ep"].nunique()
146:         M = inc.groupby("ep")["ep"].transform("size")
147:         df[wcol] = 0.0
148:         df.loc[inc.index, wcol] = 1.0 / (E * M)
149:     df["t1_max_dN"] = -1 if max_dN is None else int(max_dN)
150:     df["family"], df["seed"] = meta["family"], meta["seed"]
151:     return df, meta
152: 
153: 
154: def weighted_corr(x, y, w):
155:     w = w / w.sum()
156:     mx, my = (w * x).sum(), (w * y).sum()
157:     cov = (w * (x - mx) * (y - my)).sum()
158:     return cov / np.sqrt((w * (x - mx) ** 2).sum() * (w * (y - my) ** 2).sum())
159: 
160: 
161: def run_summary(df, meta):
162:     inc = df[df["incl"]]
163:     ex = df.loc[~df["incl"], "excl_reason"].value_counts().to_dict()
164:     eps = df.groupby("ep").agg(task=("task", "first"), AB0=("AB0", "first"))
165:     kinds = np.array([df.loc[df["task"] == t, "x_rule"].iloc[0] for t in eps["task"]])
166:     out = {
167:         "family": meta["family"], "seed": meta["seed"],
168:         "childhood_gate": meta["childhood"]["gate_passed"], "childhood_acc": round(meta["childhood"]["acc_reference"], 4),
169:         "childhood_acc_rule": (round(meta["childhood"]["acc_reference_rule"], 4)
170:                                if meta["childhood"]["acc_reference_rule"] is not None else None),
171:         "episodes": int(len(eps)), "valid_episodes": int(inc["ep"].nunique()),
172:         "episodes_competent_fact": int(((eps["AB0"] >= 0.9) & (kinds == 0)).sum()),
173:         "episodes_competent_rule": int(((eps["AB0"] >= 0.9) & (kinds == 1)).sum()),
174:         "episodes_fact": int((kinds == 0).sum()), "episodes_rule": int((kinds == 1).sum()),
175:         "triplets": int(len(df)), "included": int(len(inc)), "excluded": ex,
176:     }
177:     if len(inc) > 2:
178:         w = inc["w"].to_numpy()
179:         out["r_AB"] = round(float(weighted_corr(inc["F_A"].to_numpy(), inc["F_B"].to_numpy(), w)), 4)
180:         out["F_A_mean"] = round(float((w * inc["F_A"]).sum() / w.sum()), 4)
181:         out["F_A_sd"] = round(float(np.sqrt((w * (inc["F_A"] - (w * inc["F_A"]).sum() / w.sum()) ** 2).sum() / w.sum())), 4)
182:         out["dAcc_A_mean"] = round(float((w * inc["dAcc_A"]).sum() / w.sum()), 4)
183:         rho = inc["rate_ce_D1"].to_numpy()
184:         out["rate_D1_cv"] = round(float(rho.std() / rho.mean()), 4) if rho.mean() > 0 else None
185:     t = meta["timing_s"]
186:     out["timing_s"] = t
187:     out["peak_rss_mb"] = meta["peak_rss_mb"]
188:     return out
189: 
190: 
191: def main(argv):
192:     for d in argv:
193:         df, meta = build(d)
194:         df.to_parquet(Path(d) / "triplets.parquet")
195:         print(json.dumps(run_summary(df, meta), indent=1, default=float))
196: 
197: 
198: if __name__ == "__main__":
199:     main(sys.argv[1:])

FILE repository\Stadiul0\p2\world.py
1: """World of Pilot 2, Stage A (protocol v0.3, sections 4.1 and 4.2).
2: 
3: Vocabulary partition; target, reference and distractor tasks; probe halves A and B; the reference
4: battery R and the behavioural battery B_beh. A world is a deterministic function of its numpy
5: Generator. Every example is [TASK, a, b, ARROW] -> answer token (one answer position).
6: """
7: from dataclasses import dataclass
8: from math import gcd
9: 
10: import numpy as np
11: 
12: # Vocabulary partition (section 4.1). Order fixes the token ids.
13: PARTITION = [("task", 64), ("v_target", 180), ("v_ref", 84), ("v_distractor", 84),
14:              ("value", 64), ("digit", 32), ("special", 4)]
15: VOCAB = 512
16: TASK_SLOTS = {"target": (0, 40), "reference": (40, 8), "distractor": (48, 8)}   # 8 spare: 56-63
17: 
18: 
19: class Vocab:
20:     def __init__(self):
21:         off = 0
22:         self.ranges = {}
23:         for name, n in PARTITION:
24:             self.ranges[name] = (off, off + n)
25:             setattr(self, name, np.arange(off, off + n, dtype=np.int64))
26:             off += n
27:         assert off == VOCAB
28:         self.ARROW = int(self.special[0])
29:         self.PAD = int(self.special[1])
30: 
31:     def task_token(self, role, i):
32:         base, n = TASK_SLOTS[role]
33:         assert 0 <= i < n, (role, i)
34:         return int(self.task[base + i])
35: 
36: 
37: @dataclass
38: class Task:
39:     role: str                 # target | reference | distractor
40:     kind: str                 # fact | rule
41:     index: int                # index within its role
42:     token: int
43:     train_x: np.ndarray       # [n_train, 4] int64
44:     train_y: np.ndarray       # [n_train] int64 (answer token ids)
45:     probe_x: np.ndarray       # [256, 4] (target tasks; fact probes are the trained items)
46:     probe_y: np.ndarray
47:     half_a: np.ndarray        # indices into probe_x (128)
48:     half_b: np.ndarray
49:     n_values: int             # number of distinct target values (64 for facts, m_k for rules)
50:     modulus: int = 0
51:     a: int = 0
52:     b: int = 0
53: 
54:     def train_pairs(self):
55:         """Input pairs (second and third token) of the training set, as ids t1 * 512 + t2."""
56:         return self.train_x[:, 1] * VOCAB + self.train_x[:, 2]
57: 
58: 
59: def _items(token, k1, k2, arrow):
60:     n = len(k1)
61:     return np.stack([np.full(n, token), k1, k2, np.full(n, arrow)], axis=1).astype(np.int64)
62: 
63: 
64: def _split_halves(rng, n):
65:     perm = rng.permutation(n)
66:     return np.sort(perm[: n // 2]), np.sort(perm[n // 2:])
67: 
68: 
69: class _PairSource:
70:     """Ordered key pairs over one key vocabulary; every fresh pair is handed out once."""
71: 
72:     def __init__(self, rng, vocab_tokens, pool_size=0):
73:         n = len(vocab_tokens)
74:         order = rng.permutation(n * n)
75:         self.tokens = vocab_tokens
76:         self.pool = order[:pool_size]
77:         self.fresh = order[pool_size:]
78:         self.ptr = 0
79: 
80:     def draw(self, rng, n_items, n_shared):
81:         shared = rng.choice(self.pool, size=n_shared, replace=False) if n_shared else np.empty(0, np.int64)
82:         fresh = self.fresh[self.ptr: self.ptr + n_items - n_shared]
83:         assert len(fresh) == n_items - n_shared, "key vocabulary exhausted"
84:         self.ptr += len(fresh)
85:         ids = rng.permutation(np.concatenate([shared, fresh]))
86:         n = len(self.tokens)
87:         return self.tokens[ids // n], self.tokens[ids % n]
88: 
89: 
90: def _fact_task(rng, vocab, role, i, pairs, n_items, n_shared):
91:     k1, k2 = pairs.draw(rng, n_items, n_shared)
92:     x = _items(vocab.task_token(role, i), k1, k2, vocab.ARROW)
93:     y = vocab.value[rng.integers(0, 64, size=n_items)]
94:     ha, hb = _split_halves(rng, n_items)
95:     return Task(role, "fact", i, vocab.task_token(role, i), x, y, x, y, ha, hb, 64)
96: 
97: 
98: def _rule_task(rng, vocab, role, i, modulus, n_items, n_inputs, form="affine"):
99:     """Rule task. form 'affine' (protocol v0.3, section 4.1): y = (a x + b) mod m, x = 32 d1 + d2.
100:     form 'unary' (Stage 0 alternative, not in the protocol): y = (a d1 + b) mod m; d2 is irrelevant,
101:     so held-out probes test generalisation to unseen (d1, d2) combinations."""
102:     a = int(rng.choice([c for c in range(1, modulus) if gcd(c, modulus) == 1]))
103:     b = int(rng.integers(0, modulus))
104:     perm = rng.permutation(n_inputs)
105:     probe_in, train_in = perm[:n_items], perm[n_items: 2 * n_items]   # training drawn from the 768 non-probes
106: 
107:     def enc(xs):
108:         x = _items(vocab.task_token(role, i), vocab.digit[xs // 32], vocab.digit[xs % 32], vocab.ARROW)
109:         arg = xs if form == "affine" else xs // 32
110:         return x, vocab.value[(a * arg + b) % modulus]
111: 
112:     tx, ty = enc(train_in)
113:     px, py = enc(probe_in)
114:     ha, hb = _split_halves(rng, n_items)
115:     n_values = int(len(np.unique(ty)))          # distinct target values in the training set (section 7)
116:     return Task(role, "rule", i, vocab.task_token(role, i), tx, ty, px, py, ha, hb, n_values, modulus, a, b)
117: 
118: 
119: class World:
120:     def __init__(self, wcfg, rng, rule_frac, pi_overlap):
121:         self.vocab = v = Vocab()
122:         n, n_items = wcfg["n_target"], wcfg["items_per_task"]
123:         assert n <= 40
124:         n_rule = int(round(rule_frac * n))
125:         kinds = rng.permutation(["rule"] * n_rule + ["fact"] * (n - n_rule))
126:         self.pi_overlap = pi_overlap
127:         form = wcfg.get("rule_form", "affine")
128:         assert form in ("affine", "unary")
129:         n_shared = int(round(pi_overlap * n_items))
130:         tgt_pairs = _PairSource(rng, v.v_target, wcfg["key_pool_size"])
131:         self.targets = []
132:         for i, kind in enumerate(kinds):
133:             if kind == "fact":
134:                 self.targets.append(_fact_task(rng, v, "target", i, tgt_pairs, n_items, n_shared))
135:             else:
136:                 m = int(rng.choice(wcfg["target_moduli"]))
137:                 self.targets.append(_rule_task(rng, v, "target", i, m, n_items, wcfg["rule_inputs"], form))
138: 
139:         ref_pairs = _PairSource(rng, v.v_ref)
140:         self.refs = [_fact_task(rng, v, "reference", i, ref_pairs, n_items, 0) for i in range(wcfg["n_ref_fact"])]
141:         for j, m in enumerate(wcfg["ref_moduli"][: wcfg["n_ref_rule"]]):
142:             self.refs.append(_rule_task(rng, v, "reference", wcfg["n_ref_fact"] + j, int(m), n_items,
143:                                          wcfg["rule_inputs"], form))
144: 
145:         dis_pairs = _PairSource(rng, v.v_distractor)
146:         self.distractors = [_fact_task(rng, v, "distractor", i, dis_pairs, n_items, 0)
147:                             for i in range(wcfg["n_distractor"])]
148: 
149:         # Batteries R and B_beh: disjoint draws from the reference tasks' trained items.
150:         m = wcfg["battery_per_ref_task"]
151:         rx, ry, bx, by = [], [], [], []
152:         for t in self.refs:
153:             perm = rng.permutation(len(t.train_x))
154:             rx.append(t.train_x[perm[:m]]); ry.append(t.train_y[perm[:m]])
155:             bx.append(t.train_x[perm[m: 2 * m]]); by.append(t.train_y[perm[m: 2 * m]])
156:         self.R_x, self.R_y = np.concatenate(rx), np.concatenate(ry)
157:         self.B_x, self.B_y = np.concatenate(bx), np.concatenate(by)
158:         self.childhood_x = np.concatenate([t.train_x for t in self.refs])
159:         self.childhood_y = np.concatenate([t.train_y for t in self.refs])
160:         self.check()
161: 
162:     # ---- assertions (gate G4, structural part) ----
163:     def check(self):
164:         v = self.vocab
165:         lo = lambda name: v.ranges[name]
166: 
167:         def inside(tokens, name):
168:             a, b = lo(name)
169:             return bool(np.all((tokens >= a) & (tokens < b)))
170: 
171:         for name_a, name_b in [("v_target", "v_ref"), ("v_target", "v_distractor"), ("v_ref", "v_distractor")]:
172:             assert not set(getattr(v, name_a)) & set(getattr(v, name_b))
173:         for t in self.targets:
174:             keyvocab = "v_target" if t.kind == "fact" else "digit"
175:             for x in (t.train_x, t.probe_x):
176:                 assert inside(x[:, 1:3], keyvocab), "target key outside its vocabulary"
177:             if t.kind == "rule":
178:                 assert not set(map(tuple, t.train_x)) & set(map(tuple, t.probe_x)), "rule probe trained"
179:             assert len(set(map(tuple, t.probe_x))) == len(t.probe_x)
180:             assert not set(t.half_a) & set(t.half_b) and len(t.half_a) + len(t.half_b) == len(t.probe_x)
181:         for t in self.refs:
182:             assert inside(t.train_x[:, 1:3], "v_ref" if t.kind == "fact" else "digit")
183:             assert t.modulus not in {tt.modulus for tt in self.targets if tt.kind == "rule"}
184:         for t in self.distractors:
185:             assert inside(t.train_x[:, 1:3], "v_distractor")
186:         tokens = [t.token for t in self.targets + self.refs + self.distractors]
187:         assert len(set(tokens)) == len(tokens)
188:         probes = set()
189:         for t in self.targets:
190:             probes |= set(map(tuple, t.probe_x))
191:         R = set(map(tuple, self.R_x))
192:         B = set(map(tuple, self.B_x))
193:         assert len(R) == len(self.R_x) and len(B) == len(self.B_x)
194:         assert not R & probes and not B & probes and not R & B, "R, B_beh and probes must be disjoint"
195:         assert np.all(self.R_x[:, 0] >= TASK_SLOTS["reference"][0]) and np.all(self.R_x[:, 0] < 48)
196: 
197:     def key_overlap(self):
198:         """Reference predictor C8 (world metadata): overlap of input pairs between target tasks.
199: 
200:         Facts: |P_k & P_j| / |P_k| on key pairs. Rules: Jaccard overlap of training input pairs.
201:         Returned as a [n, n] matrix (row k, column j); facts and rules never share input pairs.
202:         """
203:         n = len(self.targets)
204:         sets = [set(t.train_pairs().tolist()) for t in self.targets]
205:         out = np.zeros((n, n))
206:         for k in range(n):
207:             for j in range(n):
208:                 if j == k:
209:                     continue
210:                 inter = len(sets[k] & sets[j])
211:                 if self.targets[k].kind == "rule":
212:                     out[k, j] = inter / max(1, len(sets[k] | sets[j]))
213:                 else:
214:                     out[k, j] = inter / len(sets[k])
215:         return out
216: 
217:     def summary(self):
218:         return {
219:             "kinds": [t.kind for t in self.targets],
220:             "moduli": [t.modulus for t in self.targets],
221:             "n_values": [t.n_values for t in self.targets],
222:             "pi_overlap": self.pi_overlap,
223:         }
